#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"

#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"

namespace GBAttributePrivate
{
	// Fallbacks only for an actor whose data has no MaxHealth / MaxMana (a warning is logged).
	constexpr float FallbackMaxHealth = 100.f;
	constexpr float FallbackMaxMana = 100.f;

	float ReadStat(const UGBStatsComponent* Stats, FName Stat, float DefaultValue)
	{
		return Stats ? Stats->GetStat(Stat, DefaultValue) : DefaultValue;
	}
}

UGBAttributeComponent::UGBAttributeComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryComponentTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
}

UGBAttributeComponent* UGBAttributeComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBAttributeComponent>() : nullptr;
}

UGBStatsComponent* UGBAttributeComponent::GetStats() const
{
	return UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::GetMaxHealth() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0.f, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::MaxHealth, GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxHealth));
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::GetMaxMana() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0.f, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::MaxMana, GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxMana));
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBStatsComponent* OwnerStats = GetStats())
	{
		OwnerStats->OnStatsChanged.AddWeakLambda(this, [this]()
		{
			// Max values may have dropped (armor removed, effect ended): clamp and let the HUD redraw.
			Health = FMath::Min(Health, GetMaxHealth());
			Mana = FMath::Min(Mana, GetMaxMana());
			OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
			OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
		});
	}

	// Safety net for owners that set their stats before BeginPlay but never call InitializeFromStats.
	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();
	if (!bDead && Health <= 0.f && Stats && Stats->HasBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth))
	{
		InitializeFromStats();
	}
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::InitializeFromStats()
{
	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();
	if (!Stats || !Stats->HasBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: no MaxHealth stat in the data; using %f."), *GetNameSafe(GetOwner()), GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxHealth);
	}

	bDead = false;
	Health = GetMaxHealth();
	Mana = GetMaxMana();
	TimeSinceDamage = 1000.f;
	TimeSinceManaSpend = 1000.f;
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::ApplyDamage(const FGBDamageInfo& Info)
{
	if (bDead || bInvulnerable || Info.Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	FGBDamageInfo Hit = Info;
	OnModifyIncomingDamage.Broadcast(Hit);

	const float Amount = FMath::Max(0.f, Hit.Amount * GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::DamageTaken, 1.f));
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		// Fully blocked or cancelled: not a hit, so no damage events and the regen delay keeps running.
		return 0.f;
	}

	const float Actual = FMath::Min(Amount, Health);
	Health -= Actual;
	TimeSinceDamage = 0.f;

	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnDamaged.Broadcast(Hit, Actual);

	if (Health <= 0.f && !bDead)
	{
		Health = 0.f;
		bDead = true;
		OnDeath.Broadcast(Hit);
	}
	return Actual;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::Heal(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewHealth = FMath::Min(Health + Amount, GetMaxHealth());
	if (NewHealth != Health)
	{
		Health = NewHealth;
		OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	}
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreMana(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewMana = FMath::Min(Mana + Amount, GetMaxMana());
	if (NewMana != Mana)
	{
		Mana = NewMana;
		OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	}
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::HasMana(float Amount) const
{
	return Mana >= Amount;
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::SpendMana(float Amount)
{
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}
	if (Mana < Amount)
	{
		return false;
	}
	Mana -= Amount;
	TimeSinceManaSpend = 0.f;
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	return true;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreAll()
{
	bDead = false;
	Health = GetMaxHealth();
	Mana = GetMaxMana();
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::SetHealthAndMana(float NewHealth, float NewMana)
{
	Health = FMath::Clamp(NewHealth, 0.f, GetMaxHealth());
	Mana = FMath::Clamp(NewMana, 0.f, GetMaxMana());
	// A snapshot with health revives; one at 0 leaves the owner dead (no OnDeath: nothing hit it).
	bDead = Health <= 0.f;
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::StepRegen(float Current, float Max, float RegenPerSecond, float RegenDelay, float TimeSinceLastLoss, float DeltaTime)
{
	if (Current >= Max || RegenPerSecond <= 0.f || DeltaTime <= 0.f)
	{
		return Current;
	}
	// Only the part of this step that lies past the delay regenerates, so the result does not depend on frame rate.
	const float RegenTime = FMath::Clamp(TimeSinceLastLoss + DeltaTime - FMath::Max(0.f, RegenDelay), 0.f, DeltaTime);
	return FMath::Min(Current + RegenPerSecond * RegenTime, Max);
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);

	if (!bDead)
	{
		const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();

		const float MaxHealth = GetMaxHealth();
		const float NewHealth = StepRegen(Health, MaxHealth, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::HealthRegen, 0.f),
			GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::HealthRegenDelay, 0.f), TimeSinceDamage, DeltaTime);
		if (NewHealth != Health)
		{
			Health = NewHealth;
			OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, MaxHealth);
		}

		const float MaxMana = GetMaxMana();
		const float NewMana = StepRegen(Mana, MaxMana, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::ManaRegen, 0.f),
			GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::ManaRegenDelay, 0.f), TimeSinceManaSpend, DeltaTime);
		if (NewMana != Mana)
		{
			Mana = NewMana;
			OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, MaxMana);
		}
	}

	TimeSinceDamage += DeltaTime;
	TimeSinceManaSpend += DeltaTime;
}
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"

#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"

UGBStatsComponent::UGBStatsComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

UGBStatsComponent* UGBStatsComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBStatsComponent>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStats(const TMap<FName, float>& InBaseStats)
{
	BaseStats = InBaseStats;
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStat(FName Stat, float Value)
{
	BaseStats.Add(Stat, Value);
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasBaseStat(FName Stat) const
{
	return BaseStats.Contains(Stat);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	const float* Base = BaseStats.Find(Stat);
	return Evaluate(Stat, Base ? *Base : DefaultValue);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::Evaluate(FName Key, float Base) const
{
	return EvaluateKeys(MakeArrayView(&Key, 1), Base);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::EvaluateKeys(TConstArrayView<FName> Keys, float Base) const
{
	TArray<FGBModifierDef, TInlineAllocator<8>> Matching;
	for (const FSourcedModifier& Entry : ActiveModifiers)
	{
		if (Keys.Contains(Entry.Modifier.Stat))
		{
			Matching.Add(Entry.Modifier);
		}
	}
	return ApplyModifiers(Base, Matching);
}

void UGBStatsComponent::AddModifiers(FName Source, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers, float ValueScale)
{
	ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		FSourcedModifier& Entry = ActiveModifiers.AddDefaulted_GetRef();
		Entry.Source = Source;
		Entry.Modifier = Modifier;
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Entry.Modifier.Value = Modifier.Value * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			// Scaled around 1, so a x1.5 buff at half strength is x1.25, not x0.75.
			Entry.Modifier.Value = 1.f + (Modifier.Value - 1.f) * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			break;
		}
	}

	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::RemoveModifiers(FName Source)
{
	if (ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; }) > 0)
	{
		OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasModifierSource(FName Source) const
{
	return ActiveModifiers.ContainsByPredicate([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });
}

float UGBStatsComponent::ApplyModifiers(float Base, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers)
{
	float Sum = Base;
	float Product = 1.f;
	const FGBModifierDef* LastOverride = nullptr;

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Sum += Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			Product *= Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			LastOverride = &Modifier;
			break;
		}
	}

	return LastOverride ? LastOverride->Value : Sum * Product;
}
#include "Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.h"

#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.h"
#include "Weapons/GBGun.h"
#include "Weapons/GBStaff.h"
#include "Weapons/GBSword.h"
#include "World/GBPickup.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

namespace
{
	/** Where a replaced weapon is dropped: in front of the player (placement offset, not a gameplay number). */
	constexpr float EquipDropDistanceCm = 120.f;

	/** Cycle order (GDD 9): Staff -> Gun -> Sword. */
	const EGBHeldSlot EquipSlotOrder[] = { EGBHeldSlot::Staff, EGBHeldSlot::Gun, EGBHeldSlot::Sword };

	int32 EquipSlotIndex(EGBHeldSlot Slot)
	{
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < static_cast<int32>(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(EquipSlotOrder)); ++Index)
		{
			if (EquipSlotOrder[Index] == Slot)
			{
				return Index;
			}
		}
		return 0;
	}
}

UGBEquipmentComponent::UGBEquipmentComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	// Quiet teardown: no unequip callbacks (they touch the camera and broadcast to a HUD that may be gone).
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get(); IsValid(Item))
		{
			Item->Destroy();
		}
	}
	Items.Reset();
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::SpawnItem(FName WeaponId)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner());
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
	if (!Player || !World || !Def)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GiveWeapon: unknown weapon '%s' (or no player / world)"), *WeaponId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	UClass* ItemClass = nullptr;
	if (!Def->ActorClass.IsEmpty())
	{
		ItemClass = LoadClass<AGBHeldItem>(nullptr, *Def->ActorClass);
		if (!ItemClass || ItemClass->HasAnyClassFlags(CLASS_Abstract))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapon '%s': ActorClass '%s' not found or abstract, using the slot default"), *WeaponId.ToString(), *Def->ActorClass);
			ItemClass = nullptr;
		}
	}
	if (!ItemClass)
	{
		switch (Def->Slot)
		{
		case EGBHeldSlot::Staff: ItemClass = AGBStaff::StaticClass(); break;
		case EGBHeldSlot::Sword: ItemClass = AGBSword::StaticClass(); break;
		default: ItemClass = AGBGun::StaticClass(); break;
		}
	}

	// Copy the definition: spawning must not rely on a pointer into the data tables.
	const FGBWeaponDefinition Definition = *Def;

	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
	Params.Owner = Player;
	Params.Instigator = Player;
	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
	AGBHeldItem* Item = World->SpawnActor<AGBHeldItem>(ItemClass, Player->GetActorTransform(), Params);
	if (!Item)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GiveWeapon: could not spawn '%s'"), *WeaponId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	USceneComponent* Anchor = Player->GetHeldItemAnchor() ? Player->GetHeldItemAnchor() : Player->GetRootComponent();
	Item->AttachToComponent(Anchor, FAttachmentTransformRules::SnapToTargetNotIncludingScale);
	Item->InitFromDefinition(Definition, Player);
	Item->OnUnequipped();
	Item->RefillAmmo();
	return Item;
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::GiveWeapon(FName WeaponId, bool bAutoSwitch, bool bDropOld)
{
	AGBHeldItem* NewItem = SpawnItem(WeaponId);
	if (!NewItem)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const EGBHeldSlot NewSlot = NewItem->GetSlot();
	const bool bHandsEmpty = GetActiveItem() == nullptr;
	bool bReplacedActive = false;

	if (AGBHeldItem* Old = GetItemInSlot(NewSlot))
	{
		bReplacedActive = Old->IsEquipped();
		Old->StopPrimary();
		Old->StopSecondary();
		Old->OnUnequipped();
		if (bDropOld)
		{
			if (const AActor* Owner = GetOwner())
			{
				FVector Forward = Owner->GetActorForwardVector();
				Forward.Z = 0.f;
				Forward = Forward.GetSafeNormal();
				FGBDropDefinition Drop;
				Drop.WeaponId = Old->GetWeaponId();
				AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(GetWorld(), Drop, Owner->GetActorLocation() + Forward * EquipDropDistanceCm);
			}
		}
		Old->Destroy();
	}
	Items.Add(NewSlot, NewItem);

	if (bHandsEmpty || bReplacedActive)
	{
		EquipSlot(NewSlot);
	}
	else if (!bAutoSwitch || !SwitchTo(NewSlot))
	{
		OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
	}
	return NewItem;
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::HasWeaponInSlot(EGBHeldSlot Slot) const
{
	return GetItemInSlot(Slot) != nullptr;
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::GetItemInSlot(EGBHeldSlot Slot) const
{
	AGBHeldItem* Item = Items.FindRef(Slot).Get();
	return IsValid(Item) ? Item : nullptr;
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::GetActiveItem() const
{
	AGBHeldItem* Item = GetItemInSlot(ActiveSlot);
	return (Item && Item->IsEquipped()) ? Item : nullptr;
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::IsHoldingStaff() const
{
	return ActiveSlot == EGBHeldSlot::Staff && GetActiveItem() != nullptr;
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::IsHoldingGun() const
{
	return ActiveSlot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun && GetActiveItem() != nullptr;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::EquipSlot(EGBHeldSlot Slot)
{
	AGBHeldItem* Target = GetItemInSlot(Slot);
	AGBHeldItem* Current = GetItemInSlot(ActiveSlot);
	if (Current && Current != Target && Current->IsEquipped())
	{
		Current->StopPrimary();
		Current->StopSecondary();
		Current->OnUnequipped();
	}

	ActiveSlot = Slot;

	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner()))
	{
		if (UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook())
		{
			Spellbook->CancelSustained();
		}
	}

	if (Target && !Target->IsEquipped())
	{
		Target->OnEquipped();
	}

	OnActiveItemChanged.Broadcast(Target);
	OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::SwitchTo(EGBHeldSlot Slot)
{
	if (bSwitchLocked || !HasWeaponInSlot(Slot))
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (Slot == ActiveSlot && GetActiveItem() != nullptr)
	{
		return true;
	}
	EquipSlot(Slot);
	return true;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::CycleSlot(int32 Direction)
{
	if (bSwitchLocked)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 Count = static_cast<int32>(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(EquipSlotOrder));
	const int32 Step = Direction >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
	const int32 Start = EquipSlotIndex(ActiveSlot);
	for (int32 Offset = 1; Offset < Count; ++Offset)
	{
		const int32 Index = ((Start + Step * Offset) % Count + Count) % Count;
		if (HasWeaponInSlot(EquipSlotOrder[Index]))
		{
			SwitchTo(EquipSlotOrder[Index]);
			return;
		}
	}
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::RefillAllAmmo()
{
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get(); IsValid(Item))
		{
			Item->RefillAmmo();
		}
	}
	OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
}

int32 UGBEquipmentComponent::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
{
	AGBHeldItem* Gun = GetItemInSlot(EGBHeldSlot::Gun);
	return Gun ? Gun->AddSpareMagazines(Count) : 0;
}

EGBHudSkin UGBEquipmentComponent::GetHudSkin() const
{
	const AGBHeldItem* Active = GetActiveItem();
	return Active ? Active->GetHudSkin() : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::DestroyAllItems()
{
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get(); IsValid(Item))
		{
			Item->StopPrimary();
			Item->StopSecondary();
			Item->OnUnequipped();
			Item->Destroy();
		}
	}
	Items.Reset();
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.Weapons.Reset();
	Snapshot.Ammo.Reset();
	Snapshot.WeaponElements.Reset();
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		const AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get();
		if (!IsValid(Item))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Snapshot.Weapons.Add(Item->GetWeaponId());
		Snapshot.WeaponElements.Add(Item->GetWeaponId(), Item->GetElement());
		if (Item->UsesAmmo())
		{
			Snapshot.Ammo.Add(Item->GetWeaponId(), Item->GetAmmo());
		}
	}
	Snapshot.ActiveSlot = ActiveSlot;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{
	DestroyAllItems();

	for (const FName& WeaponId : Snapshot.Weapons)
	{
		AGBHeldItem* Item = SpawnItem(WeaponId);
		if (!Item)
		{
			continue;
		}
		if (AGBHeldItem* Previous = GetItemInSlot(Item->GetSlot()))
		{
			Previous->Destroy(); // two weapons of one slot in a snapshot: the last one wins
		}
		Items.Add(Item->GetSlot(), Item);

		if (const EGBElement* SavedElement = Snapshot.WeaponElements.Find(WeaponId))
		{
			Item->SetElement(*SavedElement);
		}
		if (const FGBAmmoState* SavedAmmo = Snapshot.Ammo.Find(WeaponId))
		{
			Item->SetAmmo(*SavedAmmo);
		}
	}

	EGBHeldSlot Slot = Snapshot.ActiveSlot;
	if (!HasWeaponInSlot(Slot))
	{
		for (const EGBHeldSlot Candidate : EquipSlotOrder)
		{
			if (HasWeaponInSlot(Candidate))
			{
				Slot = Candidate;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	if (HasWeaponInSlot(Slot))
	{
		EquipSlot(Slot);
	}
	else
	{
		ActiveSlot = Snapshot.ActiveSlot;
		OnActiveItemChanged.Broadcast(nullptr);
		OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}
#include "Weapons/GBGun.h"

#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
#include "Camera/CameraComponent.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "DrawDebugHelpers.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"

void AGBGun::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	Super::InitFromDefinition(Definition, InOwner);

	const FName Required[] = { GBStat::Damage, GBStat::FireInterval, GBStat::MagazineSize, GBStat::SpareMagazines, GBStat::ReloadTime, GBStat::Range };
	for (const FName& Stat : Required)
	{
		if (!Definition.Stats.Contains(Stat))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapons.json '%s': missing stat '%s', using a default"), *Definition.Id.ToString(), *Stat.ToString());
		}
	}

	BuildViewModel(Definition);
}

// ---- Looks ----

UStaticMeshComponent* AGBGun::AddPart(FName Name, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& Location, const FVector& Size, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bGlow)
{
	UStaticMeshComponent* Part = NewObject<UStaticMeshComponent>(this, Name);
	Part->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Part->SetRelativeLocation(Location);
	Part->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Part->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Part->SetCastShadow(false);
	Part->RegisterComponent();
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Part, Shape, Size, Color, bGlow);
	Parts.Add(Part);
	return Part;
}

void AGBGun::BuildViewModel(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition)
{
	// Placeholder viewmodel (art rule: guns are 3D). Sizes in cm are purely visual; X = forward, Z = up.
	const float* PelletStat = Definition.Stats.Find(GBStat::Pellets);
	const bool bShotgun = PelletStat && *PelletStat > 1.f;
	const bool bAutomaticLook = Definition.bAutomatic;

	const float BodyLength = bShotgun ? 22.f : (bAutomaticLook ? 26.f : 16.f);
	const float BarrelLength = bShotgun ? 18.f : (bAutomaticLook ? 16.f : 6.f);
	const float BarrelThickness = bShotgun ? 4.f : 2.5f;

	const FLinearColor BodyColor(0.08f, 0.08f, 0.10f);
	const FLinearColor BarrelColor(0.03f, 0.03f, 0.035f);
	const FLinearColor GripColor(0.12f, 0.10f, 0.10f);
	const FLinearColor NeonColor(0.0f, 0.9f, 1.0f);

	AddPart(TEXT("Body"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector::ZeroVector, FVector(BodyLength, 4.f, 7.f), BodyColor, false);
	AddPart(TEXT("Barrel"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyLength * 0.5f + BarrelLength * 0.5f, 0.f, 1.5f),
		FVector(BarrelLength, BarrelThickness, BarrelThickness), BarrelColor, false);
	AddPart(TEXT("Grip"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(-BodyLength * 0.5f + 3.f, 0.f, -6.f), FVector(3.5f, 3.5f, 8.f), GripColor, false);
	if (bAutomaticLook)
	{
		AddPart(TEXT("Magazine"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyLength * 0.15f, 0.f, -6.f), FVector(3.f, 2.5f, 8.f), BodyColor, false);
	}
	AddPart(TEXT("Neon"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(0.f, 2.1f, 1.f), FVector(BodyLength * 0.8f, 0.3f, 0.6f), NeonColor, true);
}

// ---- Ammo ----

int32 AGBGun::GetMagazineSize() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::MagazineSize, 1.f)));
}

int32 AGBGun::GetMaxSpareMagazines() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::SpareMagazines, 0.f)));
}

void AGBGun::SetAmmo(const FGBAmmoState& InAmmo)
{
	CancelReload();
	Ammo.InMagazine = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.InMagazine, 0, GetMagazineSize());
	Ammo.SpareMagazines = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.SpareMagazines, 0, GetMaxSpareMagazines());
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::RefillAmmo()
{
	CancelReload();
	Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
	Ammo.SpareMagazines = GetMaxSpareMagazines();
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

int32 AGBGun::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
{
	if (Count <= 0)
	{
		return 0;
	}
	const int32 Added = FMath::Clamp(GetMaxSpareMagazines() - Ammo.SpareMagazines, 0, Count);
	if (Added > 0)
	{
		Ammo.SpareMagazines += Added;
		NotifyEquipmentChanged();
	}
	return Added;
}

// ---- Reload ----

void AGBGun::Reload()
{
	if (bReloading || !bEquipped)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (Ammo.InMagazine >= GetMagazineSize() || Ammo.SpareMagazines <= 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	bReloading = true;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	ReloadDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, GetStat(GBStat::ReloadTime, 1.f));
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::CancelReload()
{
	if (!bReloading)
	{
		return;
	}
	bReloading = false;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::FinishReload()
{
	bReloading = false;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	if (Ammo.SpareMagazines > 0)
	{
		// The rounds left in the old magazine are lost (provisional, classic tactical style).
		--Ammo.SpareMagazines;
		Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
	}
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

float AGBGun::GetReloadProgress() const
{
	if (!bReloading)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	return ReloadDuration > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? FMath::Clamp(ReloadElapsed / ReloadDuration, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
}

// ---- Aim ----

void AGBGun::StartSecondary()
{
	if (bEquipped)
	{
		SetAiming(true);
	}
}

void AGBGun::StopSecondary()
{
	SetAiming(false);
}

void AGBGun::SetAiming(bool bInAiming)
{
	if (bAiming == bInAiming)
	{
		return;
	}
	bAiming = bInAiming;
	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner())
	{
		Player->SetMoveSpeedScale(bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimMoveSpeedMult, 1.f) : 1.f);
	}
}

void AGBGun::UpdateAimFOV(float DeltaSeconds, bool bInstant)
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UCameraComponent* Camera = Player ? Player->GetCamera() : nullptr;
	if (!Camera)
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Target = bAiming ? 1.f : 0.f;
	if (AimAlpha == Target)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AimAlpha <= 0.f)
	{
		HipFOV = Camera->FieldOfView;
	}

	if (bInstant)
	{
		AimAlpha = Target;
	}
	else
	{
		const float AimTime = GetStat(GBWeaponStat::AimTime, 0.15f);
		const float Step = AimTime > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? DeltaSeconds / AimTime : 1.f;
		AimAlpha = FMath::Clamp(Target > AimAlpha ? AimAlpha + Step : AimAlpha - Step, 0.f, 1.f);
	}
	Camera->SetFieldOfView(FMath::Lerp(HipFOV, GetStat(GBStat::AimFOV, HipFOV), AimAlpha));
}

void AGBGun::OnUnequipped()
{
	CancelReload();
	SetAiming(false);
	UpdateAimFOV(0.f, true);
	Super::OnUnequipped();
}

void AGBGun::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (bReloading)
	{
		ReloadElapsed += DeltaSeconds;
		if (ReloadElapsed >= ReloadDuration)
		{
			FinishReload();
		}
	}
	if (bEquipped)
	{
		UpdateAimFOV(DeltaSeconds, false);
	}
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
}

// ---- Shooting ----

bool AGBGun::PerformAttack()
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	if (!Player || !GetWorld() || bReloading)
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
	{
		Reload();
		return false;
	}

	const FGBDamageInfo BaseInfo = MakeDamageInfo();
	const float SpreadDegrees = bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimSpread, 0.f) : GetStat(GBStat::Spread, 0.f);

	--Ammo.InMagazine;

	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	if (Def && Def->FireMode.Equals(TEXT("Projectile"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
	{
		FireProjectile(BaseInfo, SpreadDegrees);
	}
	else
	{
		FireHitscan(BaseInfo, SpreadDegrees);
	}

	const float RecoilYaw = GetStat(GBStat::RecoilYaw, 0.f);
	Player->AddRecoil(GetStat(GBStat::RecoilPitch, 0.f), Random.FRandRange(-RecoilYaw, RecoilYaw), GetStat(GBStat::RecoilRecovery, 0.f));

	NotifyEquipmentChanged();

	if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
	{
		Reload();
	}
	return true;
}

void AGBGun::FireHitscan(const FGBDamageInfo& BaseInfo, float SpreadDegrees)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();

	const FVector Origin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector AimDirection = Player->GetAimDirection();
	const FVector Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::Range, 5000.f);
	const int32 Pellets = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::Pellets, 1.f)));
	const float FalloffStart = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffStart, 0.f);
	const float FalloffEnd = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffEnd, 0.f);
	const float NearMult = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffNearMult, 1.f);
	const float FarMult = GetStat(GBStat::FalloffFarMult, 1.f);
	const float HeadshotMult = GetStat(GBStat::HeadshotMult, 1.f);

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBGunTrace), false, Player);
	Params.AddIgnoredActor(this);

	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Pellets; ++Index)
	{
		const FVector Direction = UGBCombatLibrary::ApplySpread(AimDirection, SpreadDegrees, Random);
		const FVector End = Origin + Direction * Range;

		FHitResult Hit;
		const bool bHit = World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Origin, End, GBWeapon::AimTraceChannel, Params);
		AActor* Target = bHit ? Hit.GetActor() : nullptr;

		if (Target && UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target))
		{
			FGBDamageInfo Info = BaseInfo;
			float Multiplier = UGBCombatLibrary::DistanceFalloffMultiplier(Hit.Distance, FalloffStart, FalloffEnd, NearMult, FarMult);
			if (const AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = Cast<AGBEnemyCharacter>(Target))
			{
				if (Enemy->IsHeadshotLocation(Hit.ImpactPoint))
				{
					Multiplier *= HeadshotMult;
					Info.bHeadshot = true;
				}
			}
			Info.Amount *= Multiplier;
			Info.HitLocation = Hit.ImpactPoint;
			Info.HitDirection = Direction;
			UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
		}

#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING
		// Placeholder tracer.
		DrawDebugLine(World, Muzzle, bHit ? Hit.ImpactPoint : End, FColor(255, 220, 120), false, 0.05f, 0, 0.6f);
#endif
	}
}

void AGBGun::FireProjectile(const FGBDamageInfo& BaseInfo, float SpreadDegrees)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();

	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::Range, 5000.f);
	const FVector Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	FVector AimDirection = (FindAimPoint(Range) - Muzzle).GetSafeNormal();
	if (AimDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		AimDirection = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage = BaseInfo;
	Config.Speed = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, 3000.f);
	Config.Radius = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, 5.f);
	Config.GravityScale = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
	Config.MaxRange = Range;
	Config.bPixelArt = false; // art rule: gun shots are 3D
	Config.Color = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(BaseInfo.Element));

	const int32 Pellets = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::Pellets, 1.f)));
	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Pellets; ++Index)
	{
		const FVector Direction = UGBCombatLibrary::ApplySpread(AimDirection, SpreadDegrees, Random);
		AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Muzzle, Direction, Player);
	}
}
#include "Weapons/GBHeldItem.h"

#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.h"

AGBHeldItem::AGBHeldItem()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;

	Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	RootComponent = Root;

	SetActorEnableCollision(false);
}

void AGBHeldItem::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	WeaponId = Definition.Id;
	Slot = Definition.Slot;
	Element = Definition.Element;
	PlayerOwner = InOwner;
	SetOwner(InOwner);
	SetInstigator(InOwner);
	Random.GenerateNewSeed();
}

const FGBWeaponDefinition* AGBHeldItem::GetDefinition() const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	return Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
}

FString AGBHeldItem::GetDisplayName() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : WeaponId.ToString();
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	float Base = DefaultValue;
	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const float* Value = Def->Stats.Find(Stat))
		{
			Base = *Value;
		}
	}

	if (const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats())
	{
		TArray<FName, TInlineAllocator<2>> Keys;
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponKey(WeaponId, Stat));
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponAllKey(Stat));
		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Base);
	}
	return Base;
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnEquipped()
{
	bEquipped = true;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(false);
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnUnequipped()
{
	bEquipped = false;
	bTriggerHeld = false;
	bAttackQueued = false;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
}

EGBHudSkin AGBHeldItem::GetHudSkin() const
{
	return Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun ? EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetTimeSinceLastAttack() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? static_cast<float>(World->GetTimeSeconds() - LastAttackTime) : 1000.f;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::IsAutomatic() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return Def && Def->bAutomatic;
}

UGBStatsComponent* AGBHeldItem::GetOwnerStats() const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	return Player ? Player->GetStats() : nullptr;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::GetActiveEnchant(EGBElement& OutElement, FName& OutOnHitEffect) const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	const UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player ? Player->GetStatusEffects() : nullptr;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Effects || !Data)
	{
		return false;
	}

	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Active : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
	{
		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Def = Data->FindStatusEffect(Active.EffectId);
		if (!Def)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FString* ElementName = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponElement);
		if (!ElementName || ElementName->IsEmpty())
		{
			continue;
		}
		const int64 Value = StaticEnum<EGBElement>()->GetValueByNameString(*ElementName);
		if (Value == INDEX_NONE)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Status effect '%s': unknown WeaponElement '%s'"), *Active.EffectId.ToString(), **ElementName);
			continue;
		}
		OutElement = static_cast<EGBElement>(Value);
		const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponOnHitEffect);
		OutOnHitEffect = (Effect && !Effect->IsEmpty()) ? FName(**Effect) : NAME_None;
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

FGBDamageInfo AGBHeldItem::MakeDamageInfo() const
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = GetStat(GBStat::Damage);
	Info.Element = Element;
	Info.Instigator = GetPlayerOwner();
	Info.Causer = const_cast<AGBHeldItem*>(this);
	Info.SourceId = WeaponId;

	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
		{
			if (!Effect->IsEmpty())
			{
				Info.OnHitEffect = FName(**Effect);
			}
		}
	}

	// The enchant's damage multiplier arrives by itself through its "Weapon.All.Damage" modifier.
	EGBElement EnchantElement = EGBElement::Physical;
	FName EnchantEffect = NAME_None;
	if (AcceptsEnchant() && GetActiveEnchant(EnchantElement, EnchantEffect))
	{
		Info.Element = EnchantElement;
		if (!EnchantEffect.IsNone())
		{
			Info.OnHitEffect = EnchantEffect;
		}
	}
	return Info;
}

void AGBHeldItem::PressTrigger()
{
	bTriggerHeld = true;
	bAttackQueued = true;
	UpdateTrigger();
}

void AGBHeldItem::ReleaseTrigger()
{
	bTriggerHeld = false;
	bAttackQueued = false;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::IsAttackReady() const
{
	return GetTimeSinceLastAttack() >= GetStat(GBStat::FireInterval, 0.f);
}

void AGBHeldItem::UpdateTrigger()
{
	if (!bEquipped || !(bAttackQueued || (bTriggerHeld && IsAutomatic())))
	{
		return;
	}
	if (!IsAttackReady())
	{
		return; // a pressed shot stays queued until the fire interval has passed
	}

	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player ? Player->GetSpellbook() : nullptr;
	if (!Player || Player->IsDead() || (Spellbook && Spellbook->IsBusy()))
	{
		bAttackQueued = false;
		return;
	}

	if (PerformAttack())
	{
		if (const UWorld* World = GetWorld())
		{
			LastAttackTime = World->GetTimeSeconds();
		}
	}
	bAttackQueued = false;
}

void AGBHeldItem::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	UpdateTrigger();
}

FVector AGBHeldItem::FindAimPoint(float Range) const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	if (!Player)
	{
		return GetActorLocation();
	}
	const FVector Start = Player->GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector End = Start + Player->GetAimDirection() * Range;

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBAimPoint), false, Player);
	Params.AddIgnoredActor(this);
	FHitResult Hit;
	if (GetWorld() && GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Start, End, GBWeapon::AimTraceChannel, Params))
	{
		return Hit.ImpactPoint;
	}
	return End;
}

void AGBHeldItem::NotifyEquipmentChanged() const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	if (UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player ? Player->GetEquipment() : nullptr)
	{
		Equipment->OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}
#include "Weapons/GBStaff.h"

#include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"

bool AGBStaff::PerformAttack()
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!Player || !World)
	{
		return false;
	}

	const float ManaCost = GetStat(GBStat::ManaCost, 0.f);
	if (ManaCost > 0.f)
	{
		UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = Player->GetAttributes();
		if (!Attributes || !Attributes->SpendMana(ManaCost))
		{
			return false;
		}
	}

	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::Range, 5000.f);
	const FVector Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	FVector Direction = FindAimPoint(Range) - Muzzle;
	if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		Direction = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage = MakeDamageInfo();
	Config.Speed = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, 3000.f);
	Config.Radius = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, 10.f);
	Config.GravityScale = GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
	Config.MaxRange = Range;
	Config.bPixelArt = true;
	Config.Color = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Config.Damage.Element));
	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const FString* Sprite = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::ProjectileSprite))
		{
			Config.SpriteId = FName(**Sprite);
		}
	}

	return AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Muzzle, Direction, Player) != nullptr;
}
#include "Weapons/GBSword.h"

#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Engine/OverlapResult.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"

namespace
{
	UStaticMeshComponent* MakeSwordHiltPart(AActor* Owner, USceneComponent* Parent, FName Name, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& Location,
		const FVector& Size, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bGlow)
	{
		UStaticMeshComponent* Part = NewObject<UStaticMeshComponent>(Owner, Name);
		Part->SetupAttachment(Parent);
		Part->SetRelativeLocation(Location);
		Part->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
		Part->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
		Part->SetCastShadow(false);
		Part->RegisterComponent();
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Part, Shape, Size, Color, bGlow);
		return Part;
	}
}

void AGBSword::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	Super::InitFromDefinition(Definition, InOwner);

	const FName Required[] = { GBStat::Damage, GBStat::FireInterval, GBStat::MeleeRange, GBStat::MeleeArc };
	for (const FName& Stat : Required)
	{
		if (!Definition.Stats.Contains(Stat))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapons.json '%s': missing stat '%s', using a default"), *Definition.Id.ToString(), *Stat.ToString());
		}
	}

	// Placeholder 3D hilt (art rule: the hilt comes from the future). Sizes in cm are purely visual; the blade
	// rises along +Z from the guard and is drawn by the HUD as pixel art.
	Grip = MakeSwordHiltPart(this, Root, TEXT("Grip"), EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(0.f, 0.f, -5.f), FVector(3.f, 3.f, 10.f), FLinearColor(0.10f, 0.10f, 0.12f), false);
	Guard = MakeSwordHiltPart(this, Root, TEXT("Guard"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(0.f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(4.f, 14.f, 2.f), FLinearColor(0.35f, 0.35f, 0.40f), false);
	ShownElement = Element;
	Gem = MakeSwordHiltPart(this, Root, TEXT("Gem"), EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(2.5f, 0.f, 1.f), FVector(2.5f, 2.5f, 2.5f),
		GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(ShownElement)), true);
}

void AGBSword::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (Gem && ShownElement != Element)
	{
		ShownElement = Element;
		GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(Gem, GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(ShownElement)));
	}
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
}

bool AGBSword::PerformAttack()
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!Player || !World)
	{
		return false;
	}

	const FVector Origin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector Forward = Player->GetAimDirection().GetSafeNormal();
	const float Range = GetStat(GBStat::MeleeRange, 200.f);
	const float HalfArcCos = FMath::Cos(FMath::DegreesToRadians(FMath::Clamp(GetStat(GBStat::MeleeArc, 90.f), 0.f, 360.f) * 0.5f));
	if (Range <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	FCollisionQueryParams OverlapParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSwordOverlap), false, Player);
	OverlapParams.AddIgnoredActor(this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Range), OverlapParams);

	const FGBDamageInfo BaseInfo = MakeDamageInfo();
	TSet<AActor*> Done;
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Target = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (!Target || Done.Contains(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Done.Add(Target);

		if (!UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target) || !UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Target) || !UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(Player, Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		const FVector TargetLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
		const FVector ToTarget = TargetLocation - Origin;
		const FVector ToTargetDir = ToTarget.GetSafeNormal();
		if (!ToTargetDir.IsNearlyZero() && FVector::DotProduct(Forward, ToTargetDir) < HalfArcCos)
		{
			continue;
		}

		// Not through walls.
		FCollisionQueryParams WallParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSwordWall), false, Player);
		WallParams.AddIgnoredActor(this);
		WallParams.AddIgnoredActor(Target);
		if (World->LineTraceTestByChannel(Origin, TargetLocation, ECC_Visibility, WallParams))
		{
			continue;
		}

		FGBDamageInfo Info = BaseInfo;
		Info.HitLocation = TargetLocation;
		Info.HitDirection = ToTargetDir;
		UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
	}
	return true;
}

#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"

namespace GBAttributePrivate
{
	// Fallbacks only for an actor whose data has no MaxHealth / MaxMana (a warning is logged).
	constexpr float FallbackMaxHealth = 100.f;
	constexpr float FallbackMaxMana = 100.f;

	float ReadStat(const UGBStatsComponent* Stats, FName Stat, float DefaultValue)
	{
		return Stats ? Stats->GetStat(Stat, DefaultValue) : DefaultValue;
	}
}

UGBAttributeComponent::UGBAttributeComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryComponentTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
}

UGBAttributeComponent* UGBAttributeComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBAttributeComponent>() : nullptr;
}

UGBStatsComponent* UGBAttributeComponent::GetStats() const
{
	return UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::GetMaxHealth() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0.f, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::MaxHealth, GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxHealth));
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::GetMaxMana() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0.f, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::MaxMana, GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxMana));
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBStatsComponent* OwnerStats = GetStats())
	{
		OwnerStats->OnStatsChanged.AddWeakLambda(this, [this]()
		{
			// Max values may have dropped (armor removed, effect ended): clamp and let the HUD redraw.
			Health = FMath::Min(Health, GetMaxHealth());
			Mana = FMath::Min(Mana, GetMaxMana());
			OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
			OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
		});
	}

	// Safety net for owners that set their stats before BeginPlay but never call InitializeFromStats.
	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();
	if (!bDead && Health <= 0.f && Stats && Stats->HasBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth))
	{
		InitializeFromStats();
	}
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::InitializeFromStats()
{
	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();
	if (!Stats || !Stats->HasBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: no MaxHealth stat in the data; using %f."), *GetNameSafe(GetOwner()), GBAttributePrivate::FallbackMaxHealth);
	}

	bDead = false;
	Health = GetMaxHealth();
	Mana = GetMaxMana();
	TimeSinceDamage = 1000.f;
	TimeSinceManaSpend = 1000.f;
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::ApplyDamage(const FGBDamageInfo& Info)
{
	if (bDead || bInvulnerable || Info.Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	FGBDamageInfo Hit = Info;
	OnModifyIncomingDamage.Broadcast(Hit);

	const float Amount = FMath::Max(0.f, Hit.Amount * GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(GetStats(), GBStat::DamageTaken, 1.f));
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		// Fully blocked or cancelled: not a hit, so no damage events and the regen delay keeps running.
		return 0.f;
	}

	const float Actual = FMath::Min(Amount, Health);
	Health -= Actual;
	TimeSinceDamage = 0.f;

	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnDamaged.Broadcast(Hit, Actual);

	if (Health <= 0.f && !bDead)
	{
		Health = 0.f;
		bDead = true;
		OnDeath.Broadcast(Hit);
	}
	return Actual;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::Heal(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewHealth = FMath::Min(Health + Amount, GetMaxHealth());
	if (NewHealth != Health)
	{
		Health = NewHealth;
		OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	}
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreMana(float Amount)
{
	if (bDead || Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float NewMana = FMath::Min(Mana + Amount, GetMaxMana());
	if (NewMana != Mana)
	{
		Mana = NewMana;
		OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	}
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::HasMana(float Amount) const
{
	return Mana >= Amount;
}

bool UGBAttributeComponent::SpendMana(float Amount)
{
	if (Amount <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}
	if (Mana < Amount)
	{
		return false;
	}
	Mana -= Amount;
	TimeSinceManaSpend = 0.f;
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
	return true;
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::RestoreAll()
{
	bDead = false;
	Health = GetMaxHealth();
	Mana = GetMaxMana();
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::SetHealthAndMana(float NewHealth, float NewMana)
{
	Health = FMath::Clamp(NewHealth, 0.f, GetMaxHealth());
	Mana = FMath::Clamp(NewMana, 0.f, GetMaxMana());
	// A snapshot with health revives; one at 0 leaves the owner dead (no OnDeath: nothing hit it).
	bDead = Health <= 0.f;
	OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, GetMaxHealth());
	OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, GetMaxMana());
}

float UGBAttributeComponent::StepRegen(float Current, float Max, float RegenPerSecond, float RegenDelay, float TimeSinceLastLoss, float DeltaTime)
{
	if (Current >= Max || RegenPerSecond <= 0.f || DeltaTime <= 0.f)
	{
		return Current;
	}
	// Only the part of this step that lies past the delay regenerates, so the result does not depend on frame rate.
	const float RegenTime = FMath::Clamp(TimeSinceLastLoss + DeltaTime - FMath::Max(0.f, RegenDelay), 0.f, DeltaTime);
	return FMath::Min(Current + RegenPerSecond * RegenTime, Max);
}

void UGBAttributeComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);

	if (!bDead)
	{
		const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetStats();

		const float MaxHealth = GetMaxHealth();
		const float NewHealth = StepRegen(Health, MaxHealth, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::HealthRegen, 0.f),
			GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::HealthRegenDelay, 0.f), TimeSinceDamage, DeltaTime);
		if (NewHealth != Health)
		{
			Health = NewHealth;
			OnHealthChanged.Broadcast(Health, MaxHealth);
		}

		const float MaxMana = GetMaxMana();
		const float NewMana = StepRegen(Mana, MaxMana, GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::ManaRegen, 0.f),
			GBAttributePrivate::ReadStat(Stats, GBStat::ManaRegenDelay, 0.f), TimeSinceManaSpend, DeltaTime);
		if (NewMana != Mana)
		{
			Mana = NewMana;
			OnManaChanged.Broadcast(Mana, MaxMana);
		}
	}

	TimeSinceDamage += DeltaTime;
	TimeSinceManaSpend += DeltaTime;
}
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"

#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"

UGBStatsComponent::UGBStatsComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

UGBStatsComponent* UGBStatsComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBStatsComponent>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStats(const TMap<FName, float>& InBaseStats)
{
	BaseStats = InBaseStats;
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::SetBaseStat(FName Stat, float Value)
{
	BaseStats.Add(Stat, Value);
	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasBaseStat(FName Stat) const
{
	return BaseStats.Contains(Stat);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	const float* Base = BaseStats.Find(Stat);
	return Evaluate(Stat, Base ? *Base : DefaultValue);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::Evaluate(FName Key, float Base) const
{
	return EvaluateKeys(MakeArrayView(&Key, 1), Base);
}

float UGBStatsComponent::EvaluateKeys(TConstArrayView<FName> Keys, float Base) const
{
	TArray<FGBModifierDef, TInlineAllocator<8>> Matching;
	for (const FSourcedModifier& Entry : ActiveModifiers)
	{
		if (Keys.Contains(Entry.Modifier.Stat))
		{
			Matching.Add(Entry.Modifier);
		}
	}
	return ApplyModifiers(Base, Matching);
}

void UGBStatsComponent::AddModifiers(FName Source, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers, float ValueScale)
{
	ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		FSourcedModifier& Entry = ActiveModifiers.AddDefaulted_GetRef();
		Entry.Source = Source;
		Entry.Modifier = Modifier;
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Entry.Modifier.Value = Modifier.Value * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			// Scaled around 1, so a x1.5 buff at half strength is x1.25, not x0.75.
			Entry.Modifier.Value = 1.f + (Modifier.Value - 1.f) * ValueScale;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			break;
		}
	}

	OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatsComponent::RemoveModifiers(FName Source)
{
	if (ActiveModifiers.RemoveAll([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; }) > 0)
	{
		OnStatsChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

bool UGBStatsComponent::HasModifierSource(FName Source) const
{
	return ActiveModifiers.ContainsByPredicate([Source](const FSourcedModifier& Entry) { return Entry.Source == Source; });
}

float UGBStatsComponent::ApplyModifiers(float Base, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers)
{
	float Sum = Base;
	float Product = 1.f;
	const FGBModifierDef* LastOverride = nullptr;

	for (const FGBModifierDef& Modifier : Modifiers)
	{
		switch (Modifier.Op)
		{
		case EGBModOp::Add:
			Sum += Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Multiply:
			Product *= Modifier.Value;
			break;
		case EGBModOp::Override:
			LastOverride = &Modifier;
			break;
		}
	}

	return LastOverride ? LastOverride->Value : Sum * Product;
}

#include "Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "QTE/GBQTEComponent.h"
#include "Spells/GBSpellActor.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"

#include "Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.h"

UGBSpellbookComponent::UGBSpellbookComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
	Loadout.Init(NAME_None, NumSlots);
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		DataReloadedHandle = Data->OnDataReloaded.AddUObject(this, &UGBSpellbookComponent::ReapplyPassives);
	}
	if (UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment())
	{
		ActiveItemChangedHandle = Equipment->OnActiveItemChanged.AddUObject(this, &UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleActiveItemChanged);
	}
	if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes())
	{
		DeathHandle = Attributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleOwnerDeath);
	}
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.Remove(DataReloadedHandle);
	}
	if (UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment())
	{
		Equipment->OnActiveItemChanged.Remove(ActiveItemChangedHandle);
	}
	if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes())
	{
		Attributes->OnDeath.Remove(DeathHandle);
	}
	CancelSustained();
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

// ---- Owner components ----

UGBStatsComponent* UGBSpellbookComponent::GetOwnerStats() const
{
	return UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
}

UGBAttributeComponent* UGBSpellbookComponent::GetOwnerAttributes() const
{
	return UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetOwner());
}

UGBEquipmentComponent* UGBSpellbookComponent::GetOwnerEquipment() const
{
	return GetOwner() ? GetOwner()->FindComponentByClass<UGBEquipmentComponent>() : nullptr;
}

UGBQTEComponent* UGBSpellbookComponent::GetOwnerQTE() const
{
	return GetOwner() ? GetOwner()->FindComponentByClass<UGBQTEComponent>() : nullptr;
}

double UGBSpellbookComponent::GetNow() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? World->GetTimeSeconds() : 0.0;
}

// ---- Setup ----

void UGBSpellbookComponent::InitializeFromData()
{
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spellbook: no data subsystem, starting with no spells."));
		return;
	}

	CancelSustained();
	RemoveAllPassives();
	KnownSpells.Reset();
	Loadout.Init(NAME_None, NumSlots);
	CooldownEnd.Reset();
	CooldownLength.Reset();

	for (const FGBSpellDefinition& Definition : Data->GetSpells())
	{
		if (Definition.bStartsKnown)
		{
			LearnSpellInternal(Definition.Id, false);
		}
	}

	const FGBPlayerDefinition& Player = Data->GetPlayer();
	for (const FName SpellId : Player.StartingSpells)
	{
		LearnSpellInternal(SpellId, false);
	}

	EnchantSpellId = Player.EnchantSpell;
	EnchantElement = Player.StartingEnchantElement;
	if (!EnchantSpellId.IsNone())
	{
		LearnSpellInternal(EnchantSpellId, false);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spellbook: Player.json has no EnchantSpell; the enchant slot is empty."));
	}

	int32 Slot = 0;
	for (const FName SpellId : Player.StartingLoadout)
	{
		if (Slot >= NumSlots)
		{
			break;
		}
		if (IsEquippable(SpellId))
		{
			Loadout[Slot++] = SpellId;
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spellbook: starting loadout spell '%s' is unknown, not known or not equippable; skipped."), *SpellId.ToString());
		}
	}

	OnLoadoutChanged.Broadcast();
}

// ---- Knowledge ----

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::KnowsSpell(FName SpellId) const
{
	return !SpellId.IsNone() && KnownSpells.Contains(SpellId);
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::LearnSpell(FName SpellId)
{
	const bool bNew = LearnSpellInternal(SpellId, true);
	if (bNew)
	{
		OnLoadoutChanged.Broadcast();
	}
	return bNew;
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::LearnSpellInternal(FName SpellId, bool bAutoEquip)
{
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spellbook: cannot learn unknown spell '%s'."), *SpellId.ToString());
		return false;
	}
	if (KnownSpells.Contains(SpellId))
	{
		return false;
	}
	KnownSpells.Add(SpellId);

	if (Definition->bPassive)
	{
		ApplyPassive(SpellId);
	}
	else if (bAutoEquip && IsEquippable(SpellId) && !Loadout.Contains(SpellId))
	{
		// A newly learned spell goes into the first free slot, so it can be used without opening the loadout.
		const int32 FreeSlot = Loadout.IndexOfByKey(NAME_None);
		if (FreeSlot != INDEX_NONE)
		{
			Loadout[FreeSlot] = SpellId;
		}
	}
	return true;
}

int32 UGBSpellbookComponent::LearnGroup(FName UnlockGroup)
{
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data || UnlockGroup.IsNone())
	{
		return 0;
	}
	TArray<FName> GroupSpells;
	for (const FGBSpellDefinition& Definition : Data->GetSpells())
	{
		if (Definition.UnlockGroup == UnlockGroup)
		{
			GroupSpells.Add(Definition.Id);
		}
	}
	int32 Learned = 0;
	for (const FName SpellId : GroupSpells)
	{
		if (LearnSpellInternal(SpellId, true))
		{
			++Learned;
		}
	}
	if (Learned > 0)
	{
		OnLoadoutChanged.Broadcast();
	}
	return Learned;
}

// ---- Passives ----

FName UGBSpellbookComponent::PassiveSource(FName SpellId)
{
	return FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Passive.%s"), *SpellId.ToString()));
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::ApplyPassive(FName SpellId)
{
	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats();
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
	if (!Stats)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (!Definition || !Definition->bPassive)
	{
		Stats->RemoveModifiers(PassiveSource(SpellId));
		return;
	}
	Stats->AddModifiers(PassiveSource(SpellId), Definition->PassiveModifiers);
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::RemoveAllPassives()
{
	if (UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats())
	{
		for (const FName SpellId : KnownSpells)
		{
			Stats->RemoveModifiers(PassiveSource(SpellId));
		}
	}
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::ReapplyPassives()
{
	for (const FName SpellId : KnownSpells)
	{
		ApplyPassive(SpellId);
	}
}

// ---- Loadout ----

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::IsEquippable(FName SpellId) const
{
	if (SpellId.IsNone() || SpellId == EnchantSpellId || !KnowsSpell(SpellId))
	{
		return false;
	}
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
	return Definition && !Definition->bPassive && !Definition->bRequiresGun && !Definition->ActorClass.IsEmpty();
}

FName UGBSpellbookComponent::GetSlotSpell(int32 Slot) const
{
	return Loadout.IsValidIndex(Slot) ? Loadout[Slot] : NAME_None;
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::EquipSpell(int32 Slot, FName SpellId)
{
	if (Slot < 0 || Slot >= NumSlots)
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (Loadout.Num() != NumSlots)
	{
		Loadout.SetNum(NumSlots);
	}
	if (SpellId.IsNone())
	{
		Loadout[Slot] = NAME_None;
		OnLoadoutChanged.Broadcast();
		return true;
	}
	if (!IsEquippable(SpellId))
	{
		return false;
	}
	for (int32 Other = 0; Other < NumSlots; ++Other)
	{
		if (Other != Slot && Loadout[Other] == SpellId)
		{
			Loadout[Other] = NAME_None;
		}
	}
	Loadout[Slot] = SpellId;
	OnLoadoutChanged.Broadcast();
	return true;
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::SetEnchantElement(EGBElement InElement)
{
	if (EnchantElement != InElement)
	{
		EnchantElement = InElement;
		OnLoadoutChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

// ---- Casting ----

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::IsBusy() const
{
	if (!PendingSpellId.IsNone())
	{
		return true;
	}
	if (const UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE())
	{
		if (QTE->IsActive())
		{
			return true;
		}
	}
	return IsValid(ActiveSustained) && ActiveSustained->IsSustaining();
}

EGBCastFailReason UGBSpellbookComponent::CanCast(FName SpellId) const
{
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition || Definition->bPassive)
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell;
	}
	if (!KnowsSpell(SpellId))
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::NotKnown;
	}
	const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment();
	const bool bRightItem = Equipment && (Definition->bRequiresGun ? Equipment->IsHoldingGun() : Equipment->IsHoldingStaff());
	if (!bRightItem)
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::WrongHeldItem;
	}
	if (IsBusy())
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::Busy;
	}
	if (GetCooldownRemaining(SpellId) > 0.f)
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::OnCooldown;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes();
	const float Cost = GetManaCost(SpellId);
	if (Cost > 0.f && (!Attributes || !Attributes->HasMana(Cost)))
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::NotEnoughMana;
	}
	return EGBCastFailReason::None;
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastSlot(int32 Slot)
{
	const FName SpellId = GetSlotSpell(Slot);
	if (!SpellId.IsNone() && IsValid(ActiveSustained) && ActiveSustained->GetSpellCast().SpellId == SpellId)
	{
		ActiveSustained->OnCastReleased();
		return true;
	}
	if (SpellId.IsNone())
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(NAME_None, EGBCastFailReason::EmptySlot);
		return false;
	}
	return TryCastSpell(SpellId);
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastEnchant()
{
	if (EnchantSpellId.IsNone())
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(NAME_None, EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell);
		return false;
	}
	return TryCastSpell(EnchantSpellId);
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastSpell(FName SpellId)
{
	const EGBCastFailReason Reason = CanCast(SpellId);
	if (Reason != EGBCastFailReason::None)
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, Reason);
		return false;
	}
	return BeginCast(SpellId);
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::BeginCast(FName SpellId)
{
	const float Cost = GetManaCost(SpellId);
	UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes();
	if (Cost > 0.f && (!Attributes || !Attributes->SpendMana(Cost)))
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::NotEnoughMana);
		return false;
	}

	UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment();
	if (Equipment)
	{
		Equipment->SetSwitchLocked(true);
	}
	PendingSpellId = SpellId;

	UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE();
	if (!QTE)
	{
		// No QTE on the owner: cast at default strength, as when the QTE is switched off.
		FGBQTEResult Result;
		Result.bSuccess = true;
		Result.bUsedQTE = false;
		FinishCast(SpellId, Result);
		return true;
	}

	// The delegate may fire before StartQTE returns (QTE off).
	if (!QTE->StartQTE(SpellId, FGBOnQTEFinished::CreateUObject(this, &UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleQTEFinished, SpellId)))
	{
		PendingSpellId = NAME_None;
		if (Equipment)
		{
			Equipment->SetSwitchLocked(false);
		}
		if (Attributes && Cost > 0.f)
		{
			Attributes->RestoreMana(Cost);
		}
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::Busy);
		return false;
	}
	return true;
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleQTEFinished(const FGBQTEResult& Result, FName SpellId)
{
	FinishCast(SpellId, Result);
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::FinishCast(FName SpellId, const FGBQTEResult& Result)
{
	PendingSpellId = NAME_None;
	if (UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment())
	{
		Equipment->SetSwitchLocked(false);
	}

	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition)
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell);
		return;
	}

	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats();
	StartCooldown(SpellId, GBSpell::ResolveParam(*Definition, GBStat::Cooldown, Result.Power, Result.Reach, Result.bUsedQTE, Stats, 0.f));

	if (!Result.bSuccess)
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::QTEFailed);
		return;
	}

	FGBSpellCast SpellCast = GBSpell::ResolveCast(*Definition, Result.Power, Result.Reach, Result.bUsedQTE, Stats);
	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	SpellCast.Caster = Owner;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Owner))
	{
		SpellCast.Origin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
		SpellCast.Direction = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}
	else if (Owner)
	{
		FRotator EyeRotation;
		Owner->GetActorEyesViewPoint(SpellCast.Origin, EyeRotation);
		SpellCast.Direction = EyeRotation.Vector();
	}
	if (SpellId == EnchantSpellId)
	{
		SpellCast.Element = EnchantElement;
	}

	if (Definition->ActorClass.IsEmpty())
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("Spellbook: spell '%s' has no ActorClass in Spells.json."), *SpellId.ToString());
		return;
	}
	UClass* BehaviorClass = LoadClass<AGBSpellActor>(nullptr, *Definition->ActorClass);
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!BehaviorClass || !World)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("Spellbook: spell '%s' ActorClass '%s' is not a GBSpellActor class."), *SpellId.ToString(), *Definition->ActorClass);
		return;
	}

	const FTransform SpawnTransform(SpellCast.Direction.Rotation(), SpellCast.Origin);
	AGBSpellActor* SpellActor = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBSpellActor>(BehaviorClass, SpawnTransform, Owner,
		Cast<APawn>(Owner), ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn);
	if (!SpellActor)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("Spellbook: could not spawn the behavior of spell '%s'."), *SpellId.ToString());
		return;
	}
	SpellActor->InitSpell(SpellCast);
	SpellActor->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);

	if (IsValid(SpellActor) && SpellActor->IsSustaining())
	{
		ActiveSustained = SpellActor;
		SpellActor->OnSpellEnded.AddUObject(this, &UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleSustainedEnded);
	}
	OnSpellCast.Broadcast(SpellCast);
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleSustainedEnded()
{
	ActiveSustained = nullptr;
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleActiveItemChanged(AGBHeldItem* NewItem)
{
	CancelSustained();
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleOwnerDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	CancelSustained();
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::CancelSustained()
{
	if (IsValid(ActiveSustained))
	{
		AGBSpellActor* Sustained = ActiveSustained;
		ActiveSustained = nullptr;
		Sustained->OnCastReleased();
	}
	ActiveSustained = nullptr;
}

// ---- Cooldowns and cost ----

void UGBSpellbookComponent::StartCooldown(FName SpellId, float Seconds)
{
	if (Seconds <= 0.f)
	{
		CooldownEnd.Remove(SpellId);
		CooldownLength.Remove(SpellId);
		return;
	}
	CooldownEnd.Add(SpellId, GetNow() + Seconds);
	CooldownLength.Add(SpellId, Seconds);
}

float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetCooldownRemaining(FName SpellId) const
{
	const double* End = CooldownEnd.Find(SpellId);
	return End ? static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(0.0, *End - GetNow())) : 0.f;
}

float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetCooldownDuration(FName SpellId) const
{
	return CooldownLength.FindRef(SpellId);
}

float UGBSpellbookComponent::GetManaCost(FName SpellId) const
{
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	return FMath::Max(0.f, GBSpell::ResolveParam(*Definition, GBStat::ManaCost, 0.f, 0.f, false, GetOwnerStats(), 0.f));
}

// ---- Snapshot ----

void UGBSpellbookComponent::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.KnownSpells = KnownSpells;
	Snapshot.Loadout = Loadout;
	Snapshot.EnchantElement = EnchantElement;
}

void UGBSpellbookComponent::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{
	CancelSustained();
	RemoveAllPassives();

	KnownSpells.Reset();
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	for (const FName SpellId : Snapshot.KnownSpells)
	{
		if (Data && Data->FindSpell(SpellId))
		{
			KnownSpells.AddUnique(SpellId);
		}
	}
	if (!EnchantSpellId.IsNone() && Data && Data->FindSpell(EnchantSpellId))
	{
		KnownSpells.AddUnique(EnchantSpellId);
	}

	Loadout.Init(NAME_None, NumSlots);
	for (int32 Slot = 0; Slot < NumSlots && Slot < Snapshot.Loadout.Num(); ++Slot)
	{
		const FName SpellId = Snapshot.Loadout[Slot];
		if (IsEquippable(SpellId) && !Loadout.Contains(SpellId))
		{
			Loadout[Slot] = SpellId;
		}
	}
	EnchantElement = Snapshot.EnchantElement;

	CooldownEnd.Reset();
	CooldownLength.Reset();
	ReapplyPassives();
	OnLoadoutChanged.Broadcast();
}

 succeeded in 161ms:
#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"

#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"

namespace
{
	// Float slack for timers so a tick that lands exactly on the end of the effect still runs.
	constexpr float GBEffectTimeEpsilon = 1.e-4f;

	const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* FindDefinition(const UObject* Context, FName EffectId)
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(Context);
		return Data ? Data->FindStatusEffect(EffectId) : nullptr;
	}
}

UGBStatusEffectComponent::UGBStatusEffectComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryComponentTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
}

UGBStatusEffectComponent* UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(const AActor* Actor)
{
	return Actor ? Actor->FindComponentByClass<UGBStatusEffectComponent>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetOwner()))
	{
		Attributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &UGBStatusEffectComponent::HandleOwnerDeath);
	}
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::HandleOwnerDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	RemoveAllEffects();
}

bool UGBStatusEffectComponent::ApplyEffect(FName EffectId, AActor* Instigator, float DurationScale, float StrengthScale)
{
	const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* FoundDefinition = FindDefinition(this, EffectId);
	if (!FoundDefinition)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("StatusEffect: unknown effect id '%s'"), *EffectId.ToString());
		return false;
	}
	// Copy: applying damage below runs other systems' callbacks; never hold a definition pointer across them.
	const FGBStatusEffectDefinition Definition = *FoundDefinition;

	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Owner);
	if (!Owner || (Attributes && Attributes->IsDead()))
	{
		return false;
	}

	const float Strength = FMath::Max(0.f, StrengthScale);
	const float Duration = Definition.Duration * FMath::Max(0.f, DurationScale);

	ApplyHealthAndMana(EffectId, Definition.InstantHealth * Strength, Definition.InstantMana * Strength, Definition.Element, Instigator);

	// The instant part may have killed the owner (death clears every effect).
	if (Attributes && Attributes->IsDead())
	{
		return true;
	}
	if (Duration <= 0.f)
	{
		return true;
	}

	int32 ExistingCount = 0;
	int32 FirstIndex = INDEX_NONE;
	int32 ShortestIndex = INDEX_NONE;
	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < ActiveEffects.Num(); ++Index)
	{
		if (ActiveEffects[Index].EffectId != EffectId)
		{
			continue;
		}
		++ExistingCount;
		if (FirstIndex == INDEX_NONE)
		{
			FirstIndex = Index;
		}
		if (ShortestIndex == INDEX_NONE || ActiveEffects[Index].TimeRemaining < ActiveEffects[ShortestIndex].TimeRemaining)
		{
			ShortestIndex = Index;
		}
	}

	if (ExistingCount > 0 && Definition.bRefreshOnReapply)
	{
		RefreshEntry(FirstIndex, Duration, Strength, Instigator);
		return true;
	}
	if (ExistingCount >= FMath::Max(1, Definition.MaxStacks))
	{
		// At max stacks: renew the stack closest to running out.
		RefreshEntry(ShortestIndex, Duration, Strength, Instigator);
		return true;
	}

	FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry = ActiveEffects.AddDefaulted_GetRef();
	Entry.EffectId = EffectId;
	Entry.TimeRemaining = Duration;
	Entry.TotalDuration = Duration;
	Entry.TimeToNextTick = Definition.TickInterval;
	Entry.StrengthScale = Strength;
	Entry.Instigator = Instigator;
	Entry.ModifierSource = FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Effect.%s#%d"), *EffectId.ToString(), NextEffectSerial++));
	const FName ModifierSource = Entry.ModifierSource;

	UpdateStackCount(EffectId);

	if (Definition.Modifiers.Num() > 0)
	{
		if (UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(Owner))
		{
			Stats->AddModifiers(ModifierSource, Definition.Modifiers, Strength);
		}
	}

	OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
	return true;
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::RefreshEntry(int32 Index, float Duration, float StrengthScale, AActor* EffectInstigator)
{
	if (!ActiveEffects.IsValidIndex(Index))
	{
		return;
	}

	FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry = ActiveEffects[Index];
	Entry.TimeRemaining = Duration;
	Entry.TotalDuration = Duration;
	if (EffectInstigator)
	{
		Entry.Instigator = EffectInstigator;
	}

	if (StrengthScale > Entry.StrengthScale)
	{
		Entry.StrengthScale = StrengthScale;
		const FName EffectId = Entry.EffectId;
		const FName ModifierSource = Entry.ModifierSource;
		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Definition = FindDefinition(this, EffectId);
		UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
		if (Definition && Stats && Definition->Modifiers.Num() > 0)
		{
			// Same source name: replaces the old modifiers with the stronger ones.
			const TArray<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers = Definition->Modifiers;
			Stats->AddModifiers(ModifierSource, Modifiers, StrengthScale);
		}
	}

	OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::UpdateStackCount(FName EffectId)
{
	int32 Count = 0;
	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : ActiveEffects)
	{
		if (Entry.EffectId == EffectId)
		{
			++Count;
		}
	}
	for (FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : ActiveEffects)
	{
		if (Entry.EffectId == EffectId)
		{
			Entry.Stacks = Count;
		}
	}
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::ApplyHealthAndMana(FName EffectId, float HealthAmount, float ManaAmount, EGBElement Element, AActor* EffectInstigator)
{
	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Owner);
	if (!Attributes || (FMath::IsNearlyZero(HealthAmount) && FMath::IsNearlyZero(ManaAmount)))
	{
		return;
	}

	if (HealthAmount > 0.f)
	{
		Attributes->Heal(HealthAmount);
	}
	else if (HealthAmount < 0.f)
	{
		FGBDamageInfo Info;
		Info.Amount = -HealthAmount;
		Info.Element = Element;
		Info.Instigator = IsValid(EffectInstigator) ? EffectInstigator : nullptr;
		Info.HitLocation = Owner->GetActorLocation();
		Info.SourceId = EffectId;
		// OnHitEffect stays None: periodic damage must not re-apply effects.
		UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Owner, Info, /*bAllowFriendlyFire*/ true);
	}

	if (Attributes->IsDead())
	{
		return;
	}

	if (ManaAmount > 0.f)
	{
		Attributes->RestoreMana(ManaAmount);
	}
	else if (ManaAmount < 0.f)
	{
		const float Drain = FMath::Min(-ManaAmount, Attributes->GetMana());
		if (Drain > 0.f)
		{
			Attributes->SpendMana(Drain);
		}
	}
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::RemoveEffect(FName EffectId)
{
	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	bool bRemoved = false;
	for (int32 Index = ActiveEffects.Num() - 1; Index >= 0; --Index)
	{
		if (ActiveEffects[Index].EffectId != EffectId)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FName ModifierSource = ActiveEffects[Index].ModifierSource;
		ActiveEffects.RemoveAt(Index);
		if (Stats)
		{
			Stats->RemoveModifiers(ModifierSource);
		}
		bRemoved = true;
	}

	if (bRemoved)
	{
		OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::RemoveAllEffects()
{
	if (ActiveEffects.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}

	TArray<FGBActiveStatusEffect> Removed = MoveTemp(ActiveEffects);
	ActiveEffects.Reset();

	if (UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner()))
	{
		for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : Removed)
		{
			Stats->RemoveModifiers(Entry.ModifierSource);
		}
	}

	OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBStatusEffectComponent::HasEffect(FName EffectId) const
{
	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : ActiveEffects)
	{
		if (Entry.EffectId == EffectId)
		{
			return true;
		}
	}
	return false;
}

float UGBStatusEffectComponent::GetTimeRemaining(FName EffectId) const
{
	float Remaining = 0.f;
	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry : ActiveEffects)
	{
		if (Entry.EffectId == EffectId)
		{
			Remaining = FMath::Max(Remaining, Entry.TimeRemaining);
		}
	}
	return Remaining;
}

void UGBStatusEffectComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);

	if (ActiveEffects.Num() == 0)
	{
		return;
	}

	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	if (Attributes && Attributes->IsDead())
	{
		return;
	}

	struct FPendingTick
	{
		FName EffectId;
		float Health = 0.f;
		float Mana = 0.f;
		EGBElement Element = EGBElement::Physical;
		TWeakObjectPtr<AActor> Instigator;
	};

	// Phase 1: advance timers only (no callbacks), so the array is not changed while we walk it.
	TArray<FPendingTick> PendingTicks;
	TArray<int32> ExpiredIndices;
	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < ActiveEffects.Num(); ++Index)
	{
		FGBActiveStatusEffect& Entry = ActiveEffects[Index];
		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Definition = FindDefinition(this, Entry.EffectId);
		if (!Definition)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("StatusEffect: effect '%s' is no longer in the data; removing it"), *Entry.EffectId.ToString());
			ExpiredIndices.Add(Index);
			continue;
		}

		// Ticks only count time inside the effect, so none run after its end.
		const float ActiveStep = FMath::Min(DeltaTime, FMath::Max(0.f, Entry.TimeRemaining));
		if (Definition->TickInterval > 0.f)
		{
			Entry.TimeToNextTick -= ActiveStep;
			while (Entry.TimeToNextTick <= GBEffectTimeEpsilon)
			{
				Entry.TimeToNextTick += Definition->TickInterval;
				FPendingTick& Pending = PendingTicks.AddDefaulted_GetRef();
				Pending.EffectId = Entry.EffectId;
				Pending.Health = Definition->HealthPerTick * Entry.StrengthScale;
				Pending.Mana = Definition->ManaPerTick * Entry.StrengthScale;
				Pending.Element = Definition->Element;
				Pending.Instigator = Entry.Instigator;
			}
		}

		Entry.TimeRemaining -= DeltaTime;
		if (Entry.TimeRemaining <= GBEffectTimeEpsilon)
		{
			ExpiredIndices.Add(Index);
		}
	}

	// Phase 2: remove expired entries and their modifiers.
	if (ExpiredIndices.Num() > 0)
	{
		UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
		TArray<FName> ChangedIds;
		for (int32 i = ExpiredIndices.Num() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
		{
			const int32 Index = ExpiredIndices[i];
			const FName ModifierSource = ActiveEffects[Index].ModifierSource;
			ChangedIds.AddUnique(ActiveEffects[Index].EffectId);
			ActiveEffects.RemoveAt(Index);
			if (Stats)
			{
				Stats->RemoveModifiers(ModifierSource);
			}
		}
		for (const FName& EffectId : ChangedIds)
		{
			UpdateStackCount(EffectId);
		}
		OnEffectsChanged.Broadcast();
	}

	// Phase 3: run the ticks (they may damage, kill and clear everything).
	for (const FPendingTick& Pending : PendingTicks)
	{
		if (Attributes && Attributes->IsDead())
		{
			break;
		}
		ApplyHealthAndMana(Pending.EffectId, Pending.Health, Pending.Mana, Pending.Element, Pending.Instigator.Get());
	}
}

#include "Spells/GBSpellActor.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"

namespace
{
	// Pure geometry, not gameplay: how far the aimed point is pulled back from a wall, and how high above a point
	// the ground search starts (so a slope or a step under the aimed point is still found).
	constexpr float SpellActorAimPullBackCm = 20.f;
	constexpr float SpellActorGroundLiftCm = 100.f;
	constexpr float SpellActorMinTickInterval = 0.05f;
