AGBSpellActor::AGBSpellActor()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = false;
	Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	RootComponent = Root;
}

void AGBSpellActor::InitSpell(const FGBSpellCast& InCast)
{
	SpellCast = InCast;
	bSpellInitialized = true;
	if (HasActorBegunPlay())
	{
		TryStartSpell();
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	if (!bSpellInitialized)
	{
		// Spawned without the spellbook (placed in a level, or InitSpell will come right after FinishSpawning).
		return;
	}
	TryStartSpell();
}

void AGBSpellActor::TryStartSpell()
{
	if (bSpellStarted || bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	bSpellStarted = true;
	StartSpell();
}

void AGBSpellActor::OnCastReleased()
{
	EndSpell();
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndSpell()
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	bEnded = true;
	GetWorldTimerManager().ClearAllTimersForObject(this);
	OnSpellEnded.Broadcast();
	Destroy();
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	// Destroyed by something else (level unload, era swap): the spellbook must still hear that the spell is over.
	if (!bEnded)
	{
		bEnded = true;
		OnSpellEnded.Broadcast();
	}
	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
	{
		World->GetTimerManager().ClearAllTimersForObject(this);
	}
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

bool AGBSpellActor::FindGround(const FVector& From, FVector& OutGround, float MaxDistance) const
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return false;
	}
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellFindGround), false, this);
	if (AActor* Caster = GetCaster())
	{
		Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
	}
	FHitResult Hit;
	const FVector End = From - FVector::UpVector * MaxDistance;
	if (World->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, From, End, FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		OutGround = Hit.ImpactPoint;
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

float AGBSpellActor::ReadParam(FName Param, float DefaultValue) const
{
	if (const float* Value = SpellCast.Values.Find(Param))
	{
		return *Value;
	}
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s' (%s): param '%s' is missing in Spells.json, using %.2f."),
		*SpellCast.SpellId.ToString(), *GetClass()->GetName(), *Param.ToString(), DefaultValue);
	return DefaultValue;
}

FLinearColor AGBSpellActor::GetSpellColor() const
{
	const FLinearColor ElementColor = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(SpellCast.Element));
	return UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(SpellCast.GetString(GBString::Color), ElementColor);
}

FName AGBSpellActor::GetSpriteId() const
{
	const FString Sprite = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::Sprite);
	return Sprite.IsEmpty() ? NAME_None : FName(*Sprite);
}

void AGBSpellActor::SetupSpellSprite(UBillboardComponent* Billboard, float DefaultHeight) const
{
	if (!Billboard)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FName SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this);
	if (SpriteId.IsNone() || !Library)
	{
		Billboard->SetHiddenInGame(true);
		Billboard->SetVisibility(false);
		return;
	}
	Library->SetupBillboard(Billboard, SpriteId, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, DefaultHeight));
}

void AGBSpellActor::GetCasterAim(FVector& OutOrigin, FVector& OutDirection) const
{
	OutOrigin = SpellCast.Origin;
	OutDirection = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Caster))
	{
		OutOrigin = Player->GetAimOrigin();
		OutDirection = Player->GetAimDirection();
	}
	else if (Caster)
	{
		FRotator EyeRotation;
		Caster->GetActorEyesViewPoint(OutOrigin, EyeRotation);
		OutDirection = EyeRotation.Vector();
	}
	if (OutDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		OutDirection = FVector::ForwardVector;
	}
}

FVector AGBSpellActor::FindAimedGroundPoint(float MaxRange) const
{
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	FVector Fallback = Caster ? Caster->GetActorLocation() : SpellCast.Origin;
	if (Caster)
	{
		Fallback.Z -= Caster->GetSimpleCollisionHalfHeight();
	}

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return Fallback;
	}

	const FVector Dir = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	FVector Point = SpellCast.Origin;
	if (MaxRange > 0.f && !Dir.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellAim), false, this);
		if (Caster)
		{
			Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
		}
		FHitResult Hit;
		const FVector End = SpellCast.Origin + Dir * MaxRange;
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, SpellCast.Origin, End, ECC_Visibility, Params))
		{
			Point = Hit.ImpactPoint - Dir * SpellActorAimPullBackCm;
		}
		else
		{
			Point = End;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		return FindGround(Fallback + FVector::UpVector * SpellActorGroundLiftCm, Point) ? Point : Fallback;
	}

	FVector Ground;
	if (FindGround(Point + FVector::UpVector * SpellActorGroundLiftCm, Ground))
	{
		return Ground;
	}
	return Fallback;
}

bool AGBSpellActor::IsHostileTarget(const AActor* Actor) const
{
	if (!Actor || Actor == this || Actor == GetCaster())
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (!UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Actor) || !UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Actor))
	{
		return false;
	}
	return UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(GetCaster(), Actor);
}

void AGBSpellActor::QueryHostiles(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FCollisionShape& Shape, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}
	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellArea), false, this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Center, Rotation, ObjectParams, Shape, Params);
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Actor = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (IsHostileTarget(Actor))
		{
			OutActors.AddUnique(Actor);
		}
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInSphere(const FVector& Center, float Radius, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Radius), OutActors);
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesOnDisc(const FVector& GroundCenter, float Radius, float Height, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Radius <= 0.f || Height <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector BoxCenter = GroundCenter + FVector(0.f, 0.f, Height * 0.5f);
	QueryHostiles(BoxCenter, FQuat::Identity, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(FVector(Radius, Radius, Height * 0.5f)), OutActors);
	// The box is only the broad phase: keep actors whose collision touches the disc horizontally.
	OutActors.RemoveAll([&GroundCenter, Radius](const AActor* Actor)
	{
		return FVector::Dist2D(Actor->GetActorLocation(), GroundCenter) > Radius + Actor->GetSimpleCollisionRadius();
	});
}

void AGBSpellActor::GatherHostilesInBox(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FVector& Extent, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const
{
	OutActors.Reset();
	if (Extent.GetMin() <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	QueryHostiles(Center, Rotation, FCollisionShape::MakeBox(Extent), OutActors);
}

void AGBSpellActor::EndSpellAfter(float Seconds)
{
	if (bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (Seconds <= 0.f)
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimerForNextTick(this, &AGBSpellActor::EndSpell);
	}
	else
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(EndTimer, this, &AGBSpellActor::EndSpell, Seconds, false);
	}
}

void AGBSpellActor::DamageTarget(AActor* Target, float Amount, const FVector& SourceLocation)
{
	if (!Target)
	{
		return;
	}
	FGBDamageInfo Info = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(NAME_None, this);
	Info.Amount = Amount;
	Info.HitLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
	Info.HitDirection = (Target->GetActorLocation() - SourceLocation).GetSafeNormal();
	UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, Info);
}

void AGBSpellActor::ApplyOnHitEffect(AActor* Target) const
{
	const FString EffectId = SpellCast.GetString(GBString::OnHitEffect);
	if (!Target || EffectId.IsEmpty())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(Target))
	{
		Effects->ApplyEffect(FName(*EffectId), GetCaster(),
			SpellCast.Get(GBStat::EffectDuration, 1.f), SpellCast.Get(GBStat::EffectStrength, 1.f));
	}
}

float AGBSpellActor::SafeInterval(float Interval)
{
	return FMath::Max(Interval, SpellActorMinTickInterval);
}
#include "Spells/GBSpellTypes.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"

float FGBSpellCast::Get(FName Param, float DefaultValue) const
{
	const float* Value = Values.Find(Param);
	return Value ? *Value : DefaultValue;
}

bool FGBSpellCast::Has(FName Param) const
{
	return Values.Contains(Param);
}

FString FGBSpellCast::GetString(FName Key, const FString& DefaultValue) const
{
	const FString* Value = Strings.Find(Key);
	return Value ? *Value : DefaultValue;
}

FGBDamageInfo FGBSpellCast::MakeDamageInfo(FName AmountParam, AActor* Causer) const
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = Get(AmountParam);
	Info.Element = Element;
	Info.Instigator = Caster;
	Info.Causer = Causer;
	Info.SourceId = SpellId;
	if (const FString* Effect = Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
	{
		if (!Effect->IsEmpty())
		{
			Info.OnHitEffect = FName(**Effect);
		}
	}
	Info.EffectDurationScale = Get(GBStat::EffectDuration, 1.f);
	Info.EffectStrengthScale = Get(GBStat::EffectStrength, 1.f);
	return Info;
}

namespace GBSpell
{
	static float ApplySpellModifiers(FName SpellId, FName Param, float Value, const UGBStatsComponent* Stats)
	{
		if (!Stats)
		{
			return Value;
		}
		const FName Keys[] = { GBStat::SpellKey(SpellId, Param), GBStat::SpellAllKey(Param) };
		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Value);
	}

	FGBSpellCast ResolveCast(const FGBSpellDefinition& Definition, float Power, float Reach, bool bUseQTE, const UGBStatsComponent* Stats)
	{
		FGBSpellCast Result;
		Result.SpellId = Definition.Id;
		Result.Element = Definition.Element;
		Result.Power = Power;
		Result.Reach = Reach;
		Result.bUsedQTE = bUseQTE;
		Result.Strings = Definition.Strings;
		Result.Values.Reserve(Definition.Params.Num());
		for (const TPair<FName, FGBScaledParam>& Pair : Definition.Params)
		{
			const float Value = Pair.Value.Resolve(Power, Reach, bUseQTE);
			Result.Values.Add(Pair.Key, ApplySpellModifiers(Definition.Id, Pair.Key, Value, Stats));
		}
		return Result;
	}

	float ResolveParam(const FGBSpellDefinition& Definition, FName Param, float Power, float Reach, bool bUseQTE, const UGBStatsComponent* Stats, float DefaultValue)
	{
		const FGBScaledParam* Scaled = Definition.Params.Find(Param);
		if (!Scaled)
		{
			return DefaultValue;
		}
		return ApplySpellModifiers(Definition.Id, Param, Scaled->Resolve(Power, Reach, bUseQTE), Stats);
	}
}
#include "Spells/GBSpell_Projectile.h"
#include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"

namespace
{
	// Extra seconds of life on top of Range / Speed, so a projectile is never removed before reaching its range.
	constexpr float ProjectileSpellLifetimeMargin = 1.f;
}

void AGBSpell_Projectile::StartSpell()
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		EndSpell();
		return;
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(GBStat::Damage, this);
	Config.Speed = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Speed, 3000.f));
	Config.Radius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 12.f));
	Config.MaxRange = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 5000.f));
	Config.ExplosionRadius = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionRadius, 0.f));
	Config.bExplosionFalloff = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ExplosionFalloff, 1.f) > 0.5f;
	Config.GravityScale = SpellCast.Get(GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 0.f);
	Config.bPierce = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::Pierce, 0.f) > 0.5f;
	Config.bPixelArt = true;
	Config.SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	Config.Color = GetSpellColor();
	Config.VisualScale = FMath::Max(0.01f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::VisualScale, 1.f));
	if (Config.MaxRange > 0.f)
	{
		Config.MaxLifetime = FMath::Max(Config.MaxLifetime, Config.MaxRange / Config.Speed + ProjectileSpellLifetimeMargin);
	}

	// Aim at what the crosshair is on, then fire from the hand so the shot does not start inside the camera.
	const FVector AimDir = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal().IsNearlyZero() ? GetActorForwardVector() : SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	const float TraceLength = Config.MaxRange > 0.f ? Config.MaxRange : Config.Speed * Config.MaxLifetime;
	FVector Target = SpellCast.Origin + AimDir * TraceLength;
	{
		FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellProjectileAim), false, this);
		if (AActor* Caster = GetCaster())
		{
			Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
		}
		FHitResult Hit;
		if (World->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, SpellCast.Origin, Target, ECC_Visibility, Params))
		{
			Target = Hit.ImpactPoint;
		}
	}

	FVector Muzzle = SpellCast.Origin;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		Muzzle = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	}
	FVector ShotDir = (Target - Muzzle).GetSafeNormal();
	if (ShotDir.IsNearlyZero() || FVector::DotProduct(ShotDir, AimDir) <= 0.f)
	{
		// The target is between the camera and the hand (point-blank wall): just fire along the aim.
		ShotDir = AimDir;
	}

	if (!AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Muzzle, ShotDir, GetCaster()))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s': the projectile could not be spawned."), *SpellCast.SpellId.ToString());
	}
	EndSpell();
}
#include "Spells/GBSpell_Orb.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "TimerManager.h"

namespace
{
	/** Visual only: how bright the area sphere is compared with the spell color. */
	constexpr float OrbAreaBrightness = 0.15f;
}

AGBSpell_Orb::AGBSpell_Orb()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = false;

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);

	Area = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Area"));
	Area->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Area->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Area->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Area->SetCastShadow(false);
}

void AGBSpell_Orb::StartSpell()
{
	MoveSpeed = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Speed, 400.f));
	RangeLeft = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 2000.f));
	OrbRadius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 250.f));
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 6.f);
	const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
	DamagePerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 20.f)) * Interval;

	MoveDirection = SpellCast.Direction.GetSafeNormal();
	if (MoveDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		MoveDirection = GetActorForwardVector();
	}
	FVector Start = SpellCast.Origin;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		Start = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	}
	SetActorLocation(Start);

	SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, 60.f);
	if (SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ShowArea, 1.f) > 0.5f)
	{
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Area, EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(OrbRadius * 2.f), GetSpellColor() * OrbAreaBrightness, true);
	}
	else
	{
		Area->SetVisibility(false);
	}

	bMoving = MoveSpeed > 0.f && RangeLeft > 0.f;
	SetActorTickEnabled(bMoving);
	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Orb::DamageTick, Interval, true, 0.f);
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Orb::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	if (!bMoving || bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Step = FMath::Min(MoveSpeed * DeltaSeconds, RangeLeft);
	const FVector From = GetActorLocation();
	const FVector To = From + MoveDirection * Step;

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellOrbMove), false, this);
	if (AActor* Caster = GetCaster())
	{
		Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);
	}
	FHitResult Hit;
	if (GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, From, To, FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		// Stops at the wall and keeps crackling there until its time runs out.
		SetActorLocation(Hit.Location);
		bMoving = false;
		SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		return;
	}

	SetActorLocation(To);
	RangeLeft -= Step;
	if (RangeLeft <= KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		EndSpell();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Orb::DamageTick()
{
	if (DamagePerTick <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	GatherHostilesInSphere(GetActorLocation(), OrbRadius, Targets);
	for (AActor* Target : Targets)
	{
		DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
	}
}

#include "Spells/GBSpell_Beam.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"
#include "TimerManager.h"
#include "UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h"

namespace
{
	// Visual only: how many sprites draw the cone.
	constexpr int32 BeamSpriteCount = 4;
}

AGBSpell_Beam::AGBSpell_Beam()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = false;
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::StartSpell()
{
	BeamRange = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 600.f));
	ConeHalfAngleDeg = FMath::Clamp(ReadParam(GBSpellParam::ConeAngle, 20.f), 1.f, 89.f);
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 4.f);
	const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.2f));
	DamagePerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadParam(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 30.f)) * Interval;
	ManaPerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::ManaPerSecond, 0.f)) * Interval;

	// Sprites along the cone, growing with its width.
	const FName SpriteId = GetSpriteId();
	UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this);
	if (!SpriteId.IsNone() && Library)
	{
		const float NearHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, 40.f));
		const float TanHalfAngle = FMath::Tan(FMath::DegreesToRadians(ConeHalfAngleDeg));
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < BeamSpriteCount; ++Index)
		{
			const float Along = BeamRange * (Index + 1) / (BeamSpriteCount + 1);
			UBillboardComponent* Sprite = NewObject<UBillboardComponent>(this);
			Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);
			Sprite->RegisterComponent();
			AddInstanceComponent(Sprite);
			Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(Along, 0.f, 0.f));
			Library->SetupBillboard(Sprite, SpriteId, FMath::Max(NearHeight, 2.f * Along * TanHalfAngle));
			Sprites.Add(Sprite);
		}
	}

	FollowAim();
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Beam::DamageTick, Interval, true, 0.f);
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	if (!bEnded)
	{
		FollowAim();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::FollowAim()
{
	FVector AimOrigin;
	FVector AimDir;
	GetCasterAim(AimOrigin, AimDir);
	FVector Start = AimOrigin;
	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetCaster()))
	{
		Start = Player->GetMuzzleLocation();
	}
	SetActorLocationAndRotation(Start, AimDir.Rotation());
}

void AGBSpell_Beam::DamageTick()
{
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	if (!Caster || !UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Caster))
	{
		EndSpell();
		return;
	}
	if (ManaPerTick > 0.f)
	{
		UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Caster);
		if (!Attributes || !Attributes->SpendMana(ManaPerTick))
		{
			EndSpell();
			return;
		}
	}
	if (DamagePerTick <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	FVector AimOrigin;
	FVector AimDir;
	GetCasterAim(AimOrigin, AimDir);
	const float MinDot = FMath::Cos(FMath::DegreesToRadians(ConeHalfAngleDeg));

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBSpellBeamSight), false, this);
	Params.AddIgnoredActor(Caster);

	TArray<AActor*> Candidates;
	GatherHostilesInSphere(AimOrigin, BeamRange, Candidates);
	for (AActor* Target : Candidates)
	{
		const FVector TargetLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
		const FVector ToTarget = TargetLocation - AimOrigin;
		const float Distance = ToTarget.Size();
		if (Distance > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER && FVector::DotProduct(ToTarget / Distance, AimDir) < MinDot)
		{
			continue;
		}
		if (GetWorld()->LineTraceTestByObjectType(AimOrigin, TargetLocation, FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
		{
			continue;
		}
		DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, AimOrigin);
	}
}
#include "Spells/GBSpell_Shield.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"

AGBSpell_Shield::AGBSpell_Shield()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = false;

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);
}

float AGBSpell_Shield::ComputeBlockedDamage(float Amount, const FVector& HitDirection, const FVector& AimDirection, float InBlockFraction, float ShieldHealthLeft)
{
	if (Amount <= 0.f || ShieldHealthLeft <= 0.f || HitDirection.IsNearlyZero() || AimDirection.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	// A hit from the front travels against the aim.
	if (FVector::DotProduct(HitDirection.GetSafeNormal(), AimDirection.GetSafeNormal()) >= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	return FMath::Min(Amount * FMath::Clamp(InBlockFraction, 0.f, 1.f), ShieldHealthLeft);
}

void AGBSpell_Shield::StartSpell()
{
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 5.f);
	BlockFraction = FMath::Clamp(ReadParam(GBStat::BlockFraction, 0.75f), 0.f, 1.f);
	ShieldHealth = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Health, 150.f));
	SpriteDistance = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::Distance, 80.f);

	if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(GetCaster()))
	{
		BoundAttributes = Attributes;
		DamageHandle = Attributes->OnModifyIncomingDamage.AddUObject(this, &AGBSpell_Shield::HandleIncomingDamage);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s': the caster has no attributes, the shield cannot block."), *SpellCast.SpellId.ToString());
	}

	SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, 45.f);
	FollowAim();
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Shield::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = BoundAttributes.Get())
	{
		Attributes->OnModifyIncomingDamage.Remove(DamageHandle);
	}
	BoundAttributes.Reset();
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBSpell_Shield::HandleIncomingDamage(FGBDamageInfo& Info)
{
	if (bEnded || ShieldHealth <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	FVector HitDirection = Info.HitDirection;
	if (HitDirection.IsNearlyZero() && Caster)
	{
		const AActor* Source = Info.Causer ? Info.Causer.Get() : Info.Instigator.Get();
		if (Source)
		{
			HitDirection = Caster->GetActorLocation() - Source->GetActorLocation();
		}
	}
	FVector AimOrigin;
	FVector AimDir;
	GetCasterAim(AimOrigin, AimDir);

	const float Blocked = ComputeBlockedDamage(Info.Amount, HitDirection, AimDir, BlockFraction, ShieldHealth);
	if (Blocked <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	Info.Amount -= Blocked;
	ShieldHealth -= Blocked;
	if (ShieldHealth <= KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		ShieldHealth = 0.f;
		// Broken: end after this damage call has finished.
		EndSpellAfter(0.f);
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Shield::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	if (!bEnded)
	{
		FollowAim();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Shield::FollowAim()
{
	FVector AimOrigin;
	FVector AimDir;
	GetCasterAim(AimOrigin, AimDir);
	SetActorLocationAndRotation(AimOrigin + AimDir * SpriteDistance, AimDir.Rotation());
}
#include "Spells/GBSpell_Enchant.h"
#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"

namespace
{
	const TCHAR* EnchantDefaultEffectPrefix = TEXT("Enchant_");
}

FName AGBSpell_Enchant::MakeEffectId(const FString& Prefix, EGBElement Element)
{
	const FString ElementName = StaticEnum<EGBElement>()->GetNameStringByValue(static_cast<int64>(Element));
	return FName(*(Prefix + ElementName));
}

float AGBSpell_Enchant::ComputeDurationScale(float WantedDuration, float EffectDuration)
{
	if (WantedDuration <= 0.f || EffectDuration <= 0.f)
	{
		return 1.f;
	}
	return WantedDuration / EffectDuration;
}

void AGBSpell_Enchant::StartSpell()
{
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(Caster);
	if (!Effects)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s': the caster has no status effect component, nothing to enchant."), *SpellCast.SpellId.ToString());
		EndSpell();
		return;
	}

	FString Prefix = SpellCast.GetString(GBSpellString::EffectPrefix, EnchantDefaultEffectPrefix);
	if (Prefix.IsEmpty())
	{
		// An empty prefix would match (and remove) every active effect.
		Prefix = EnchantDefaultEffectPrefix;
	}
	const FName EffectId = MakeEffectId(Prefix, SpellCast.Element);

	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* EffectDefinition = Data ? Data->FindStatusEffect(EffectId) : nullptr;
	if (!EffectDefinition)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s': status effect '%s' is not in StatusEffects.json."), *SpellCast.SpellId.ToString(), *EffectId.ToString());
		EndSpell();
		return;
	}

	// Only one enchant at a time.
	TArray<FName> ToRemove;
	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Active : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
	{
		if (Active.EffectId != EffectId && Active.EffectId.ToString().StartsWith(Prefix))
		{
			ToRemove.AddUnique(Active.EffectId);
		}
	}
	for (const FName OldId : ToRemove)
	{
		Effects->RemoveEffect(OldId);
	}

	const float DurationScale = ComputeDurationScale(ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 0.f), EffectDefinition->Duration);
	const float StrengthScale = SpellCast.Get(GBStat::EffectStrength, 1.f);
	Effects->ApplyEffect(EffectId, Caster, DurationScale, StrengthScale);
	EndSpell();
}

	// Visual and geometric limits only.
	constexpr int32 MaxWallSprites = 8;

	constexpr float WallTouchMarginCm = 15.f;

AGBSpell_Wall::AGBSpell_Wall()
{
	Box = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBoxComponent>(TEXT("Box"));
	Box->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Box->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Box->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);

void AGBSpell_Wall::StartSpell()
{
	const float Distance = ReadParam(GBSpellParam::Distance, 300.f);
	const float Width = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Width, 300.f));
	const float Height = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Height, 200.f));
	const float Thickness = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Thickness, 50.f));
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 5.f);
	const bool bBlocking = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::Blocking, 0.f) > 0.5f;

	// Stand on the ground in front of the caster, facing the caster's yaw.
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	const FVector CasterLocation = Caster ? Caster->GetActorLocation() : SpellCast.Origin;
	const FRotator WallRotation(0.f, SpellCast.Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f);
	const FVector Point = CasterLocation + WallRotation.Vector() * Distance;
	FVector Ground;
	if (!FindGround(Point + FVector::UpVector * Height, Ground, Height + WallGroundSearchExtraCm))
	{
		Ground = Point;
		Ground.Z = CasterLocation.Z - (Caster ? Caster->GetSimpleCollisionHalfHeight() : 0.f);
	}
	SetActorLocationAndRotation(Ground + FVector::UpVector * (Height * 0.5f), WallRotation, false, nullptr, ETeleportType::TeleportPhysics);

	const FVector Size(Thickness, Width, Height);
	Box->SetBoxExtent(Size * 0.5f);
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Visual, EGBShape::Cube, Size, GetSpellColor(), true);

	// A row of camera-facing sprites along the wall (pixel-art flames / ice).
	if (!GetSpriteId().IsNone())
	{
		const float SpriteHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, Height));
		const int32 Count = FMath::Clamp(FMath::RoundToInt(Width / SpriteHeight), 1, MaxWallSprites);
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count; ++Index)
		{
			UBillboardComponent* Sprite = NewObject<UBillboardComponent>(this);
			Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);
			Sprite->RegisterComponent();
			AddInstanceComponent(Sprite);
			Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, -Width * 0.5f + (Index + 0.5f) * Width / Count, 0.f));
			SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, Height);
			Sprites.Add(Sprite);
		}
	}

	if (bBlocking)
	{
		Box->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::BlockAllDynamic_ProfileName);
		SetupHealth(ReadParam(GBStat::Health, 100.f));
	}

	const float DamagePerSecond = SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 0.f);
	if (DamagePerSecond > 0.f)
	{
		const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
		DamagePerTick = DamagePerSecond * Interval;
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Wall::DamageTick, Interval, true);
	}

	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::SetupHealth(float Health)
{
	UGBStatsComponent* WallStats = NewObject<UGBStatsComponent>(this, TEXT("WallStats"));
	WallStats->RegisterComponent();
	AddInstanceComponent(WallStats);
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::MaxHealth, FMath::Max(1.f, Health));
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::DamageTaken, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamageTaken, 1.f));
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::HealthRegen, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::HealthRegen, 0.f));
	WallStats->SetBaseStat(GBStat::MaxMana, 0.f);

	UGBAttributeComponent* WallAttributes = NewObject<UGBAttributeComponent>(this, TEXT("WallAttributes"));
	// Neutral: nobody targets the wall on purpose, but shots and enemies that hit it wear it down.
	WallAttributes->Team = EGBTeam::Neutral;
	WallAttributes->RegisterComponent();
	AddInstanceComponent(WallAttributes);
	WallAttributes->InitializeFromStats();
	WallAttributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &AGBSpell_Wall::HandleWallDeath);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::HandleWallDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	// Not inside the damage call that killed it.
	EndSpellAfter(0.f);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::DamageTick()
{
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	const FVector Extent = Box->GetUnscaledBoxExtent() + FVector(WallTouchMarginCm);
	GatherHostilesInBox(GetActorLocation(), GetActorQuat(), Extent, Targets);
	for (AActor* Target : Targets)
	{
		DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Zone::StartSpell()
{
	ZoneRadius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 300.f));
	ZoneHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Height, 200.f));
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 5.f);
	const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
	DamagePerTick = FMath::Max(0.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 0.f)) * Interval;

	SetActorLocationAndRotation(FindAimedGroundPoint(SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Range, 0.f)), FRotator(0.f, SpellCast.Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f));

	Disc->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, ZoneDiscLiftCm + ZoneDiscThicknessCm * 0.5f));
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Disc, EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(ZoneRadius * 2.f, ZoneRadius * 2.f, ZoneDiscThicknessCm), GetSpellColor(), true);

	const float SpriteHeight = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, 60.f);
	Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, SpriteHeight * 0.5f));
	SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, 60.f);

	if (SpellCast.GetString(GBString::OnHitEffect).IsEmpty() && DamagePerTick <= 0.f)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Spell '%s': zone with no OnHitEffect and no DamagePerSecond does nothing."), *SpellCast.SpellId.ToString());
	}

	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Zone::ZoneTick, Interval, true, 0.f);
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Zone::ZoneTick()
{
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	GatherHostilesOnDisc(GetActorLocation(), ZoneRadius, ZoneHeight, Targets);
	for (AActor* Target : Targets)
	{
		if (DamagePerTick > 0.f)
		{
			// DealDamage also applies the on-hit effect.
			DamageTarget(Target, DamagePerTick, GetActorLocation());
		}
		else
		{
			ApplyOnHitEffect(Target);
		}
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Trap::StartSpell()
{
	TriggerRadius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBSpellParam::TriggerRadius, 150.f));
	TriggerHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBStat::Height, 200.f));
	const float ArmTime = ReadParam(GBSpellParam::ArmTime, 0.5f);
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 20.f);

	SetActorLocationAndRotation(FindAimedGroundPoint(ReadParam(GBStat::Range, 1500.f)), FRotator(0.f, SpellCast.Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f));

	Disc->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, TrapDiscLiftCm + TrapDiscThicknessCm * 0.5f));
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Disc, EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(TriggerRadius * 2.f, TriggerRadius * 2.f, TrapDiscThicknessCm),
		GetSpellColor() * TrapUnarmedBrightness, true);

	const float SpriteHeight = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, 50.f);
	Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, SpriteHeight * 0.5f));
	SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, 50.f);

	if (ArmTime > 0.f)
	{
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(ArmTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Trap::Arm, ArmTime, false);
	}
	else
	{
		Arm();
	}
	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Trap::Arm()
{
	bArmed = true;
	GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(Disc, GetSpellColor());
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
}

void AGBSpell_Trap::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	if (!bArmed || bEnded)
	{
		return;
	}
	TArray<AActor*> Targets;
	GatherHostilesOnDisc(GetActorLocation(), TriggerRadius, TriggerHeight, Targets);
	if (Targets.Num() > 0)
	{
		Trigger();
	}
}

void AGBSpell_Trap::Trigger()
{
	bArmed = false;
	SetActorTickEnabled(false);

	const FVector BlastCenter = GetActorLocation() + FVector::UpVector * SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::BlastHeight, 50.f);
	FGBDamageInfo Info = SpellCast.MakeDamageInfo(GBStat::Damage, this);
	Info.HitLocation = BlastCenter;
	const bool bFalloff = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::Falloff, 0.f) > 0.5f;
	TArray<AActor*> Ignore;
	Ignore.Add(this);
	UGBCombatLibrary::DealRadialDamage(this, BlastCenter, FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Radius, 300.f)), Info, Ignore, bFalloff);
	EndSpell();
}

#include "QTE/GBQTEComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Kismet/GameplayStatics.h"
#include "HAL/IConsoleManager.h"
#include "Misc/App.h"

static TAutoConsoleVariable<int32> CVarGBQTE(
	TEXT("gb.QTE"),
	1,
	TEXT("Spell quick-time event. 1 = on (if QTE.json bEnabled), 0 = off: spells cast at their default strength."),
	ECVF_Default);

namespace GBQTEComponentPrivate
{
	// Fallbacks used only when QTE.json lacks the key (a warning is logged). The real values live in QTE.json.
	constexpr float DefaultTimeDilation = 0.1f;
	constexpr float DefaultGestureIdleSubmitTime = 0.25f;
	constexpr float DefaultLineSubmitLengthMult = 2.5f;
	constexpr float DefaultStickGestureScale = 600.f;
	constexpr float FullCircleDegrees = 360.f;
}

UGBQTEComponent::UGBQTEComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	// Ticks only while a QTE runs (enabled in StartQTE, disabled in Finish). Not while the game is paused (default).
	PrimaryComponentTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = false;
	Random.GenerateNewSeed();
}

bool UGBQTEComponent::IsEnabled() const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const bool bDataEnabled = Data ? Data->GetQTE().bEnabled : true;
	return bDataEnabled && CVarGBQTE.GetValueOnGameThread() != 0;
}

float UGBQTEComponent::ReadStat(FName Key, float Default) const
{
	float Base = Default;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const float* Found = Data ? Data->GetQTE().Stats.Find(Key) : nullptr;
	if (Found)
	{
		Base = *Found;
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("QTE: QTE.json has no Stats entry '%s'; using %f."), *Key.ToString(), Default);
	}
	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	return Stats ? Stats->Evaluate(Key, Base) : Base;
}

float UGBQTEComponent::GetStickGestureScale() const
{
	return ReadStat(GBQTEStat::StickGestureScale, GBQTEComponentPrivate::DefaultStickGestureScale);
}

FGBQTEConfig UGBQTEComponent::BuildConfig() const
{
	const FGBQTEConfig Defaults;
	FGBQTEConfig Config;
	Config.SequenceLength = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(ReadStat(GBStat::QTESequenceLength, static_cast<float>(Defaults.SequenceLength))));
	Config.GestureCount = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(ReadStat(GBStat::QTEGestureCount, static_cast<float>(Defaults.GestureCount))));
	Config.TimeLimit = ReadStat(GBStat::QTETimeLimit, Defaults.TimeLimit);
	Config.GestureMinScore = ReadStat(GBQTEStat::GestureMinScore, Defaults.GestureMinScore);
	Config.GestureMinLength = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadStat(GBQTEStat::GestureMinLength, Defaults.GestureMinLength));

	if (const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		const UEnum* GestureEnum = StaticEnum<EGBGesture>();
		const int64 MaxGesture = static_cast<int64>(EGBGesture::CircleCounterClockwise);
		for (const FString& Name : Data->GetQTE().Gestures)
		{
			const int64 Value = GestureEnum->GetValueByNameString(Name);
			if (Value == INDEX_NONE || Value < 0 || Value > MaxGesture)
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("QTE: unknown gesture '%s' in QTE.json; ignored."), *Name);
				continue;
			}
			Config.AllowedGestures.AddUnique(static_cast<EGBGesture>(Value));
		}
	}
	return Config;
}

bool UGBQTEComponent::StartQTE(FName SpellId, FGBOnQTEFinished OnFinished)
{
	if (bActive)
	{
		return false;
	}

	if (!IsEnabled())
	{
		FGBQTEResult Result;
		Result.Power = 0.f;
		Result.Reach = 0.f;
		Result.bUsedQTE = false;
		Result.bSuccess = true;
		LastResult = Result;
		OnFinished.ExecuteIfBound(Result);
		return true;
	}

	const float TimeDilation = ReadStat(GBStat::QTETimeDilation, GBQTEComponentPrivate::DefaultTimeDilation);
	GestureIdleSubmitTime = ReadStat(GBQTEStat::GestureIdleSubmitTime, GBQTEComponentPrivate::DefaultGestureIdleSubmitTime);
	LineSubmitLengthMult = ReadStat(GBQTEStat::LineSubmitLengthMult, GBQTEComponentPrivate::DefaultLineSubmitLengthMult);

	Session = FGBQTESession();
	Session.Start(BuildConfig(), Random);
	ActiveSpellId = SpellId;
	FinishedDelegate = OnFinished;
	GestureIdleTime = 0.f;
	bActive = true;

	if (Session.IsFinished())
	{
		// No steps configured: nothing to play.
		Finish();
		return true;
	}

	UGameplayStatics::SetGlobalTimeDilation(this, TimeDilation);
	SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
	return true;
}

void UGBQTEComponent::CancelQTE()
{
	if (!bActive)
	{
		return;
	}
	Session.Phase = EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
	Finish();
}

void UGBQTEComponent::HandleKey(EGBQTEKey Key)
{
	if (!bActive)
	{
		return;
	}
	Session.PressKey(Key);
	if (Session.IsFinished())
	{
		Finish();
	}
}

void UGBQTEComponent::HandleGestureInput(const FVector2D& Delta)
{
	if (!bActive || Session.Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Gesture || Delta.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		return;
	}
	Session.AddGestureDelta(Delta);
	GestureIdleTime = 0.f;
}

void UGBQTEComponent::TickComponent(float DeltaTime, ELevelTick TickType, FActorComponentTickFunction* ThisTickFunction)
{
	Super::TickComponent(DeltaTime, TickType, ThisTickFunction);

	if (!bActive)
	{
		return;
	}

	// DeltaTime is dilated by the QTE slow-down; the QTE runs on the undilated frame time.
	const float RealDelta = static_cast<float>(FApp::GetDeltaTime());
	Session.Advance(RealDelta);

	if (Session.Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Gesture)
	{
		GestureIdleTime += RealDelta;
		if (Session.IsGestureReadyToJudge() && Session.Gestures.IsValidIndex(Session.GestureScores.Num()))
		{
			const EGBGesture Wanted = Session.Gestures[Session.GestureScores.Num()];
			const bool bCircle = Wanted == EGBGesture::CircleClockwise || Wanted == EGBGesture::CircleCounterClockwise;

			bool bSubmit = GestureIdleTime >= GestureIdleSubmitTime;
			if (bCircle)
			{
				bSubmit |= FMath::Abs(FGBGestureRecognizer::TotalTurnDegrees(Session.CurrentStroke)) >= GBQTEComponentPrivate::FullCircleDegrees;
			}
			else
			{
				bSubmit |= FGBGestureRecognizer::PathLength(Session.CurrentStroke) >= LineSubmitLengthMult * Session.Config.GestureMinLength;
			}

			if (bSubmit)
			{
				Session.SubmitGesture();
				GestureIdleTime = 0.f;
			}
		}
	}

	if (Session.IsFinished())
	{
		Finish();
	}
}

void UGBQTEComponent::Finish()
{
	if (!bActive)
	{
		return;
	}
	UGameplayStatics::SetGlobalTimeDilation(this, 1.f);
	bActive = false;
	SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
	const FGBQTEResult Result = Session.GetResult();
	LastResult = Result;
	ActiveSpellId = NAME_None;

	// Copy first: the callback may start a new QTE, which overwrites FinishedDelegate (and LastResult).
	FGBOnQTEFinished Delegate = FinishedDelegate;
	FinishedDelegate.Unbind();
	Delegate.ExecuteIfBound(Result);
}

void UGBQTEComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (bActive)
	{
		UGameplayStatics::SetGlobalTimeDilation(this, 1.f);
		bActive = false;
		FinishedDelegate.Unbind();
	}
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}
#include "QTE/GBQTELogic.h"

namespace GBQTELogicPrivate
{
	// Segments shorter than this carry no direction (repeated points, sub-pixel jitter).
	constexpr double MinSegmentLength = 1e-3;

	constexpr int32 NumKeys = 4;
	const EGBGesture AllGestures[] = {
		EGBGesture::LineRight, EGBGesture::LineLeft, EGBGesture::LineDown, EGBGesture::LineUp,
		EGBGesture::CircleClockwise, EGBGesture::CircleCounterClockwise };

	bool IsCircle(EGBGesture Gesture)
	{
		return Gesture == EGBGesture::CircleClockwise || Gesture == EGBGesture::CircleCounterClockwise;
	}

	FVector2D LineDirection(EGBGesture Gesture)
	{
		switch (Gesture)
		{
		case EGBGesture::LineRight: return FVector2D(1.0, 0.0);
		case EGBGesture::LineLeft:  return FVector2D(-1.0, 0.0);
		case EGBGesture::LineDown:  return FVector2D(0.0, 1.0);
		case EGBGesture::LineUp:    return FVector2D(0.0, -1.0);
		default:                    return FVector2D::ZeroVector;
		}
	}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// FGBGestureRecognizer

float FGBGestureRecognizer::PathLength(TConstArrayView<FVector2D> Points)
{
	double Length = 0.0;
	for (int32 i = 1; i < Points.Num(); ++i)
	{
		Length += FVector2D::Distance(Points[i - 1], Points[i]);
	}
	return static_cast<float>(Length);
}

float FGBGestureRecognizer::TotalTurnDegrees(TConstArrayView<FVector2D> Points)
{
	double TotalRadians = 0.0;
	bool bHasPrevious = false;
	FVector2D Previous = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
	for (int32 i = 1; i < Points.Num(); ++i)
	{
		const FVector2D Segment = Points[i] - Points[i - 1];
		if (Segment.Size() < GBQTELogicPrivate::MinSegmentLength)
		{
			continue;
		}
		if (bHasPrevious)
		{
			// With +Y down, a positive cross product is a clockwise turn as seen on screen.
			const double Cross = Previous.X * Segment.Y - Previous.Y * Segment.X;
			const double Dot = Previous.X * Segment.X + Previous.Y * Segment.Y;
			TotalRadians += FMath::Atan2(Cross, Dot);
		}
		Previous = Segment;
		bHasPrevious = true;
	}
	return static_cast<float>(FMath::RadiansToDegrees(TotalRadians));
}

float FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(TConstArrayView<FVector2D> Points, EGBGesture Gesture, float MinLength)
{
	if (Points.Num() < 2)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	const float Length = PathLength(Points);
	if (Length < MinLength || Length < GBQTELogicPrivate::MinSegmentLength)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	const FVector2D Start = Points[0];
	const FVector2D End = Points.Last();

	if (!GBQTELogicPrivate::IsCircle(Gesture))
	{
		const FVector2D Chord = End - Start;
		const double ChordLength = Chord.Size();
		if (ChordLength < GBQTELogicPrivate::MinSegmentLength)
		{
			return 0.f;
		}
		const double Straightness = FMath::Clamp(ChordLength / Length, 0.0, 1.0);
		const double DirectionMatch = FMath::Clamp(FVector2D::DotProduct(Chord / ChordLength, GBQTELogicPrivate::LineDirection(Gesture)), 0.0, 1.0);
		return static_cast<float>(Straightness * DirectionMatch);
	}

	const float Sign = Gesture == EGBGesture::CircleClockwise ? 1.f : -1.f;
	const float Turn = TotalTurnDegrees(Points) * Sign;
	const float TurnScore = FMath::Clamp(Turn / 360.f, 0.f, 1.f);
	if (TurnScore <= 0.f)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}

	// Closure: 1 when the stroke ends where it began, 0 when the gap is as large as the stroke's bounding box diagonal.
	FVector2D Min = Start;
	FVector2D Max = Start;
	for (const FVector2D& P : Points)
	{
		Min = FVector2D::Min(Min, P);
		Max = FVector2D::Max(Max, P);
	}
	const double Size = (Max - Min).Size();
	if (Size < GBQTELogicPrivate::MinSegmentLength)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	const double Gap = FVector2D::Distance(Start, End);
	const double Closure = FMath::Clamp(1.0 - Gap / Size, 0.0, 1.0);
	return static_cast<float>(TurnScore * Closure);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// FGBQTESession

void FGBQTESession::Start(const FGBQTEConfig& InConfig, FRandomStream& Random)
{
	Config = InConfig;
	Config.SequenceLength = FMath::Max(0, Config.SequenceLength);
	Config.GestureCount = FMath::Max(0, Config.GestureCount);

	Sequence.Reset();
	SequenceResults.Reset();
	Gestures.Reset();
	GestureScores.Reset();
	CurrentStroke.Reset();
	TimeElapsed = 0.f;
	bTimedOut = false;

	for (int32 i = 0; i < Config.SequenceLength; ++i)
	{
		Sequence.Add(static_cast<EGBQTEKey>(Random.RandRange(0, GBQTELogicPrivate::NumKeys - 1)));
	}

	TArray<EGBGesture> Pool = Config.AllowedGestures;
	if (Pool.Num() == 0)
	{
		Pool.Append(GBQTELogicPrivate::AllGestures, UE_ARRAY_COUNT(GBQTELogicPrivate::AllGestures));
	}
	for (int32 i = 0; i < Config.GestureCount; ++i)
	{
		Gestures.Add(Pool[Random.RandRange(0, Pool.Num() - 1)]);
	}

	if (Sequence.Num() > 0)
	{
		Phase = EGBQTEPhase::Sequence;
	}
	else
	{
		Phase = Gestures.Num() > 0 ? EGBQTEPhase::Gesture : EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
	}
}

bool FGBQTESession::PressKey(EGBQTEKey Key)
{
	if (Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Sequence || !Sequence.IsValidIndex(SequenceResults.Num()))
	{
		return false;
	}
	const bool bRight = Sequence[SequenceResults.Num()] == Key;
	SequenceResults.Add(bRight);
	if (SequenceResults.Num() >= Sequence.Num())
	{
		CurrentStroke.Reset();
		Phase = Gestures.Num() > 0 ? EGBQTEPhase::Gesture : EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
	}
	return bRight;
}

void FGBQTESession::AddGestureDelta(const FVector2D& Delta)
{
	if (Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Gesture || Delta.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (CurrentStroke.Num() == 0)
	{
		CurrentStroke.Add(FVector2D::ZeroVector);
	}
	CurrentStroke.Add(CurrentStroke.Last() + Delta);
}

float FGBQTESession::SubmitGesture()
{
	if (Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Gesture || !Gestures.IsValidIndex(GestureScores.Num()))
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	float GestureScore = FGBGestureRecognizer::Score(CurrentStroke, Gestures[GestureScores.Num()], Config.GestureMinLength);
	if (GestureScore < Config.GestureMinScore)
	{
		GestureScore = 0.f;
	}
	GestureScores.Add(GestureScore);
	CurrentStroke.Reset();
	if (GestureScores.Num() >= Gestures.Num())
	{
		Phase = EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
	}
	return GestureScore;
}

bool FGBQTESession::IsGestureReadyToJudge() const
{
	return Phase == EGBQTEPhase::Gesture && CurrentStroke.Num() >= 2
		&& FGBGestureRecognizer::PathLength(CurrentStroke) >= Config.GestureMinLength;
}

void FGBQTESession::Advance(float RealDeltaSeconds)
{
	if (Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Sequence && Phase != EGBQTEPhase::Gesture)
	{
		return;
	}
	TimeElapsed += FMath::Max(0.f, RealDeltaSeconds);
	if (TimeElapsed >= Config.TimeLimit)
	{
		bTimedOut = true;
		Phase = EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
	}
}

FGBQTEResult FGBQTESession::GetResult() const
{
	FGBQTEResult Result;
	Result.bUsedQTE = true;
	Result.bTimedOut = bTimedOut;

	int32 RightKeys = 0;
	for (bool bRight : SequenceResults)
	{
		RightKeys += bRight ? 1 : 0;
	}

	float ScoreSum = 0.f;
	bool bAnyGesture = false;
	for (float GestureScore : GestureScores)
	{
		ScoreSum += GestureScore;
		bAnyGesture |= GestureScore > 0.f;
	}

	// A part with no steps does not weaken the spell (gesture-only or sequence-only setups).
	Result.Power = Sequence.Num() > 0 ? static_cast<float>(RightKeys) / Sequence.Num() : 1.f;
	Result.Reach = Gestures.Num() > 0 ? ScoreSum / Gestures.Num() : 1.f;

	// GDD 7: the spell fails only if every step is missed (no steps = nothing missed).
	const bool bNoSteps = Sequence.Num() == 0 && Gestures.Num() == 0;
	Result.bSuccess = bNoSteps || RightKeys > 0 || bAnyGesture;
	return Result;
}

#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"

namespace
{
	// Feel and visual constants (not gameplay balance). Mouse2D values come scaled by the engine's
	// default axis sensitivity (0.07 per pixel, BaseInput.ini), hence the conversions below.
	constexpr float MouseDegreesPerUnit = 2.5f;
	constexpr float MouseUnitsPerPixel = 0.07f;
	constexpr float StickYawDegreesPerSecond = 200.f;
	constexpr float StickPitchDegreesPerSecond = 140.f;
	/** Right stick to QTE gesture: screen pixels per second at full tilt. */
	constexpr float StickGesturePixelsPerSecond = 900.f;
	constexpr float MaxLookPitch = 89.f;
	/** Ignore axis noise on wheel / D-pad style 1D actions. */
	constexpr float AxisStepThreshold = 0.1f;
	/** Interactables must be roughly in front of the view. */
	constexpr float InteractFacingMinDot = 0.3f;
	/** Snappy shooter movement: acceleration and braking as multiples of the walk speed (per second). */
	constexpr float AccelerationPerSpeed = 10.f;
	constexpr float BrakingPerSpeed = 8.f;
	constexpr float GroundFrictionValue = 8.f;
	/** Held item anchor and muzzle offsets from the camera (forward, right, up), cm. */
	const FVector HeldItemOffset(30.f, 15.f, -15.f);
	const FVector MuzzleOffset(40.f, 15.f, -12.f);

	TSet<FName> GWarnedMissingPlayerStats;
}

AGBPlayerCharacter::AGBPlayerCharacter()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	bUseControllerRotationYaw = true;
	bUseControllerRotationPitch = false;
	bUseControllerRotationRoll = false;

	Camera = CreateDefaultSubobject<UCameraComponent>(TEXT("Camera"));
	Camera->SetupAttachment(GetCapsuleComponent());
	Camera->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, BaseEyeHeight)); // replaced by the EyeHeight stat on BeginPlay
	Camera->bUsePawnControlRotation = true;

	HeldItemAnchor = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("HeldItemAnchor"));
	HeldItemAnchor->SetupAttachment(Camera);
	HeldItemAnchor->SetRelativeLocation(HeldItemOffset);

	Stats = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBStatsComponent>(TEXT("Stats"));
	Attributes = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBAttributeComponent>(TEXT("Attributes"));
	StatusEffects = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBStatusEffectComponent>(TEXT("StatusEffects"));
	Equipment = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBEquipmentComponent>(TEXT("Equipment"));
	Spellbook = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBSpellbookComponent>(TEXT("Spellbook"));
	QTE = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBQTEComponent>(TEXT("QTE"));
	Inventory = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBInventoryComponent>(TEXT("Inventory"));
	SkillTree = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBSkillTreeComponent>(TEXT("SkillTree"));

	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		MoveComp->bOrientRotationToMovement = false;
		MoveComp->GroundFriction = GroundFrictionValue;
	}
}

AGBPlayerCharacter* AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	UWorld* World = GEngine ? GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull) : nullptr;
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	APlayerController* PC = World->GetFirstPlayerController();
	return PC ? Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(PC->GetPawn()) : nullptr;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Lifecycle

void AGBPlayerCharacter::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: no data subsystem, the player starts with default stats."));
	}

	if (Data)
	{
		Stats->SetBaseStats(Data->GetPlayer().Stats);
	}
	Attributes->Team = EGBTeam::Player;
	Attributes->InitializeFromStats();

	if (Data)
	{
		const TArray<FName>& StartingWeapons = Data->GetPlayer().StartingWeapons;
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < StartingWeapons.Num(); ++Index)
		{
			if (!Equipment->GiveWeapon(StartingWeapons[Index], Index == 0, false))
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: starting weapon '%s' is not in Weapons.json."), *StartingWeapons[Index].ToString());
			}
		}
	}

	Spellbook->InitializeFromData();
	Inventory->InitializeFromData();
	SkillTree->InitializeFromData();

	Stats->OnStatsChanged.AddUObject(this, &AGBPlayerCharacter::ApplyMovementStats);
	Attributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &AGBPlayerCharacter::HandleDeath);
	if (Data)
	{
		DataReloadedHandle = Data->OnDataReloaded.AddUObject(this, &AGBPlayerCharacter::HandleDataReloaded);
	}

	// Passives learned above changed stats before the binding existed.
	ApplyMovementStats();

	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		if (!Progress->HasCheckpoint())
		{
			Progress->SaveCheckpoint(this);
		}
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.Remove(DataReloadedHandle);
	}
	DataReloadedHandle.Reset();
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::HandleDataReloaded()
{
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Stats->SetBaseStats(Data->GetPlayer().Stats);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	UpdateFocusedInteractable();

	if (IsQTEActive())
	{
		// GDD 7: the movement keys are busy with the QTE, so the last movement input is held.
		if (CanUseGameplayInput() && !bDashing && !LastMoveInput.IsNearlyZero())
		{
			ApplyMoveInput(LastMoveInput);
		}
	}
	else if (GFrameCounter > LastMoveInputFrame + 1)
	{
		// No Move event for a frame: the stick / keys were released (also covers releases during a QTE).
		LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
	}

	if (bDashing)
	{
		DashTimeLeft -= DeltaSeconds;
		if (DashTimeLeft <= 0.f)
		{
			EndDash();
		}
		else if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
		{
			MoveComp->Velocity = DashVelocity;
		}
	}

	TickRecoil(DeltaSeconds);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Stats and movement

float AGBPlayerCharacter::ReadStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	if (!Stats)
	{
		return DefaultValue;
	}
	if (!Stats->HasBaseStat(Stat) && !GWarnedMissingPlayerStats.Contains(Stat))
	{
		GWarnedMissingPlayerStats.Add(Stat);
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: stat '%s' is missing from Player.json, using %.2f."), *Stat.ToString(), DefaultValue);
	}
	return Stats->GetStat(Stat, DefaultValue);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::ApplyMovementStats()
{
	UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement();
	if (!MoveComp)
	{
		return;
	}

	UpdateWalkSpeed();
	MoveComp->JumpZVelocity = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadStat(GBStat::JumpVelocity, 620.f));
	MoveComp->AirControl = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadStat(GBStat::AirControl, 0.6f));
	MoveComp->GravityScale = ReadStat(GBStat::GravityScale, 1.6f);
	JumpMaxCount = FMath::Max(1, FMath::RoundToInt(ReadStat(GBStat::MaxJumps, 1.f)));

	UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
	const float Radius = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadStat(GBStat::CapsuleRadius, 38.f));
	const float HalfHeight = FMath::Max(Radius, ReadStat(GBStat::CapsuleHalfHeight, 90.f));
	const float OldHalfHeight = Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleHalfHeight();
	if (!FMath::IsNearlyEqual(OldHalfHeight, HalfHeight) || !FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleRadius(), Radius))
	{
		const bool bWasOnGround = MoveComp->IsMovingOnGround();
		Capsule->SetCapsuleSize(Radius, HalfHeight, true);
		if (bWasOnGround && HalfHeight > OldHalfHeight)
		{
			// Keep the feet on the floor instead of sinking into it.
			AddActorWorldOffset(FVector(0.f, 0.f, HalfHeight - OldHalfHeight));
		}
	}

	BaseEyeHeight = ReadStat(GBStat::EyeHeight, 70.f);
	Camera->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, BaseEyeHeight));
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::UpdateWalkSpeed()
{
	UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement();
	if (!MoveComp)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float Speed = FMath::Max(0.f, ReadStat(GBStat::MoveSpeed, 750.f) * MoveSpeedScale);
	MoveComp->MaxWalkSpeed = Speed;
	MoveComp->MaxAcceleration = FMath::Max(1.f, Speed * AccelerationPerSpeed);
	MoveComp->BrakingDecelerationWalking = Speed * BrakingPerSpeed;
	MoveComp->GroundFriction = GroundFrictionValue;
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::SetMoveSpeedScale(float Scale)
{
	MoveSpeedScale = FMath::Max(0.f, Scale);
	UpdateWalkSpeed();
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::ApplyMoveInput(const FVector2D& Input)
{
	const FRotator YawRotation(0.f, GetControlRotation().Yaw, 0.f);
	const FRotationMatrix YawMatrix(YawRotation);
	AddMovementInput(YawMatrix.GetUnitAxis(EAxis::X), Input.Y);
	AddMovementInput(YawMatrix.GetUnitAxis(EAxis::Y), Input.X);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::AddLookDelta(float YawDegrees, float PitchDegrees)
{
	if (!Controller)
	{
		return;
	}
	FRotator Rotation = Controller->GetControlRotation();
	Rotation.Yaw = FRotator::NormalizeAxis(Rotation.Yaw + YawDegrees);
	Rotation.Pitch = FMath::Clamp(FRotator::NormalizeAxis(Rotation.Pitch + PitchDegrees), -static_cast<double>(MaxLookPitch), static_cast<double>(MaxLookPitch));
	Rotation.Roll = 0.f;
	Controller->SetControlRotation(Rotation);
}

float AGBPlayerCharacter::GetDashCooldownRemaining() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? FMath::Max(0.f, static_cast<float>(DashReadyTime - World->GetTimeSeconds())) : 0.f;
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::EndDash()
{
	if (!bDashing)
	{
		return;
	}
	bDashing = false;
	DashTimeLeft = 0.f;

	UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement();
	if (MoveComp && MoveComp->MovementMode == MOVE_Flying)
	{
		// Changing movement mode may reset the jump count: keep it, so a dash never refills jumps.
		const int32 SavedJumpCount = JumpCurrentCount;
		MoveComp->SetMovementMode(MOVE_Falling);
		JumpCurrentCount = SavedJumpCount;
		MoveComp->Velocity = DashVelocity.GetSafeNormal() * MoveComp->MaxWalkSpeed;
	}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Aiming

FVector AGBPlayerCharacter::GetAimOrigin() const
{
	return Camera ? Camera->GetComponentLocation() : GetActorLocation();
}

FVector AGBPlayerCharacter::GetAimDirection() const
{
	// Control rotation is current this frame; the camera component only catches up when the view is computed.
	if (Controller)
	{
		return Controller->GetControlRotation().Vector();
	}
	return Camera ? Camera->GetForwardVector() : GetActorForwardVector();
}

FVector AGBPlayerCharacter::GetMuzzleLocation() const
{
	const FRotator AimRotation = GetAimDirection().Rotation();
	return GetAimOrigin() + AimRotation.RotateVector(MuzzleOffset);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::AddRecoil(float Pitch, float Yaw, float RecoverPerSecond)
{
	AddLookDelta(Yaw, Pitch);
	PendingRecoil.Pitch += Pitch;
	PendingRecoil.Yaw += Yaw;
	RecoilRecoverSpeed = FMath::Max(0.f, RecoverPerSecond);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::TickRecoil(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (IsQTEActive() || RecoilRecoverSpeed <= 0.f)
	{
		return; // the camera is locked during the QTE
	}
	const FVector2D Remaining(PendingRecoil.Pitch, PendingRecoil.Yaw);
	const float Length = static_cast<float>(Remaining.Size());
	if (Length <= KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		PendingRecoil = FRotator::ZeroRotator;
		return;
	}
	const float Step = FMath::Min(Length, RecoilRecoverSpeed * DeltaSeconds);
	const float BackPitch = static_cast<float>(Remaining.X) / Length * Step;
	const float BackYaw = static_cast<float>(Remaining.Y) / Length * Step;
	PendingRecoil.Pitch -= BackPitch;
	PendingRecoil.Yaw -= BackYaw;
	AddLookDelta(-BackYaw, -BackPitch);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Interaction

IGBInteractable* AGBPlayerCharacter::GetFocusedInteractable() const
{
	return Cast<IGBInteractable>(FocusedInteractable.Get());
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::UpdateFocusedInteractable()
{
	FocusedInteractable = nullptr;

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World || !CanUseGameplayInput())
	{
		return;
	}
	const float Range = ReadStat(GBStat::InteractRange, 250.f);
	if (Range <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector Origin = GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector ViewDir = GetAimDirection();

	FCollisionObjectQueryParams ObjectParams;
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_WorldStatic);
	ObjectParams.AddObjectTypesToQuery(ECC_Pawn);
	FCollisionQueryParams QueryParams(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBInteract), false, this);

	TArray<FOverlapResult> Overlaps;
	World->OverlapMultiByObjectType(Overlaps, Origin, FQuat::Identity, ObjectParams, FCollisionShape::MakeSphere(Range), QueryParams);

	AActor* Best = nullptr;
	float BestDot = InteractFacingMinDot;
	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Actor = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (!Actor || Actor == this || Actor == Best)
		{
			continue;
		}
		IGBInteractable* Interactable = Cast<IGBInteractable>(Actor);
		if (!Interactable || !Interactable->CanInteract(this))
		{
			continue;
		}
		const float Dot = FVector::DotProduct((Actor->GetActorLocation() - Origin).GetSafeNormal(), ViewDir);
		if (Dot > BestDot)
		{
			BestDot = Dot;
			Best = Actor;
		}
	}
	FocusedInteractable = Best;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State

bool AGBPlayerCharacter::IsDead() const
{
	return Attributes && Attributes->IsDead();
}

bool AGBPlayerCharacter::CanUseGameplayInput() const
{
	return bControlEnabled && !IsDead();
}

bool AGBPlayerCharacter::IsQTEActive() const
{
	return QTE && QTE->IsActive();
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::RestoreAll()
{
	Attributes->RestoreAll();
	Equipment->RefillAllAmmo();
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::ReleaseHeldInputs()
{
	if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment ? Equipment->GetActiveItem() : nullptr)
	{
		Item->StopPrimary();
		Item->StopSecondary();
	}
	bSecondaryHeld = false;
	LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
	StopJumping();
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::SetControlEnabled(bool bEnabled)
{
	bControlEnabled = bEnabled;
	if (!bEnabled)
	{
		ReleaseHeldInputs();
		EndDash();
	}
	if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment ? Equipment->GetActiveItem() : nullptr)
	{
		Item->SetActorHiddenInGame(!bEnabled);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	SetControlEnabled(false);
	Spellbook->CancelSustained();
	QTE->CancelQTE();
	if (AGBGameMode* GameMode = AGBGameMode::Get(this))
	{
		GameMode->HandlePlayerDeath(this);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: died without an AGBGameMode, nothing will respawn the player."));
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.bValid = true;
	Snapshot.Transform = GetActorTransform();
	Snapshot.ControlRotation = Controller ? Controller->GetControlRotation() : GetActorRotation();
	if (const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Snapshot.Era = Era->GetCurrentEra();
	}
	Snapshot.Health = Attributes->GetHealth();
	Snapshot.Mana = Attributes->GetMana();

	Equipment->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Spellbook->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Inventory->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
	SkillTree->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{
	if (QTE->IsActive())
	{
		QTE->CancelQTE();
	}
	EndDash();

	SetActorTransform(Snapshot.Transform, false, nullptr, ETeleportType::TeleportPhysics);
	if (Controller)
	{
		Controller->SetControlRotation(Snapshot.ControlRotation);
	}
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->SetEraImmediate(Snapshot.Era);
	}

	// Components first: armor and skill nodes change MaxHealth / MaxMana, which clamp the values set below.
	Equipment->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Spellbook->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Inventory->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);
	SkillTree->LoadFromSnapshot(Snapshot);

	Attributes->RestoreAll(); // revives
	const float Health = Snapshot.Health > 0.f ? Snapshot.Health : Attributes->GetMaxHealth();
	Attributes->SetHealthAndMana(Health, Snapshot.Mana);

	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		MoveComp->StopMovementImmediately();
	}
	PendingRecoil = FRotator::ZeroRotator;
	LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
	SetControlEnabled(true);
	ApplyMovementStats();

	if (AGBPlayerController* PC = Cast<AGBPlayerController>(Controller))
	{
		if (PC->GetOpenMenu() == EGBMenu::Death)
		{
			PC->SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
		}
	}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Input

void AGBPlayerCharacter::SetupPlayerInputComponent(UInputComponent* PlayerInputComponent)
{
	Super::SetupPlayerInputComponent(PlayerInputComponent);

	if (!InputSet)
	{
		InputSet = NewObject<UGBInputSet>(this);
		InputSet->Build();
	}

	UEnhancedInputComponent* EIC = Cast<UEnhancedInputComponent>(PlayerInputComponent);
	if (!EIC)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: the input component is not an UEnhancedInputComponent (check DefaultInput.ini)."));
		return;
	}

	const UGBInputSet* S = InputSet;

	EIC->BindAction(S->Move, ETriggerEvent::Triggered, this, &ThisClass::InputMove);
	EIC->BindAction(S->Move, ETriggerEvent::Completed, this, &ThisClass::InputMoveCompleted);
	EIC->BindAction(S->Look, ETriggerEvent::Triggered, this, &ThisClass::InputLook);
	EIC->BindAction(S->LookStick, ETriggerEvent::Triggered, this, &ThisClass::InputLookStick);

	EIC->BindAction(S->Jump, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputJump);
	EIC->BindAction(S->Jump, ETriggerEvent::Completed, this, &ThisClass::InputJumpReleased);
	EIC->BindAction(S->Dash, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputDash);

	EIC->BindAction(S->Primary, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputPrimaryPressed);
	EIC->BindAction(S->Primary, ETriggerEvent::Completed, this, &ThisClass::InputPrimaryReleased);
	EIC->BindAction(S->Secondary, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSecondaryPressed);
	EIC->BindAction(S->Secondary, ETriggerEvent::Completed, this, &ThisClass::InputSecondaryReleased);

	EIC->BindAction(S->SpellSlot1, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSpellSlot, 0);
	EIC->BindAction(S->SpellSlot2, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSpellSlot, 1);
	EIC->BindAction(S->SpellSlot3, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSpellSlot, 2);
	EIC->BindAction(S->SpellSlot4, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSpellSlot, 3);
	EIC->BindAction(S->SpellSlot5, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSpellSlot, 4);
	EIC->BindAction(S->Enchant, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputEnchant);

	EIC->BindAction(S->Reload, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputReload);
	EIC->BindAction(S->Interact, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputInteract);
	EIC->BindAction(S->QuickUse, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputQuickUse);
	EIC->BindAction(S->CycleQuickUse, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputCycleQuickUse);
	EIC->BindAction(S->SwitchWeapon, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSwitchWeapon);

	EIC->BindAction(S->QTEUp, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputQTEKey, static_cast<int32>(EGBQTEKey::Up));
	EIC->BindAction(S->QTELeft, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputQTEKey, static_cast<int32>(EGBQTEKey::Left));
	EIC->BindAction(S->QTEDown, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputQTEKey, static_cast<int32>(EGBQTEKey::Down));
	EIC->BindAction(S->QTERight, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputQTEKey, static_cast<int32>(EGBQTEKey::Right));

	EIC->BindAction(S->FaceUp, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputFace, EGBQTEKey::Up);
	EIC->BindAction(S->FaceLeft, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputFace, EGBQTEKey::Left);
	EIC->BindAction(S->FaceDown, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputFace, EGBQTEKey::Down);
	EIC->BindAction(S->FaceRight, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputFace, EGBQTEKey::Right);
	EIC->BindAction(S->FaceDown, ETriggerEvent::Completed, this, &ThisClass::InputJumpReleased);

	if (AGBPlayerController* PC = Cast<AGBPlayerController>(Controller))
	{
		PC->SetupInput(InputSet);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: not possessed by an AGBPlayerController, menus and mapping contexts are not set up."));
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputMove(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (IsQTEActive())
	{
		return; // W A S D belong to the QTE; LastMoveInput stays as it was
	}
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput())
	{
		LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
		return;
	}
	LastMoveInput = Value.Get<FVector2D>();
	LastMoveInputFrame = GFrameCounter;
	if (!bDashing)
	{
		ApplyMoveInput(LastMoveInput);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputMoveCompleted(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (!IsQTEActive())
	{
		LastMoveInput = FVector2D::ZeroVector;
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputLook(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput())
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector2D Delta = Value.Get<FVector2D>(); // mouse: x right, y up
	if (IsQTEActive())
	{
		// Camera locked; the mouse draws the gesture in screen space (y down).
		QTE->HandleGestureInput(FVector2D(Delta.X, -Delta.Y) / MouseUnitsPerPixel);
		return;
	}
	AddLookDelta(static_cast<float>(Delta.X) * MouseDegreesPerUnit, static_cast<float>(Delta.Y) * MouseDegreesPerUnit);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputLookStick(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput())
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector2D Stick = Value.Get<FVector2D>(); // x right, y up
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	// Real time: the QTE runs in real time while the world is slowed.
	const float RealDelta = World ? World->DeltaRealTimeSeconds : 0.f;
	if (IsQTEActive())
	{
		QTE->HandleGestureInput(FVector2D(Stick.X, -Stick.Y) * StickGesturePixelsPerSecond * RealDelta);
		return;
	}
	AddLookDelta(static_cast<float>(Stick.X) * StickYawDegreesPerSecond * RealDelta, static_cast<float>(Stick.Y) * StickPitchDegreesPerSecond * RealDelta);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputFace(EGBQTEKey Key)
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (IsQTEActive())
	{
		QTE->HandleKey(Key);
		return;
	}
	// GDD 9: hold L2 + Cross, Circle, Square, Triangle = spell slots 2-5 with the staff.
	if (bSecondaryHeld && Equipment->IsHoldingStaff())
	{
		int32 Slot = 1;
		switch (Key)
		{
		case EGBQTEKey::Down:  Slot = 1; break;
		case EGBQTEKey::Right: Slot = 2; break;
		case EGBQTEKey::Left:  Slot = 3; break;
		case EGBQTEKey::Up:    Slot = 4; break;
		}
		Spellbook->TryCastSlot(Slot);
		return;
	}
	switch (Key)
	{
	case EGBQTEKey::Down:  InputJump(); break;
	case EGBQTEKey::Right: InputDash(); break;
	case EGBQTEKey::Left:  InputReload(); break;
	case EGBQTEKey::Up:    InputInteract(); break;
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputJump()
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive() || bDashing)
	{
		return;
	}
	Jump();
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputJumpReleased()
{
	StopJumping();
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputDash()
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive() || bDashing || GetDashCooldownRemaining() > 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}
	UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = GetCharacterMovement();
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!MoveComp || !World)
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Distance = ReadStat(GBStat::DashDistance, 700.f);
	const float Duration = ReadStat(GBStat::DashDuration, 0.18f);
	const float Cooldown = ReadStat(GBStat::DashCooldown, 1.2f);
	if (Distance <= 0.f || Duration <= KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FRotationMatrix YawMatrix(FRotator(0.f, GetControlRotation().Yaw, 0.f));
	const FVector Forward = YawMatrix.GetUnitAxis(EAxis::X);
	FVector Direction = Forward * LastMoveInput.Y + YawMatrix.GetUnitAxis(EAxis::Y) * LastMoveInput.X;
	Direction.Z = 0.f;
	if (!Direction.Normalize())
	{
		Direction = Forward;
	}

	bDashing = true;
	DashTimeLeft = Duration;
	DashVelocity = Direction * (Distance / Duration);
	DashReadyTime = World->GetTimeSeconds() + FMath::Max(0.f, Cooldown);

	// Flying = no gravity and no ground friction while the velocity is forced each tick.
	const int32 SavedJumpCount = JumpCurrentCount;
	MoveComp->SetMovementMode(MOVE_Flying);
	JumpCurrentCount = SavedJumpCount;
	MoveComp->Velocity = DashVelocity;
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputPrimaryPressed()
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment->GetActiveItem())
	{
		Item->StartPrimary();
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputPrimaryReleased()
{
	if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment->GetActiveItem())
	{
		Item->StopPrimary();
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputSecondaryPressed()
{
	bSecondaryHeld = true;
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive())
	{
		return;
	}
	// With the staff, right click is spell slot 1 (its own action) and L2 is the spell modifier: nothing here.
	if (Equipment->IsHoldingGun())
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment->GetActiveItem())
		{
			Item->StartSecondary();
		}
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputSecondaryReleased()
{
	bSecondaryHeld = false;
	if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment->GetActiveItem())
	{
		Item->StopSecondary();
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputReload()
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive())
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment->GetActiveItem())
	{
		Item->Reload();
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputSwitchWeapon(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive())
	{
		return;
	}
	const float Axis = Value.Get<float>();
	if (FMath::Abs(Axis) < AxisStepThreshold)
	{
		return;
	}
	Equipment->CycleSlot(Axis > 0.f ? 1 : -1);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputSpellSlot(int32 Slot)
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive() || !Equipment->IsHoldingStaff())
	{
		return;
	}
	Spellbook->TryCastSlot(Slot);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputEnchant()
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive() || !Equipment->IsHoldingGun())
	{
		return;
	}
	Spellbook->TryCastEnchant();
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputInteract()
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive())
	{
		return;
	}
	IGBInteractable* Interactable = GetFocusedInteractable();
	if (Interactable && Interactable->CanInteract(this))
	{
		Interactable->Interact(this);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputQuickUse()
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive())
	{
		return;
	}
	Inventory->UseQuickItem();
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputCycleQuickUse(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || IsQTEActive())
	{
		return;
	}
	const float Axis = Value.Get<float>();
	if (FMath::Abs(Axis) < AxisStepThreshold)
	{
		return;
	}
	Inventory->CycleQuickUse(Axis > 0.f ? 1 : -1);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputQTEKey(int32 KeyIndex)
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput() || !IsQTEActive())
	{
		return;
	}
	QTE->HandleKey(static_cast<EGBQTEKey>(FMath::Clamp(KeyIndex, 0, 3)));
}

#include "World/GBEraSubsystem.h"

#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Engine/LevelStreaming.h"
#include "GameFramework/Controller.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
