namespace GBEraSubsystemLocal
{
	// A hitch (loading, breakpoint) must not skip the whole fade in one frame.
	constexpr float MaxFadeStep = 0.1f;
}

UGBEraSubsystem* UGBEraSubsystem::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject || !GEngine)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	UWorld* World = GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull);
	return World ? World->GetSubsystem<UGBEraSubsystem>() : nullptr;
}

bool UGBEraSubsystem::DoesSupportWorldType(const EWorldType::Type WorldType) const
{
	return WorldType == EWorldType::Game || WorldType == EWorldType::PIE;
}

TStatId UGBEraSubsystem::GetStatId() const
{
	RETURN_QUICK_DECLARE_CYCLE_STAT(UGBEraSubsystem, STATGROUP_Tickables);
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::OnWorldBeginPlay(UWorld& InWorld)
{
	Super::OnWorldBeginPlay(InWorld);
	ApplyEra();
}

bool UGBEraSubsystem::IsPresentIn(EGBEraPresence Presence, EGBEra Era)
{
	switch (Presence)
	{
	case EGBEraPresence::PastOnly:   return Era == EGBEra::Past;
	case EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly: return Era == EGBEra::Future;
	default:                         return true;
	}
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::SetEraImmediate(EGBEra NewEra)
{
	CurrentEra = NewEra;
	ApplyEra();
}

bool UGBEraSubsystem::RequestTravel(EGBEra TargetEra, AActor* Traveller, bool bTeleport, const FTransform& Destination)
{
	if (bTravelling)
	{
		return false;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Era travel: no data subsystem, using default fade times."));
	}
	FadeOutTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::EraFadeOutTime, 0.35f) : 0.35f;
	HoldTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::EraHoldTime, 0.25f) : 0.25f;
	FadeInTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::EraFadeInTime, 0.45f) : 0.45f;

	bTravelling = true;
	TravelStage = 0;
	StageTime = 0.f;
	FadeAlpha = 0.f;
	PendingEra = TargetEra;
	PendingTraveller = Traveller;
	bPendingTeleport = bTeleport;
	PendingDestination = Destination;

	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Traveller))
	{
		Player->SetControlEnabled(false);
	}
	return true;
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::Tick(float DeltaTime)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaTime);

	if (!bTravelling)
	{
		return;
	}

	// The fade runs in real time: the spell QTE slows the world, the fade must not slow with it.
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const float RealDelta = FMath::Clamp(World ? World->DeltaRealTimeSeconds : DeltaTime, 0.f, GBEraSubsystemLocal::MaxFadeStep);
	StageTime += RealDelta;

	if (TravelStage == 0)
	{
		FadeAlpha = FadeOutTime > 0.f ? FMath::Clamp(StageTime / FadeOutTime, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
		if (StageTime >= FadeOutTime)
		{
			FadeAlpha = 1.f;
			PerformSwap();
			TravelStage = 1;
			StageTime = 0.f;
		}
	}
	else if (TravelStage == 1)
	{
		FadeAlpha = 1.f;
		if (StageTime >= HoldTime)
		{
			TravelStage = 2;
			StageTime = 0.f;
		}
	}
	else
	{
		FadeAlpha = FadeInTime > 0.f ? 1.f - FMath::Clamp(StageTime / FadeInTime, 0.f, 1.f) : 0.f;
		if (StageTime >= FadeInTime)
		{
			FinishTravel();
		}
	}
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::PerformSwap()
{
	CurrentEra = PendingEra;
	ApplyEra();

	AActor* Traveller = PendingTraveller.Get();
	if (!Traveller)
	{
		return;
	}

	if (bPendingTeleport)
	{
		const FVector Location = PendingDestination.GetLocation();
		const FRotator Rotation = PendingDestination.Rotator();
		if (!Traveller->TeleportTo(Location, Rotation))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Era travel: teleport of %s to %s failed."), *Traveller->GetName(), *Location.ToString());
		}
		if (UMovementComponent* Movement = Traveller->FindComponentByClass<UMovementComponent>())
		{
			Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
		}
		if (APawn* Pawn = Cast<APawn>(Traveller))
		{
			if (AController* Controller = Pawn->GetController())
			{
				FRotator Control = Controller->GetControlRotation();
				Control.Yaw = Rotation.Yaw;
				Control.Roll = 0.f;
				Controller->SetControlRotation(Control);
			}
		}
	}

	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(Traveller))
	{
		// GDD 4: era travel restores health, mana and ammo; portals are the checkpoints of the map.
		Player->RestoreAll();
		if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
		{
			Progress->SaveCheckpoint(Player);
		}
	}
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::FinishTravel()
{
	FadeAlpha = 0.f;
	bTravelling = false;
	TravelStage = 0;
	StageTime = 0.f;

	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(PendingTraveller.Get()))
	{
		Player->SetControlEnabled(true);
	}
	PendingTraveller.Reset();

	OnTravelFinished.Broadcast(CurrentEra);
}

void UGBEraSubsystem::ApplyEra()
{
	if (UWorld* World = GetWorld())
	{
		for (ULevelStreaming* Level : World->GetStreamingLevels())
		{
			if (!Level)
			{
				continue;
			}
			const FString PackageName = Level->GetWorldAssetPackageName();
			const bool bPast = PackageName.EndsWith(TEXT("_Past"));
			const bool bFuture = PackageName.EndsWith(TEXT("_Future"));
			if (!bPast && !bFuture)
			{
				continue;
			}
			// Both versions stay loaded so the swap behind the fade is only a visibility change.
			Level->SetShouldBeLoaded(true);
			Level->SetShouldBeVisible(bPast ? CurrentEra == EGBEra::Past : CurrentEra == EGBEra::Future);
		}
	}

	OnEraChanged.Broadcast(CurrentEra);
}
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/GameInstance.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"

UGBProgressSubsystem* UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (const UGameInstance* DirectInstance = Cast<UGameInstance>(WorldContextObject))
	{
		return DirectInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBProgressSubsystem>();
	}
	const UWorld* World = GEngine ? GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull) : nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = World ? World->GetGameInstance() : nullptr;
	return GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBProgressSubsystem>() : nullptr;
}

bool UGBProgressSubsystem::HasFlag(FName Flag) const
{
	return Flags.Contains(Flag);
}

void UGBProgressSubsystem::SetFlag(FName Flag, bool bValue)
{
	if (Flag.IsNone())
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Progress: SetFlag called with an empty flag name."));
		return;
	}

	const bool bChanged = bValue ? !Flags.Contains(Flag) : Flags.Contains(Flag);
	if (!bChanged)
	{
		return;
	}

	if (bValue)
	{
		Flags.Add(Flag);
	}
	else
	{
		Flags.Remove(Flag);
	}
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Log, TEXT("Progress: flag %s = %s"), *Flag.ToString(), bValue ? TEXT("true") : TEXT("false"));
	OnFlagChanged.Broadcast(Flag);
}

void UGBProgressSubsystem::SaveCheckpoint(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!Player)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Progress: SaveCheckpoint without a player."));
		return;
	}

	FGBPlayerSnapshot Snapshot;
	Player->SaveToSnapshot(Snapshot);
	Snapshot.bValid = true;
	Checkpoint = MoveTemp(Snapshot);
	OnCheckpointSaved.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBProgressSubsystem::RestoreCheckpoint(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!Player || !Checkpoint.bValid)
	{
		return false;
	}
	Player->LoadFromSnapshot(Checkpoint);
	return true;
}

void UGBProgressSubsystem::ResetProgress()
{
	const TSet<FName> Cleared = MoveTemp(Flags);
	Flags.Reset();
	Checkpoint = FGBPlayerSnapshot();
	for (const FName& Flag : Cleared)
	{
		OnFlagChanged.Broadcast(Flag);
	}
}

#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"

namespace GBDataPrivate
{
	void ReportError(const FString& Message)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("%s"), *Message);
#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING
		if (GEngine)
		{
			GEngine->AddOnScreenDebugMessage(-1, 15.f, FColor::Red, Message);
		}
#endif
	}

	/** Reads and parses one file of the data folder. False (and an error) if it is missing or not valid JSON. */
	bool ReadJsonFile(const FString& FileName, TSharedPtr<FJsonObject>& OutRoot)
	{
		const FString Path = UGBDataSubsystem::GetDataDir() / FileName;
		FString Text;
		if (!FFileHelper::LoadFileToString(Text, *Path))
		{
			ReportError(FString::Printf(TEXT("Data: cannot read %s"), *Path));
			return false;
		}

		const TSharedRef<TJsonReader<>> Reader = TJsonReaderFactory<>::Create(Text);
		if (!FJsonSerializer::Deserialize(Reader, OutRoot) || !OutRoot.IsValid())
		{
			ReportError(FString::Printf(TEXT("Data: %s is not valid JSON: %s"), *FileName, *Reader->GetErrorMessage()));
			OutRoot.Reset();
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	/** Loads the array field Field of FileName into Out. Out is left empty on any error. */
	template <typename T>
	void LoadArray(const FString& FileName, const TCHAR* Field, TArray<T>& Out)
	{
		Out.Reset();

		TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Root;
		if (!ReadJsonFile(FileName, Root))
		{
			return;
		}

		const TArray<TSharedPtr<FJsonValue>>* JsonArray = nullptr;
		if (!Root->TryGetArrayField(Field, JsonArray) || !JsonArray)
		{
			ReportError(FString::Printf(TEXT("Data: %s has no array \"%s\""), *FileName, Field));
			return;
		}

		FText FailReason;
		if (!FJsonObjectConverter::JsonArrayToUStruct(*JsonArray, &Out, 0, 0, false, &FailReason))
		{
			ReportError(FString::Printf(TEXT("Data: %s \"%s\" could not be read: %s"), *FileName, Field, *FailReason.ToString()));
			Out.Reset();
		}
	}

	/** Loads the whole of FileName into Out. Out is reset to defaults on any error. */
	template <typename T>
	void LoadStruct(const FString& FileName, T& Out)
	{
		Out = T();

		TSharedPtr<FJsonObject> Root;
		if (!ReadJsonFile(FileName, Root))
		{
			return;
		}

		FText FailReason;
		if (!FJsonObjectConverter::JsonObjectToUStruct(Root.ToSharedRef(), &Out, 0, 0, false, &FailReason))
		{
			ReportError(FString::Printf(TEXT("Data: %s could not be read: %s"), *FileName, *FailReason.ToString()));
			Out = T();
		}
	}

	/** Id -> index. Warns about empty and duplicate ids; the first entry with an id wins. */
	template <typename T>
	void BuildIndex(const TArray<T>& Rows, TMap<FName, int32>& OutIndex, const TCHAR* FileName)
	{
		OutIndex.Reset();
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Rows.Num(); ++i)
		{
			const FName Id = Rows[i].Id;
			if (Id.IsNone())
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Data: %s entry %d has an empty Id; it cannot be found by id."), FileName, i);
				continue;
			}
			if (OutIndex.Contains(Id))
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Data: %s has a duplicate Id \"%s\" (entry %d); the first one is used."), FileName, *Id.ToString(), i);
				continue;
			}
			OutIndex.Add(Id, i);
		}
	}

	template <typename T>
	const T* FindIn(const TArray<T>& Rows, const TMap<FName, int32>& Index, FName Id)
	{
		const int32* Found = Index.Find(Id);
		return (Found && Rows.IsValidIndex(*Found)) ? &Rows[*Found] : nullptr;
	}
}

UGBDataSubsystem* UGBDataSubsystem::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (const UGameInstance* DirectInstance = Cast<UGameInstance>(WorldContextObject))
	{
		return DirectInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>();
	}
	const UWorld* World = GEngine ? GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull) : nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = World ? World->GetGameInstance() : nullptr;
	return GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBDataSubsystem::Initialize(FSubsystemCollectionBase& Collection)
{
	Super::Initialize(Collection);
	LoadAll();
}

void UGBDataSubsystem::ReloadAll()
{
	LoadAll();
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Log, TEXT("Data: reloaded from %s"), *GetDataDir());
	OnDataReloaded.Broadcast();
}

FString UGBDataSubsystem::GetDataDir()
{
	return FPaths::ProjectContentDir() / TEXT("Data/");
}

void UGBDataSubsystem::LoadAll()
{
	using namespace GBDataPrivate;

	LoadStruct(TEXT("Player.json"), Player);
	LoadStruct(TEXT("QTE.json"), QTE);
	LoadStruct(TEXT("Game.json"), Game);

	LoadArray(TEXT("Spells.json"), TEXT("Spells"), Spells);
	LoadArray(TEXT("Weapons.json"), TEXT("Weapons"), Weapons);
	LoadArray(TEXT("StatusEffects.json"), TEXT("StatusEffects"), StatusEffects);
	LoadArray(TEXT("Items.json"), TEXT("Items"), Items);
	LoadArray(TEXT("Items.json"), TEXT("Recipes"), Recipes);
	LoadArray(TEXT("Enemies.json"), TEXT("Enemies"), Enemies);
	LoadArray(TEXT("Encounters.json"), TEXT("Encounters"), Encounters);
	LoadArray(TEXT("Skills.json"), TEXT("Skills"), SkillNodes);
	LoadArray(TEXT("Sprites.json"), TEXT("Sprites"), Sprites);

	BuildIndex(Spells, SpellIndex, TEXT("Spells.json"));
	BuildIndex(Weapons, WeaponIndex, TEXT("Weapons.json"));
	BuildIndex(StatusEffects, StatusEffectIndex, TEXT("StatusEffects.json"));
	BuildIndex(Items, ItemIndex, TEXT("Items.json"));
	BuildIndex(Enemies, EnemyIndex, TEXT("Enemies.json"));
	BuildIndex(Encounters, EncounterIndex, TEXT("Encounters.json"));
	BuildIndex(SkillNodes, SkillNodeIndex, TEXT("Skills.json"));
	BuildIndex(Sprites, SpriteIndex, TEXT("Sprites.json"));

	// Recipes are never looked up by id, but a duplicate is still a data mistake worth a warning.
	TMap<FName, int32> RecipeIndex;
	BuildIndex(Recipes, RecipeIndex, TEXT("Items.json (Recipes)"));

	WarnedGameKeys.Reset();
}

const FGBSpellDefinition* UGBDataSubsystem::FindSpell(FName Id) const { return GBDataPrivate::FindIn(Spells, SpellIndex, Id); }
const FGBWeaponDefinition* UGBDataSubsystem::FindWeapon(FName Id) const { return GBDataPrivate::FindIn(Weapons, WeaponIndex, Id); }
const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* UGBDataSubsystem::FindStatusEffect(FName Id) const { return GBDataPrivate::FindIn(StatusEffects, StatusEffectIndex, Id); }
const FGBItemDefinition* UGBDataSubsystem::FindItem(FName Id) const { return GBDataPrivate::FindIn(Items, ItemIndex, Id); }
const FGBEnemyDefinition* UGBDataSubsystem::FindEnemy(FName Id) const { return GBDataPrivate::FindIn(Enemies, EnemyIndex, Id); }
const FGBEncounterDefinition* UGBDataSubsystem::FindEncounter(FName Id) const { return GBDataPrivate::FindIn(Encounters, EncounterIndex, Id); }
const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* UGBDataSubsystem::FindSkillNode(FName Id) const { return GBDataPrivate::FindIn(SkillNodes, SkillNodeIndex, Id); }
const FGBSpriteDefinition* UGBDataSubsystem::FindSprite(FName Id) const { return GBDataPrivate::FindIn(Sprites, SpriteIndex, Id); }

float UGBDataSubsystem::GetGameValue(FName Key, float DefaultValue) const
{
	if (const float* Found = Game.Values.Find(Key))
	{
		return *Found;
	}
	if (!WarnedGameKeys.Contains(Key))
	{
		WarnedGameKeys.Add(Key);
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Data: Game.json has no Values \"%s\"; using %f."), *Key.ToString(), DefaultValue);
	}
	return DefaultValue;
}

FString UGBDataSubsystem::GetGameString(FName Key, const FString& DefaultValue) const
{
	if (const FString* Found = Game.Strings.Find(Key))
	{
		return *Found;
	}
	if (!WarnedGameKeys.Contains(Key))
	{
		WarnedGameKeys.Add(Key);
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Data: Game.json has no Strings \"%s\"; using \"%s\"."), *Key.ToString(), *DefaultValue);
	}
	return DefaultValue;
}

FLinearColor UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(const FString& Hex, const FLinearColor& DefaultColor)
{
	FString Digits = Hex.TrimStartAndEnd();
	Digits.RemoveFromStart(TEXT("#"));
	if (Digits.Len() != 6 && Digits.Len() != 8)
	{
		return DefaultColor;
	}
	for (const TCHAR Char : Digits)
	{
		if (!FChar::IsHexDigit(Char))
		{
			return DefaultColor;
		}
	}
	return FLinearColor::FromSRGBColor(FColor::FromHex(Digits));
}

#include "Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.h"

#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.h"
#include "Weapons/GBGun.h"
#include "Weapons/GBStaff.h"
#include "Weapons/GBSword.h"
#include "World/GBPickup.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

namespace
{
	/** Where a replaced weapon is dropped: in front of the player (placement offset, not a gameplay number). */
	constexpr float EquipDropDistanceCm = 120.f;

	/** Cycle order (GDD 9): Staff -> Gun -> Sword. */
	const EGBHeldSlot EquipSlotOrder[] = { EGBHeldSlot::Staff, EGBHeldSlot::Gun, EGBHeldSlot::Sword };

	int32 EquipSlotIndex(EGBHeldSlot Slot)
	{
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < static_cast<int32>(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(EquipSlotOrder)); ++Index)
		{
			if (EquipSlotOrder[Index] == Slot)
			{
				return Index;
			}
		}
		return 0;
	}
}

UGBEquipmentComponent::UGBEquipmentComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	// Quiet teardown: no unequip callbacks (they touch the camera and broadcast to a HUD that may be gone).
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get(); IsValid(Item))
		{
			Item->Destroy();
		}
	}
	Items.Reset();
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::SpawnItem(FName WeaponId)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner());
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
	if (!Player || !World || !Def)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GiveWeapon: unknown weapon '%s' (or no player / world)"), *WeaponId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	UClass* ItemClass = nullptr;
	if (!Def->ActorClass.IsEmpty())
	{
		ItemClass = LoadClass<AGBHeldItem>(nullptr, *Def->ActorClass);
		if (!ItemClass || ItemClass->HasAnyClassFlags(CLASS_Abstract))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapon '%s': ActorClass '%s' not found or abstract, using the slot default"), *WeaponId.ToString(), *Def->ActorClass);
			ItemClass = nullptr;
		}
	}
	if (!ItemClass)
	{
		switch (Def->Slot)
		{
		case EGBHeldSlot::Staff: ItemClass = AGBStaff::StaticClass(); break;
		case EGBHeldSlot::Sword: ItemClass = AGBSword::StaticClass(); break;
		default: ItemClass = AGBGun::StaticClass(); break;
		}
	}

	// Copy the definition: spawning must not rely on a pointer into the data tables.
	const FGBWeaponDefinition Definition = *Def;

	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
	Params.Owner = Player;
	Params.Instigator = Player;
	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
	AGBHeldItem* Item = World->SpawnActor<AGBHeldItem>(ItemClass, Player->GetActorTransform(), Params);
	if (!Item)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GiveWeapon: could not spawn '%s'"), *WeaponId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	USceneComponent* Anchor = Player->GetHeldItemAnchor() ? Player->GetHeldItemAnchor() : Player->GetRootComponent();
	Item->AttachToComponent(Anchor, FAttachmentTransformRules::SnapToTargetNotIncludingScale);
	Item->InitFromDefinition(Definition, Player);
	Item->OnUnequipped();
	Item->RefillAmmo();
	return Item;
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::GiveWeapon(FName WeaponId, bool bAutoSwitch, bool bDropOld)
{
	AGBHeldItem* NewItem = SpawnItem(WeaponId);
	if (!NewItem)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const EGBHeldSlot NewSlot = NewItem->GetSlot();
	const bool bHandsEmpty = GetActiveItem() == nullptr;
	bool bReplacedActive = false;

	if (AGBHeldItem* Old = GetItemInSlot(NewSlot))
	{
		bReplacedActive = Old->IsEquipped();
		Old->StopPrimary();
		Old->StopSecondary();
		Old->OnUnequipped();
		if (bDropOld)
		{
			if (const AActor* Owner = GetOwner())
			{
				FVector Forward = Owner->GetActorForwardVector();
				Forward.Z = 0.f;
				Forward = Forward.GetSafeNormal();
				FGBDropDefinition Drop;
				Drop.WeaponId = Old->GetWeaponId();
				AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(GetWorld(), Drop, Owner->GetActorLocation() + Forward * EquipDropDistanceCm);
			}
		}
		Old->Destroy();
	}
	Items.Add(NewSlot, NewItem);

	if (bHandsEmpty || bReplacedActive)
	{
		EquipSlot(NewSlot);
	}
	else if (!bAutoSwitch || !SwitchTo(NewSlot))
	{
		OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
	}
	return NewItem;
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::HasWeaponInSlot(EGBHeldSlot Slot) const
{
	return GetItemInSlot(Slot) != nullptr;
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::GetItemInSlot(EGBHeldSlot Slot) const
{
	AGBHeldItem* Item = Items.FindRef(Slot).Get();
	return IsValid(Item) ? Item : nullptr;
}

AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::GetActiveItem() const
{
	AGBHeldItem* Item = GetItemInSlot(ActiveSlot);
	return (Item && Item->IsEquipped()) ? Item : nullptr;
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::IsHoldingStaff() const
{
	return ActiveSlot == EGBHeldSlot::Staff && GetActiveItem() != nullptr;
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::IsHoldingGun() const
{
	return ActiveSlot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun && GetActiveItem() != nullptr;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::EquipSlot(EGBHeldSlot Slot)
{
	AGBHeldItem* Target = GetItemInSlot(Slot);
	AGBHeldItem* Current = GetItemInSlot(ActiveSlot);
	if (Current && Current != Target && Current->IsEquipped())
	{
		Current->StopPrimary();
		Current->StopSecondary();
		Current->OnUnequipped();
	}

	ActiveSlot = Slot;

	if (const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner()))
	{
		if (UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook())
		{
			Spellbook->CancelSustained();
		}
	}

	if (Target && !Target->IsEquipped())
	{
		Target->OnEquipped();
	}

	OnActiveItemChanged.Broadcast(Target);
	OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
}

bool UGBEquipmentComponent::SwitchTo(EGBHeldSlot Slot)
{
	if (bSwitchLocked || !HasWeaponInSlot(Slot))
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (Slot == ActiveSlot && GetActiveItem() != nullptr)
	{
		return true;
	}
	EquipSlot(Slot);
	return true;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::CycleSlot(int32 Direction)
{
	if (bSwitchLocked)
	{
		return;
	}
	const int32 Count = static_cast<int32>(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(EquipSlotOrder));
	const int32 Step = Direction >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
	const int32 Start = EquipSlotIndex(ActiveSlot);
	for (int32 Offset = 1; Offset < Count; ++Offset)
	{
		const int32 Index = ((Start + Step * Offset) % Count + Count) % Count;
		if (HasWeaponInSlot(EquipSlotOrder[Index]))
		{
			SwitchTo(EquipSlotOrder[Index]);
			return;
		}
	}
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::RefillAllAmmo()
{
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get(); IsValid(Item))
		{
			Item->RefillAmmo();
		}
	}
	OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
}

int32 UGBEquipmentComponent::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
{
	AGBHeldItem* Gun = GetItemInSlot(EGBHeldSlot::Gun);
	return Gun ? Gun->AddSpareMagazines(Count) : 0;
}

EGBHudSkin UGBEquipmentComponent::GetHudSkin() const
{
	const AGBHeldItem* Active = GetActiveItem();
	return Active ? Active->GetHudSkin() : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::DestroyAllItems()
{
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get(); IsValid(Item))
		{
			Item->StopPrimary();
			Item->StopSecondary();
			Item->OnUnequipped();
			Item->Destroy();
		}
	}
	Items.Reset();
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.Weapons.Reset();
	Snapshot.Ammo.Reset();
	Snapshot.WeaponElements.Reset();
	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
	{
		const AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get();
		if (!IsValid(Item))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Snapshot.Weapons.Add(Item->GetWeaponId());
		Snapshot.WeaponElements.Add(Item->GetWeaponId(), Item->GetElement());
		if (Item->UsesAmmo())
		{
			Snapshot.Ammo.Add(Item->GetWeaponId(), Item->GetAmmo());
		}
	}
	Snapshot.ActiveSlot = ActiveSlot;
}

void UGBEquipmentComponent::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{
	DestroyAllItems();

	for (const FName& WeaponId : Snapshot.Weapons)
	{
		AGBHeldItem* Item = SpawnItem(WeaponId);
		if (!Item)
		{
			continue;
		}
		if (AGBHeldItem* Previous = GetItemInSlot(Item->GetSlot()))
		{
			Previous->Destroy(); // two weapons of one slot in a snapshot: the last one wins
		}
		Items.Add(Item->GetSlot(), Item);

		if (const EGBElement* SavedElement = Snapshot.WeaponElements.Find(WeaponId))
		{
			Item->SetElement(*SavedElement);
		}
		if (const FGBAmmoState* SavedAmmo = Snapshot.Ammo.Find(WeaponId))
		{
			Item->SetAmmo(*SavedAmmo);
		}
	}

	EGBHeldSlot Slot = Snapshot.ActiveSlot;
	if (!HasWeaponInSlot(Slot))
	{
		for (const EGBHeldSlot Candidate : EquipSlotOrder)
		{
			if (HasWeaponInSlot(Candidate))
			{
				Slot = Candidate;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	if (HasWeaponInSlot(Slot))
	{
		EquipSlot(Slot);
	}
	else
	{
		ActiveSlot = Snapshot.ActiveSlot;
		OnActiveItemChanged.Broadcast(nullptr);
		OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}
#include "Weapons/GBGun.h"

#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
#include "Camera/CameraComponent.h"
#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Combat/GBProjectile.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "DrawDebugHelpers.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Stats/GBAttributeComponent.h"

void AGBGun::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	Super::InitFromDefinition(Definition, InOwner);

	const FName Required[] = { GBStat::Damage, GBStat::FireInterval, GBStat::MagazineSize, GBStat::SpareMagazines, GBStat::ReloadTime, GBStat::Range };
	for (const FName& Stat : Required)
	{
		if (!Definition.Stats.Contains(Stat))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapons.json '%s': missing stat '%s', using a default"), *Definition.Id.ToString(), *Stat.ToString());
		}
	}

	BuildViewModel(Definition);
}

// ---- Looks ----

UStaticMeshComponent* AGBGun::AddPart(FName Name, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& Location, const FVector& Size, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bGlow)
{
	UStaticMeshComponent* Part = NewObject<UStaticMeshComponent>(this, Name);
	Part->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Part->SetRelativeLocation(Location);
	Part->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Part->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Part->SetCastShadow(false);
	Part->RegisterComponent();
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Part, Shape, Size, Color, bGlow);
	Parts.Add(Part);
	return Part;
}

void AGBGun::BuildViewModel(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition)
{
	// Placeholder viewmodel (art rule: guns are 3D). Sizes in cm are purely visual; X = forward, Z = up.
	const float* PelletStat = Definition.Stats.Find(GBStat::Pellets);
	const bool bShotgun = PelletStat && *PelletStat > 1.f;
	const bool bAutomaticLook = Definition.bAutomatic;

	const float BodyLength = bShotgun ? 22.f : (bAutomaticLook ? 26.f : 16.f);
	const float BarrelLength = bShotgun ? 18.f : (bAutomaticLook ? 16.f : 6.f);
	const float BarrelThickness = bShotgun ? 4.f : 2.5f;

	const FLinearColor BodyColor(0.08f, 0.08f, 0.10f);
	const FLinearColor BarrelColor(0.03f, 0.03f, 0.035f);
	const FLinearColor GripColor(0.12f, 0.10f, 0.10f);
	const FLinearColor NeonColor(0.0f, 0.9f, 1.0f);

	AddPart(TEXT("Body"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector::ZeroVector, FVector(BodyLength, 4.f, 7.f), BodyColor, false);
	AddPart(TEXT("Barrel"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyLength * 0.5f + BarrelLength * 0.5f, 0.f, 1.5f),
		FVector(BarrelLength, BarrelThickness, BarrelThickness), BarrelColor, false);
	AddPart(TEXT("Grip"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(-BodyLength * 0.5f + 3.f, 0.f, -6.f), FVector(3.5f, 3.5f, 8.f), GripColor, false);
	if (bAutomaticLook)
	{
		AddPart(TEXT("Magazine"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyLength * 0.15f, 0.f, -6.f), FVector(3.f, 2.5f, 8.f), BodyColor, false);
	}
	AddPart(TEXT("Neon"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(0.f, 2.1f, 1.f), FVector(BodyLength * 0.8f, 0.3f, 0.6f), NeonColor, true);
}

// ---- Ammo ----

int32 AGBGun::GetMagazineSize() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::MagazineSize, 1.f)));
}

int32 AGBGun::GetMaxSpareMagazines() const
{
	return FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::SpareMagazines, 0.f)));
}

void AGBGun::SetAmmo(const FGBAmmoState& InAmmo)
{
	CancelReload();
	Ammo.InMagazine = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.InMagazine, 0, GetMagazineSize());
	Ammo.SpareMagazines = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.SpareMagazines, 0, GetMaxSpareMagazines());
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::RefillAmmo()
{
	CancelReload();
	Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
	Ammo.SpareMagazines = GetMaxSpareMagazines();
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

int32 AGBGun::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
{
	if (Count <= 0)
	{
		return 0;
	}
	const int32 Added = FMath::Clamp(GetMaxSpareMagazines() - Ammo.SpareMagazines, 0, Count);
	if (Added > 0)
	{
		Ammo.SpareMagazines += Added;
		NotifyEquipmentChanged();
	}
	return Added;
}

// ---- Reload ----

void AGBGun::Reload()
{
	if (bReloading || !bEquipped)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (Ammo.InMagazine >= GetMagazineSize() || Ammo.SpareMagazines <= 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	bReloading = true;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	ReloadDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, GetStat(GBStat::ReloadTime, 1.f));
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::CancelReload()
{
	if (!bReloading)
	{
		return;
	}
	bReloading = false;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

void AGBGun::FinishReload()
{
	bReloading = false;
	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
	if (Ammo.SpareMagazines > 0)
	{
		// The rounds left in the old magazine are lost (provisional, classic tactical style).
		--Ammo.SpareMagazines;
		Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
	}
	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
}

float AGBGun::GetReloadProgress() const
{
	if (!bReloading)
	{
		return 0.f;
	}
	return ReloadDuration > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? FMath::Clamp(ReloadElapsed / ReloadDuration, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
}

// ---- Aim ----

void AGBGun::StartSecondary()
{
	if (bEquipped)
	{
		SetAiming(true);
	}
}

void AGBGun::StopSecondary()
{
	SetAiming(false);
}
	if (!PendingSpellId.IsNone())
	{
		return true;
	}
	if (const UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE())
	{
		if (QTE->IsActive())
		{
			return true;
		}
	}
	return IsValid(ActiveSustained) && ActiveSustained->IsSustaining();
}

EGBCastFailReason UGBSpellbookComponent::CanCast(FName SpellId) const
{
	UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSpellDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSpell(SpellId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition || Definition->bPassive)
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell;
	}
	if (!KnowsSpell(SpellId))
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::NotKnown;
	}
	const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment();
	const bool bRightItem = Equipment && (Definition->bRequiresGun ? Equipment->IsHoldingGun() : Equipment->IsHoldingStaff());
	if (!bRightItem)
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::WrongHeldItem;
	}
	if (IsBusy())
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::Busy;
	}
	if (GetCooldownRemaining(SpellId) > 0.f)
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::OnCooldown;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes();
	const float Cost = GetManaCost(SpellId);
	if (Cost > 0.f && (!Attributes || !Attributes->HasMana(Cost)))
	{
		return EGBCastFailReason::NotEnoughMana;
	}
	return EGBCastFailReason::None;
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastSlot(int32 Slot)
{
	const FName SpellId = GetSlotSpell(Slot);
	if (!SpellId.IsNone() && IsValid(ActiveSustained) && ActiveSustained->GetSpellCast().SpellId == SpellId)
	{
		ActiveSustained->OnCastReleased();
		return true;
	}
	if (SpellId.IsNone())
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(NAME_None, EGBCastFailReason::EmptySlot);
		return false;
	}
	return TryCastSpell(SpellId);
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastEnchant()
{
	if (EnchantSpellId.IsNone())
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(NAME_None, EGBCastFailReason::UnknownSpell);
		return false;
	}
	return TryCastSpell(EnchantSpellId);
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastSpell(FName SpellId)
{
	const EGBCastFailReason Reason = CanCast(SpellId);
	if (Reason != EGBCastFailReason::None)
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, Reason);
		return false;
	}
	return BeginCast(SpellId);
}

bool UGBSpellbookComponent::BeginCast(FName SpellId)
{
	const float Cost = GetManaCost(SpellId);
	UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = GetOwnerAttributes();
	if (Cost > 0.f && (!Attributes || !Attributes->SpendMana(Cost)))
	{
		OnCastFailed.Broadcast(SpellId, EGBCastFailReason::NotEnoughMana);
		return false;
	}

	UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwnerEquipment();
	if (Equipment)
	{
		Equipment->SetSwitchLocked(true);
	}
	PendingSpellId = SpellId;

	UGBQTEComponent* QTE = GetOwnerQTE();
	if (!QTE)
	{
		// No QTE on the owner: cast at default strength, as when the QTE is switched off.
		FGBQTEResult Result;
		Result.bSuccess = true;
		Result.bUsedQTE = false;
		FinishCast(SpellId, Result);
		return true;
	}

	// The delegate may fire before StartQTE returns (QTE off).
	if (!QTE->StartQTE(SpellId, FGBOnQTEFinished::CreateUObject(this, &UGBSpellbookComponent::HandleQTEFinished, SpellId)))
	{
		PendingSpellId = NAME_None;
		if (Equipment)
		{
			Equipment->SetSwitchLocked(false);
		}
		if (Attributes && Cost > 0.f)
		{
			Attributes->RestoreMana(Cost);

#include "AI/GBEncounter.h"

#include "Components/BoxComponent.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"

#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"

AGBEncounter::AGBEncounter()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	Trigger = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBoxComponent>(TEXT("Trigger"));
	RootComponent = Trigger;
	Trigger->InitBoxExtent(TriggerExtent);
	Trigger->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
	Trigger->SetCollisionObjectType(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	Trigger->SetCollisionResponseToAllChannels(ECR_Ignore);
	Trigger->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Overlap);
	Trigger->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
	Trigger->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
	Trigger->SetHiddenInGame(true);
}

void AGBEncounter::OnConstruction(const FTransform& Transform)
{
	Super::OnConstruction(Transform);
	Trigger->SetBoxExtent(TriggerExtent);
}

void AGBEncounter::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	Trigger->SetBoxExtent(TriggerExtent);
	Trigger->OnComponentBeginOverlap.AddUniqueDynamic(this, &AGBEncounter::HandleTriggerOverlap);

	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->OnEraChanged.AddUObject(this, &AGBEncounter::HandleEraChanged);
	}

	ApplyClearedFlagIfSet();
}

void AGBEncounter::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->OnEraChanged.RemoveAll(this);
	}
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBEncounter::Configure(FName InEncounterId, EGBEraPresence InPresence, const FVector& InTriggerExtent, TConstArrayView<FVector> InSpawnPoints)
{
	EncounterId = InEncounterId;
	Presence = InPresence;
	TriggerExtent = InTriggerExtent;
	SpawnPoints.Reset();
	SpawnPoints.Append(InSpawnPoints.GetData(), InSpawnPoints.Num());
	bLoggedMissingDefinition = false;

	Trigger->SetBoxExtent(TriggerExtent);
	if (HasActorBegunPlay())
	{
		ApplyClearedFlagIfSet();
	}
}

const FGBEncounterDefinition* AGBEncounter::FindDefinition() const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	return Data ? Data->FindEncounter(EncounterId) : nullptr;
}

int32 AGBEncounter::GetNumWaves() const
{
	const FGBEncounterDefinition* Def = FindDefinition();
	return Def ? Def->Waves.Num() : 0;
}

int32 AGBEncounter::GetAliveCount() const
{
	int32 Count = 0;
	for (const TObjectPtr<AGBEnemyCharacter>& Enemy : AliveEnemies)
	{
		if (IsValid(Enemy) && !Enemy->IsDead())
		{
			++Count;
		}
	}
	return Count;
}

FName AGBEncounter::GetClearedFlag() const
{
	const FGBEncounterDefinition* Def = FindDefinition();
	if (Def && !Def->ClearedFlag.IsNone())
	{
		return Def->ClearedFlag;
	}
	return FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Encounter.%s.Cleared"), *EncounterId.ToString()));
}

void AGBEncounter::ApplyClearedFlagIfSet()
{
	if (State == EGBEncounterState::Cleared || EncounterId.IsNone())
	{
		return;
	}
	const UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (Progress && Progress->HasFlag(GetClearedFlag()))
	{
		ResetEncounter();
		State = EGBEncounterState::Cleared;
		SetActorTickEnabled(false);
	}
}

void AGBEncounter::HandleTriggerOverlap(UPrimitiveComponent* OverlappedComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp,
	int32 OtherBodyIndex, bool bFromSweep, const FHitResult& SweepResult)
{
	if (OtherActor && OtherActor == AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this))
	{
		TryStartFromTrigger(true);
	}
}

void AGBEncounter::HandleEraChanged(EGBEra NewEra)
{
	TryStartFromTrigger();
}

void AGBEncounter::TryStartFromTrigger(bool bPlayerKnownInside)
{
	if (State != EGBEncounterState::Waiting || EncounterId.IsNone())
	{
		return;
	}

	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	if (!Player || Player->IsDead())
	{
		return;
	}

	const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this);
	const EGBEra CurrentEra = Era ? Era->GetCurrentEra() : EGBEra::Past;
	if (!UGBEraSubsystem::IsPresentIn(Presence, CurrentEra))
	{
		return;
	}

	if (!bPlayerKnownInside && !Trigger->IsOverlappingActor(Player))
	{
		return;
	}

	StartEncounter();
}

void AGBEncounter::StartEncounter()
{
	if (State != EGBEncounterState::Waiting)
	{
		return;
	}

	ApplyClearedFlagIfSet();
	if (State == EGBEncounterState::Cleared)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FGBEncounterDefinition* Def = FindDefinition();
	if (!Def)
	{
		if (!bLoggedMissingDefinition)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: unknown encounter id '%s'"), *GetName(), *EncounterId.ToString());
			bLoggedMissingDefinition = true;
		}
		return;
	}
	const int32 NumWaves = Def->Waves.Num();

	State = EGBEncounterState::Active;
	CurrentWave = -1;
	NextWaveTimer = -1.f;
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
	OnStarted.Broadcast(this);

	if (NumWaves == 0)
	{
		Clear();
		return;
	}
	// The first wave comes at once; DelayBefore counts from the previous wave being cleared.
	SpawnWave(0);
}

void AGBEncounter::SpawnWave(int32 WaveIndex)
{
	const FGBEncounterDefinition* Def = FindDefinition();
	if (!Def || !Def->Waves.IsValidIndex(WaveIndex))
	{
		Clear();
		return;
	}

	CurrentWave = WaveIndex;
	const FGBEncounterWave& Wave = Def->Waves[WaveIndex];
	const int32 NumWaves = Def->Waves.Num();

	int32 Total = 0;
	for (const FGBEncounterSpawn& Spawn : Wave.Spawns)
	{
		Total += FMath::Max(0, Spawn.Count);
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const float RingRadius = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEncounterKey::SpawnRingRadius, 400.f) : 400.f;
	const FVector Center = GetActorLocation();
	const FTransform& ActorTransform = GetActorTransform();

	// Copy the spawn list: spawning runs BeginPlay code that could, in theory, reload data.
	const TArray<FGBEncounterSpawn> Spawns = Wave.Spawns;
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	int32 SpawnIndex = 0;
	for (const FGBEncounterSpawn& Spawn : Spawns)
	{
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Spawn.Count; ++i, ++SpawnIndex)
		{
			FVector Location;
			if (SpawnPoints.Num() > 0)
			{
				Location = ActorTransform.TransformPosition(SpawnPoints[SpawnIndex % SpawnPoints.Num()]);
			}
			else
			{
				const float Angle = 2.f * PI * static_cast<float>(SpawnIndex) / static_cast<float>(FMath::Max(Total, 1));
				Location = Center + FVector(FMath::Cos(Angle), FMath::Sin(Angle), 0.f) * RingRadius;
			}

			const FRotator Facing(0.f, (Center - Location).GetSafeNormal2D().Rotation().Yaw, 0.f);
			if (AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(World, Spawn.EnemyId, Location, Facing, Presence))
			{
				Enemy->OnDied.AddUObject(this, &AGBEncounter::HandleEnemyDied);
				AliveEnemies.Add(Enemy);
			}
		}
	}

	if (AGBHUD* HUD = AGBHUD::Get(this))
	{
		HUD->ShowMessage(FString::Printf(TEXT("Wave %d / %d"), WaveIndex + 1, NumWaves));
	}
	OnWaveStarted.Broadcast(this);
}

void AGBEncounter::HandleEnemyDied(AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy)
{
	if (Enemy)
	{
		Enemy->OnDied.RemoveAll(this);
	}
	AliveEnemies.Remove(Enemy);

	if (State == EGBEncounterState::Active && GetAliveCount() == 0)
	{
		HandleWaveDefeated();
	}
}

void AGBEncounter::HandleWaveDefeated()
{
	if (State != EGBEncounterState::Active || NextWaveTimer >= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FGBEncounterDefinition* Def = FindDefinition();
	const int32 NextWave = CurrentWave + 1;
	if (Def && Def->Waves.IsValidIndex(NextWave))
	{
		NextWaveTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, Def->Waves[NextWave].DelayBefore);
	}
	else
	{
		Clear();
	}
}

void AGBEncounter::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	switch (State)
	{
	case EGBEncounterState::Waiting:
		// Covers the player already standing inside (revived at a checkpoint in the box, encounter configured late).
		TryStartFromTrigger();
		break;

	case EGBEncounterState::Active:
	{
		const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
		if (Player && Player->IsDead())
		{
			ResetEncounter();
			break;
		}

		if (NextWaveTimer >= 0.f)
		{
			NextWaveTimer -= DeltaSeconds;
			if (NextWaveTimer <= 0.f)
			{
				NextWaveTimer = -1.f;
				SpawnWave(CurrentWave + 1);
			}
			break;
		}

		// Enemies removed without dying (fell out of the world, destroyed by a script) do not block the wave.
		AliveEnemies.RemoveAll([](const TObjectPtr<AGBEnemyCharacter>& Enemy) { return !IsValid(Enemy) || Enemy->IsDead(); });
		if (AliveEnemies.Num() == 0)
		{
			HandleWaveDefeated();
		}
		break;
	}

	case EGBEncounterState::Cleared:
	default:
		SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		break;
	}
}

void AGBEncounter::Clear()
{
	if (State == EGBEncounterState::Cleared)
	{
		return;
	}

	State = EGBEncounterState::Cleared;
	NextWaveTimer = -1.f;

	const FGBEncounterDefinition* Def = FindDefinition();
	const int32 Points = Def ? FMath::Max(0, Def->SkillPoints) : 0;
	const TArray<FGBDropDefinition> Rewards = Def ? Def->Rewards : TArray<FGBDropDefinition>();

	UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (Progress)
	{
		Progress->SetFlag(GetClearedFlag(), true);
	}

	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	if (Player && Points > 0)
	{
		if (UGBSkillTreeComponent* SkillTree = Player->GetSkillTree())
		{
			SkillTree->AddPoints(Points);
		}
	}

	if (Rewards.Num() > 0)
	{
		AGBPickup::SpawnDrops(GetWorld(), Rewards, GetActorLocation());
	}

	if (AGBHUD* HUD = AGBHUD::Get(this))
	{
		const FString Text = Points > 0
			? FString::Printf(TEXT("Encounter cleared: +%d skill point%s"), Points, Points == 1 ? TEXT("") : TEXT("s"))
			: FString(TEXT("Encounter cleared"));
		HUD->ShowMessage(Text);
	}

	if (Progress && Player && !Player->IsDead())
	{
		Progress->SaveCheckpoint(Player);
	}

	OnCleared.Broadcast(this);
	SetActorTickEnabled(false);
}

void AGBEncounter::ResetEncounter()
{
	if (State == EGBEncounterState::Cleared)
	{
		return;
	}

	for (const TObjectPtr<AGBEnemyCharacter>& Enemy : AliveEnemies)
	{
		if (IsValid(Enemy))
		{
			Enemy->OnDied.RemoveAll(this);
			Enemy->Destroy();
		}
	}
	AliveEnemies.Reset();

	State = EGBEncounterState::Waiting;
	CurrentWave = -1;
	NextWaveTimer = -1.f;
	SetActorTickEnabled(true);
}
#include "World/GBEraComponent.h"

#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "GameFramework/Pawn.h"
#include "GameFramework/PawnMovementComponent.h"
#include "World/GBEraSubsystem.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

UGBEraComponent::UGBEraComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

void UGBEraComponent::Configure(EGBEraPresence InPresence, FName InRequiredFlag, FName InRemovedByFlag)
{
	Presence = InPresence;
	RequiredFlag = InRequiredFlag;
	RemovedByFlag = InRemovedByFlag;
	if (HasBegunPlay())
	{
		Refresh();
	}
}

bool UGBEraComponent::ComputePresent(EGBEraPresence InPresence, EGBEra CurrentEra, bool bRequiredFlagOk, bool bRemovedFlagSet)
{
	return UGBEraSubsystem::IsPresentIn(InPresence, CurrentEra) && bRequiredFlagOk && !bRemovedFlagSet;
}

void UGBEraComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		EraChangedHandle = Era->OnEraChanged.AddUObject(this, &UGBEraComponent::HandleEraChanged);
	}
	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		FlagChangedHandle = Progress->OnFlagChanged.AddUObject(this, &UGBEraComponent::HandleFlagChanged);
	}
	Refresh();
}

void UGBEraComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->OnEraChanged.Remove(EraChangedHandle);
	}
	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Progress->OnFlagChanged.Remove(FlagChangedHandle);
	}
	EraChangedHandle.Reset();
	FlagChangedHandle.Reset();

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void UGBEraComponent::HandleEraChanged(EGBEra NewEra)
{
	Refresh();
}

void UGBEraComponent::HandleFlagChanged(FName Flag)
{
	if (Flag == RequiredFlag || Flag == RemovedByFlag)
	{
		Refresh();
	}
}

void UGBEraComponent::Refresh()
{
	const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this);
	const EGBEra CurrentEra = Era ? Era->GetCurrentEra() : EGBEra::Past;

	const UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	const bool bRequiredOk = RequiredFlag.IsNone() || (Progress && Progress->HasFlag(RequiredFlag));
	const bool bRemoved = !RemovedByFlag.IsNone() && Progress && Progress->HasFlag(RemovedByFlag);

	ApplyPresence(ComputePresent(Presence, CurrentEra, bRequiredOk, bRemoved));
}

void UGBEraComponent::ApplyPresence(bool bNewPresent)
{
	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	if (!Owner || bNewPresent == bPresent)
	{
		return;
	}
	bPresent = bNewPresent;

	APawn* Pawn = Cast<APawn>(Owner);
	UMovementComponent* Movement = Pawn ? Pawn->GetMovementComponent() : nullptr;

	if (!bNewPresent)
	{
		// Remember the owner's own settings (it may have turned collision or tick off itself).
		bSavedHidden = Owner->IsHidden();
		bSavedCollision = Owner->GetActorEnableCollision();
		bSavedTick = Owner->IsActorTickEnabled();
		bHasSaved = true;

		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(false);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		// An enemy of the other era must neither fall (its collision is off) nor walk.
		if (Movement)
		{
			Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(bHasSaved ? bSavedHidden : false);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(bHasSaved ? bSavedCollision : true);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(bHasSaved ? bSavedTick : true);
		if (Movement)
		{
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
		}
	}

	OnPresenceChanged.Broadcast();
}

AGBGameMode::AGBGameMode()
{
	DefaultPawnClass = AGBPlayerCharacter::StaticClass();
	PlayerControllerClass = AGBPlayerController::StaticClass();
	HUDClass = AGBHUD::StaticClass();

	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bTickEvenWhenPaused = true;
}

AGBGameMode* AGBGameMode::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	if (!WorldContextObject || !GEngine)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	UWorld* World = GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull);
	return World ? Cast<AGBGameMode>(World->GetAuthGameMode()) : nullptr;
}

void AGBGameMode::InitGame(const FString& MapName, const FString& Options, FString& ErrorMessage)
{
	Super::InitGame(MapName, Options, ErrorMessage);

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}

	bool bHasPlayerStart = false;
	for (TActorIterator<APlayerStart> It(World); It; ++It)
	{
		bHasPlayerStart = true;
		break;
	}
	if (bHasPlayerStart)
	{
		return;
	}

	// The default map is the engine's empty Entry map: build the test level in code before the player spawns.
	FActorSpawnParameters Params;
	Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
	AGBTestArena* Arena = World->SpawnActor<AGBTestArena>(AGBTestArena::StaticClass(), FTransform::Identity, Params);
	if (!Arena)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("GameMode: could not spawn the test arena."));
		return;
	}
	Arena->BuildStatic();
	ArenaStart = Arena->GetPlayerStartTransform();
	bHasArenaStart = true;
}

AActor* AGBGameMode::ChoosePlayerStart_Implementation(AController* Player)
{
	// The arena spawned a real APlayerStart, so the engine's choice already finds it.
	return Super::ChoosePlayerStart_Implementation(Player);
}

void AGBGameMode::HandlePlayerDeath(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (bWaitingToRespawn)
	{
		return;
	}
	bWaitingToRespawn = true;

	if (Player)
	{
		if (AGBPlayerController* Controller = Cast<AGBPlayerController>(Player->GetController()))
		{
			Controller->SetMenu(EGBMenu::Death);
		}
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GameMode: no data subsystem, using the default respawn delay."));
	}
	const float Delay = FMath::Max(0.f, Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBGameValue::RespawnDelay, 2.5f) : 2.5f);
	if (Delay <= 0.f)
	{
		RespawnPlayer();
		return;
	}

	RespawnRealTime = GetWorld()->GetRealTimeSeconds() + Delay;
	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(RespawnTimer, this, &AGBGameMode::RespawnPlayer, Delay, false);
}

void AGBGameMode::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	// Timers do not run while the game is paused (the death screen pauses it): fall back to real time.
	if (bWaitingToRespawn && GetWorld() && GetWorld()->GetRealTimeSeconds() >= RespawnRealTime)
	{
		RespawnPlayer();
	}
}

void AGBGameMode::RespawnPlayer()
{
	if (!bWaitingToRespawn)
	{
		return;
	}
	bWaitingToRespawn = false;

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return;
	}
	GetWorldTimerManager().ClearTimer(RespawnTimer);

	// Provisional rule (GDD 10): a fight in progress starts over; cleared encounters stay cleared.
	for (TActorIterator<AGBEncounter> It(World); It; ++It)
	{
		if (It->GetState() == EGBEncounterState::Active)
		{
			It->ResetEncounter();
		}
	}

	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	AGBPlayerController* Controller = Player ? Cast<AGBPlayerController>(Player->GetController()) : nullptr;
	if (!Controller)
	{
		Controller = Cast<AGBPlayerController>(World->GetFirstPlayerController());
	}

	UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	const bool bRestored = Player && Progress && Progress->RestoreCheckpoint(Player);

	if (Controller && Controller->GetOpenMenu() == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		Controller->SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
	}

	if (bRestored)
	{
		Player->SetControlEnabled(true);
		return;
	}

	// No checkpoint yet: start the map again (progress flags survive in the game instance).
	UE_LOG(LogGB, Log, TEXT("GameMode: no checkpoint to restore, reloading the map."));
	const FString MapPath = UWorld::RemovePIEPrefix(World->GetPackage()->GetName());
	UGameplayStatics::OpenLevel(this, FName(*MapPath));
}

#pragma once

/**
 * Spell parameter names that are not in GBStat (Core/GBStatNames.h). Shared ones live here; the ones only one
 * behavior reads live at the top of that behavior's header. All of them are keys of Spells.json "Params".
 */
namespace GBSpellParam
{
	/** World height (cm) of the spell's pixel-art sprite (Strings "Sprite"). Every behavior with a sprite reads it. */
	inline const FName SpriteSize(TEXT("SpriteSize"));
	/** cm from the caster: where a wall is raised, how far in front of the eyes the shield sprite sits. */
	inline const FName Distance(TEXT("Distance"));
}

/**
 * A behavior is spawned by UGBSpellbookComponent at the cast origin, receives the resolved FGBSpellCast in
 * InitSpell, and does its thing using only SpellCast.Get("<Param>") values. It destroys itself when done.
 *
 * Behaviors (each documents the params and strings it reads at the top of its header):
 *   AGBSpell_Projectile  fireball, lightning bolt      (launches an AGBProjectile)
 *   AGBSpell_Wall        fire wall, ice wall           (a wall in front of the caster; blocks and/or burns)
 *   AGBSpell_Zone        ice floor                     (ground area that applies an effect / damage over time)
 *   AGBSpell_Trap        lightning rune                (arms on the ground, triggers when an enemy steps on it)
 *   AGBSpell_Orb         static-electricity ball       (moves forward and damages everything around it)
 *   AGBSpell_Beam        flamethrower                  (sustained cone from the caster's aim)
 *   AGBSpell_Shield      ice shield                    (sustained frontal block on the caster)
 *   AGBSpell_Enchant     weapon enchant                (timed status effect on the caster that buffs the gun)
 * To add a spell that fits an existing behavior: only add an entry to Spells.json.
 * To add a new behavior: subclass this, document its params, and reference the class from Spells.json.
 *
 * Subclasses put their logic in StartSpell(), which runs once both InitSpell and BeginPlay have happened (in
 * whichever order), so the actor is in the world with its final cast data.
 */
UCLASS(Abstract)
class GLYPHBOUND_API AGBSpellActor : public AActor
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	AGBSpellActor();

	/** Called once, right after spawn. Subclasses call Super first (it stores SpellCast). */
	virtual void InitSpell(const FGBSpellCast& InCast);

	/** Sustained spells: the cast input was pressed again, the caster switched weapon, or died. Default: EndSpell(). */
	virtual void OnCastReleased();

	/** True while a sustained spell is running (the spellbook keeps a reference and blocks other casts). */
	virtual bool IsSustaining() const { return false; }

	const FGBSpellCast& GetSpellCast() const { return SpellCast; }
	AActor* GetCaster() const { return SpellCast.Caster; }

	/** Fired once when the spell is over, just before the actor is destroyed. */
	FSimpleMulticastDelegate OnSpellEnded;

protected:
	virtual void BeginPlay() override;
	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;

	/** The behavior itself. Runs once, after InitSpell and BeginPlay. */
	virtual void StartSpell() {}

	/** Broadcasts OnSpellEnded and destroys the actor. Safe to call twice. */
	virtual void EndSpell();

	/** Finds the ground under a point (line trace down, world static). False if nothing within MaxDistance. */
	bool FindGround(const FVector& From, FVector& OutGround, float MaxDistance = 2000.f) const;

	// ---- Helpers shared by the behaviors ----

	/** SpellCast.Get with a warning when the spell's data does not define the param (the default is then used). */
	float ReadParam(FName Param, float DefaultValue) const;

	/** Strings "Color", or the element color when it is missing. */
	FLinearColor GetSpellColor() const;

	/** Strings "Sprite" as an id (NAME_None when missing). */
	FName GetSpriteId() const;

	/** Sets a billboard to the spell sprite (Strings "Sprite"), SpriteSize cm tall (DefaultHeight if not in data). Hides it if there is no sprite. */
	void SetupSpellSprite(UBillboardComponent* Billboard, float DefaultHeight) const;

	/** Where the caster looks from and towards right now (player aim, pawn eyes, or the cast's origin/direction). */
	void GetCasterAim(FVector& OutOrigin, FVector& OutDirection) const;

	/** The point the caster aims at within MaxRange, dropped to the ground. Falls back to the caster's feet. */
	FVector FindAimedGroundPoint(float MaxRange) const;

	/** Alive actors with attributes that are hostile to the caster. */
	bool IsHostileTarget(const AActor* Actor) const;

	/** Hostile targets whose root is within Radius of Center. */
	void GatherHostilesInSphere(const FVector& Center, float Radius, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const;

	/** Hostile targets standing on a disc: within Radius horizontally of GroundCenter and up to Height above it. */
	void GatherHostilesOnDisc(const FVector& GroundCenter, float Radius, float Height, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const;

	/** Hostile targets touching an oriented box (Extent = half size). */
	void GatherHostilesInBox(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FVector& Extent, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const;

	/** Ends the spell after Seconds (<= 0: next tick, never inside the current call). */
	void EndSpellAfter(float Seconds);

	/** One tick of area damage: DealDamage(Amount) with the spell's element, on-hit effect and its scales. */
	void DamageTarget(AActor* Target, float Amount, const FVector& SourceLocation);

	/** Applies the spell's on-hit effect (Strings "OnHitEffect") without damage, with EffectDuration / EffectStrength. */
	void ApplyOnHitEffect(AActor* Target) const;

	/** Clamps a tick interval read from data so a 0 or negative value cannot flood the timer. */
	static float SafeInterval(float Interval);

	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<USceneComponent> Root;

	/** Named SpellCast, not Cast: a member called Cast would hide the engine Cast<T>() template. */
	UPROPERTY() FGBSpellCast SpellCast;
	bool bEnded = false;
	bool bSpellInitialized = false;
	bool bSpellStarted = false;

private:
	void TryStartSpell();
	void QueryHostiles(const FVector& Center, const FQuat& Rotation, const FCollisionShape& Shape, TArray<AActor*>& OutActors) const;

	FTimerHandle EndTimer;
};

// Params (Spells.json "Params"):

//   DamagePerSecond   damage per second to each hostile actor in the cone (dealt every TickInterval)

//   ManaPerSecond     optional; > 0 = mana drained while it runs; it stops when mana runs out (default 0)
//   EffectDuration / EffectStrength   optional multipliers on the on-hit effect (default 1)

#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"

#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Engine/StaticMesh.h"
#include "Materials/MaterialInstanceDynamic.h"
#include "Materials/MaterialInterface.h"

namespace GBPlaceholderPrivate
{
	const FName ColorParam(TEXT("Color"));

	// The engine shapes are 100 cm across.
	constexpr float ShapeSizeCm = 100.f;

	UMaterialInterface* GetMaterial(bool bGlow)
	{
		static TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface> LitMaterial;
		static TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface> GlowMaterial;

		TWeakObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface>& Cached = bGlow ? GlowMaterial : LitMaterial;
		if (!Cached.IsValid())
		{
			const TCHAR* Path = bGlow
				? TEXT("/Engine/EngineMaterials/EmissiveMeshMaterial.EmissiveMeshMaterial")
				: TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/BasicShapeMaterial.BasicShapeMaterial");
			Cached = LoadObject<UMaterialInterface>(nullptr, Path);
			if (!Cached.IsValid())
			{
				UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: cannot load material %s"), Path);
			}
		}
		return Cached.Get();
	}
}

UStaticMesh* GBPlaceholder::GetShapeMesh(EGBShape Shape)
{
	static TWeakObjectPtr<UStaticMesh> Cache[5];

	const int32 Index = FMath::Clamp(static_cast<int32>(Shape), 0, 4);
	if (!Cache[Index].IsValid())
	{
		static const TCHAR* const Paths[5] =
		{
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cube.Cube"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Sphere.Sphere"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cylinder.Cylinder"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Cone.Cone"),
			TEXT("/Engine/BasicShapes/Plane.Plane"),
		};
		Cache[Index] = LoadObject<UStaticMesh>(nullptr, Paths[Index]);
		if (!Cache[Index].IsValid())
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Placeholder: cannot load mesh %s"), Paths[Index]);
		}
	}
	return Cache[Index].Get();
}

UMaterialInstanceDynamic* GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(UStaticMeshComponent* Component, EGBShape Shape, const FVector& SizeCm, const FLinearColor& Color, bool bGlow)
{
	if (!Component)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	Component->SetStaticMesh(GetShapeMesh(Shape));
	Component->SetRelativeScale3D(SizeCm / GBPlaceholderPrivate::ShapeSizeCm);

	UMaterialInterface* BaseMaterial = GBPlaceholderPrivate::GetMaterial(bGlow);
	if (!BaseMaterial)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	UMaterialInstanceDynamic* Material = UMaterialInstanceDynamic::Create(BaseMaterial, Component);
	Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::ColorParam, Color);
	Component->SetMaterial(0, Material);
	return Material;
}

void GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(UStaticMeshComponent* Component, const FLinearColor& Color)
{
	if (!Component)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (UMaterialInstanceDynamic* Material = Cast<UMaterialInstanceDynamic>(Component->GetMaterial(0)))
	{
		Material->SetVectorParameterValue(GBPlaceholderPrivate::ColorParam, Color);
	}
}

FLinearColor GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(uint8 ElementAsByte)
{
	switch (static_cast<EGBElement>(ElementAsByte))
	{
	case EGBElement::Physical:  return FLinearColor(0.55f, 0.55f, 0.55f);
	case EGBElement::Arcane:    return FLinearColor(0.55f, 0.20f, 1.00f);
	case EGBElement::Fire:      return FLinearColor(1.00f, 0.40f, 0.05f);
	case EGBElement::Lightning: return FLinearColor(1.00f, 0.90f, 0.15f);
	case EGBElement::Ice:       return FLinearColor(0.20f, 0.90f, 1.00f);
	case EGBElement::Air:       return FLinearColor(0.65f, 1.00f, 0.70f);
	default:                    return FLinearColor::White;
	}
}

namespace

	const FName ArmorModifierSource(TEXT("Armor"));

	float ReadItemStat(const FGBItemDefinition& Def, FName Key, float DefaultValue)
	{
		if (const float* Value = Def.Stats.Find(Key))
		{
			return *Value;
		}
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Item '%s' has no stat '%s'; using %.2f."), *Def.Id.ToString(), *Key.ToString(), DefaultValue);
		return DefaultValue;
	}

	FString ItemDisplayName(const FGBItemDefinition* Def, FName ItemId)
	{
		return (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : ItemId.ToString();
	}

	FString ElementName(EGBElement Element)
	{
		return StaticEnum<EGBElement>()->GetNameStringByValue(static_cast<int64>(Element));
	}

UGBInventoryComponent::UGBInventoryComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		DataReloadedHandle = Data->OnDataReloaded.AddUObject(this, &UGBInventoryComponent::HandleDataReloaded);
	}
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.Remove(DataReloadedHandle);
	}
	DataReloadedHandle.Reset();

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::InitializeFromData()
{
	if (!EquippedArmor.IsNone())
	{
		EquippedArmor = NAME_None;
		ApplyArmorModifiers();
	}
	Items.Reset();
	QuickUseItem = NAME_None;

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Inventory: no data subsystem, no starting items."));
		OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
		return;
	}

	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Pair : Data->GetPlayer().StartingItems)
	{
		AddItem(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
	}
	OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
}

int32 UGBInventoryComponent::AddItem(FName ItemId, int32 Count)
{
	if (Count <= 0)
	{
		return 0;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(ItemId) : nullptr;
	if (!Def)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("AddItem: unknown item '%s'."), *ItemId.ToString());
		return 0;
	}

	if (Def->Type == EGBItemType::Ammo)
	{
		UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwner() ? GetOwner()->FindComponentByClass<UGBEquipmentComponent>() : nullptr;
		if (!Equipment)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		const float* PerItem = Def->Stats.Find(GBStat::SpareMagazines);
		const int32 Magazines = Count * (PerItem ? FMath::RoundToInt(*PerItem) : 1);
		// A full gun takes nothing, so the pickup stays on the floor.
		return (Magazines > 0 && Equipment->AddSpareMagazines(Magazines) > 0) ? Count : 0;
	}

	if (Def->MaxStack <= 0)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("AddItem: item '%s' has MaxStack %d; treating it as 1."), *ItemId.ToString(), Def->MaxStack);
	}
	const int32 MaxStack = FMath::Max(1, Def->MaxStack);
	const int32 Current = GetCount(ItemId);
	const int32 Taken = FMath::Clamp(MaxStack - Current, 0, Count);
	if (Taken <= 0)
	{
		return 0;
	}

	Items.Add(ItemId, Current + Taken);
	if (QuickUseItem.IsNone() && Def->bQuickUse)
	{
		QuickUseItem = ItemId;
	}

	OnItemCountChanged.Broadcast(ItemId, Current + Taken);
	OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
	return Taken;
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::RemoveItem(FName ItemId, int32 Count)
{
	if (Count <= 0)
	{
		return true;
	}

	const int32 Current = GetCount(ItemId);
	if (Current < Count)
	{
		return false;
	}

	const int32 NewCount = Current - Count;
	if (NewCount > 0)
	{
		Items.Add(ItemId, NewCount);
	}
	else
	{
		Items.Remove(ItemId);
		HandleStackEmptied(ItemId);
	}

	OnItemCountChanged.Broadcast(ItemId, NewCount);
	OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
	return true;
}

int32 UGBInventoryComponent::GetCount(FName ItemId) const
{
	return Items.FindRef(ItemId);
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::HandleStackEmptied(FName ItemId)
{
	if (QuickUseItem == ItemId)
	{
		QuickUseItem = FindQuickUseFrom(ItemId, 1);
	}
	if (EquippedArmor == ItemId)
	{
		EquippedArmor = NAME_None;
		ApplyArmorModifiers();
	}
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::UseItem(FName ItemId)
{
	if (!HasItem(ItemId))
	{
		return false;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(ItemId) : nullptr;
	if (!Def)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("UseItem: unknown item '%s'."), *ItemId.ToString());
		return false;
	}

	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner());
	if (Player && Player->IsDead())
	{
		return false;
	}

	if (Def->Type == EGBItemType::Consumable)
	{
		UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(GetOwner());
		if (!Effects)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("UseItem: '%s' has no status effect component to apply '%s'."), *GetNameSafe(GetOwner()), *Def->UseEffect.ToString());
			return false;
		}
		if (!Effects->ApplyEffect(Def->UseEffect, GetOwner()))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("UseItem: item '%s' has an unknown UseEffect '%s'."), *ItemId.ToString(), *Def->UseEffect.ToString());
			return false;
		}
		return RemoveItem(ItemId, 1);
	}

	if (Def->Type == EGBItemType::Throwable)
	{
		// Copy: removing the item broadcasts, and a listener could reload the data.
		const FGBItemDefinition DefCopy = *Def;
		if (!UseThrowable(DefCopy))
		{
			return false;
		}
		return RemoveItem(ItemId, 1);
	}

	return false;
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::UseThrowable(const FGBItemDefinition& Def)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner());
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!Player || !World)
	{
		return false;
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage.Amount = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::Damage, 0.f);
	Config.Damage.Element = Def.Element;
	Config.Damage.Instigator = Player;
	Config.Damage.SourceId = Def.Id;
	if (const FString* OnHit = Def.Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
	{
		Config.Damage.OnHitEffect = FName(**OnHit);
	}

	Config.ExplosionRadius = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::Radius, 0.f);
	Config.Speed = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::Speed, Config.Speed);
	Config.FuseTime = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::FuseTime, 0.f);
	Config.GravityScale = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 1.f);
	Config.Radius = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::ProjectileRadius, Config.Radius);
	if (const float* Range = Def.Stats.Find(GBStat::Range))
	{
		Config.MaxRange = *Range;
	}

	// Art rule (GDD 3): the grenade is cyberpunk, so it is 3D.
	Config.bPixelArt = false;
	const FLinearColor ElementTint = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Def.Element));
	const FString* ColorText = Def.Strings.Find(GBString::Color);
	Config.Color = ColorText ? UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(*ColorText, ElementTint) : ElementTint;

	FRotator ThrowRotation = Player->GetAimDirection().Rotation();
	const float Tilt = ReadItemStat(Def, GBItemStat::ThrowPitch, 8.f);
	ThrowRotation.Pitch = FMath::Clamp(ThrowRotation.Pitch + static_cast<double>(Tilt), -89.0, 89.0);

	return AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Player->GetMuzzleLocation(), ThrowRotation.Vector(), Player) != nullptr;
}

FName UGBInventoryComponent::FindQuickUseFrom(FName From, int32 Direction) const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		return NAME_None;
	}

	const TArray<FGBItemDefinition>& Defs = Data->GetItems();
	const int32 Num = Defs.Num();
	if (Num == 0)
	{
		return NAME_None;
	}

	const int32 Step = Direction >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
	int32 Start = Defs.IndexOfByPredicate([From](const FGBItemDefinition& Def) { return Def.Id == From; });
	if (Start == INDEX_NONE)
	{
		// So that the first step lands on the first (or last) item.
		Start = Step > 0 ? Num - 1 : 0;
	}

	for (int32 Offset = 1; Offset <= Num; ++Offset)
	{
		const int32 Index = ((Start + Step * Offset) % Num + Num) % Num;
		const FGBItemDefinition& Def = Defs[Index];
		if (Def.bQuickUse && GetCount(Def.Id) > 0)
		{
			return Def.Id;
		}
	}
	return NAME_None;
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::CycleQuickUse(int32 Direction)
{
	const FName Next = FindQuickUseFrom(QuickUseItem, Direction);
	if (Next != QuickUseItem)
	{
		QuickUseItem = Next;
		OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::UseQuickItem()
{
	if (QuickUseItem.IsNone())
	{
		return false;
	}
	return UseItem(QuickUseItem);
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::EquipArmor(FName ItemId)
{
	if (!ItemId.IsNone())
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(ItemId) : nullptr;
		if (!Def || Def->Type != EGBItemType::Armor)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("EquipArmor: '%s' is not an armor."), *ItemId.ToString());
			return false;
		}
		if (!HasItem(ItemId))
		{
			return false;
		}
	}

	EquippedArmor = ItemId;
	ApplyArmorModifiers();
	OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
	return true;
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::ApplyArmorModifiers()
{
	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	if (!Stats)
	{
		return;
	}

	Stats->RemoveModifiers(ArmorModifierSource);
	if (EquippedArmor.IsNone())
	{
		return;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(EquippedArmor) : nullptr;
	if (!Def || Def->Type != EGBItemType::Armor)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Equipped armor '%s' is no longer an armor in the data; unequipping it."), *EquippedArmor.ToString());
		EquippedArmor = NAME_None;
		return;
	}
	Stats->AddModifiers(ArmorModifierSource, Def->Modifiers);
}

const FGBRecipeDefinition* UGBInventoryComponent::FindRecipe(FName ItemA, FName ItemB) const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	for (const FGBRecipeDefinition& Recipe : Data->GetRecipes())
	{
		if ((Recipe.A == ItemA && Recipe.B == ItemB) || (Recipe.A == ItemB && Recipe.B == ItemA))
		{
			return &Recipe;
		}
	}
	return nullptr;
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::TryCombine(FName ItemA, FName ItemB)
{
	const FGBRecipeDefinition* Found = FindRecipe(ItemA, ItemB);
	if (!Found)
	{
		ShowHudMessage(TEXT("These items cannot be combined"));
		return false;
	}
	// Copy: the steps below broadcast, and the pointer must not outlive this frame's data anyway.
	const FGBRecipeDefinition Recipe = *Found;

	const bool bSameItem = Recipe.A == Recipe.B;
	if (GetCount(Recipe.A) < (bSameItem ? 2 : 1) || !HasItem(Recipe.B))
	{
		return false;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Recipe.ResultItem.IsNone() && !(Data && Data->FindItem(Recipe.ResultItem)))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Recipe '%s': unknown ResultItem '%s'."), *Recipe.Id.ToString(), *Recipe.ResultItem.ToString());
		return false;
	}
	if (!Recipe.ResultWeapon.IsNone() && !(Data && Data->FindWeapon(Recipe.ResultWeapon)))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Recipe '%s': unknown ResultWeapon '%s'."), *Recipe.Id.ToString(), *Recipe.ResultWeapon.ToString());
		return false;
	}
	UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwner() ? GetOwner()->FindComponentByClass<UGBEquipmentComponent>() : nullptr;
	if (!Recipe.ResultWeapon.IsNone() && !Equipment)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Recipe '%s': the owner has no equipment to receive '%s'."), *Recipe.Id.ToString(), *Recipe.ResultWeapon.ToString());
		return false;
	}

	const FString NameA = ItemDisplayName(Data ? Data->FindItem(Recipe.A) : nullptr, Recipe.A);
	const FString NameB = ItemDisplayName(Data ? Data->FindItem(Recipe.B) : nullptr, Recipe.B);

	if (Recipe.bConsumeA)
	{
		RemoveItem(Recipe.A, 1);
	}
	if (Recipe.bConsumeB)
	{
		RemoveItem(Recipe.B, 1);
	}

	FString Message;
	if (!Recipe.ResultWeapon.IsNone())
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Weapon = Equipment->GiveWeapon(Recipe.ResultWeapon, true, false))
		{
			Weapon->SetElement(Recipe.ResultElement);
			Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Forged: %s (%s)"), *Weapon->GetDisplayName(), *ElementName(Recipe.ResultElement));
		}
	}
	if (!Recipe.ResultItem.IsNone())
	{
		UGBDataSubsystem* DataNow = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const FString ResultName = ItemDisplayName(DataNow ? DataNow->FindItem(Recipe.ResultItem) : nullptr, Recipe.ResultItem);
		if (AddItem(Recipe.ResultItem, 1) > 0 && Message.IsEmpty())
		{
			Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Crafted: %s"), *ResultName);
		}
	}
	if (!Recipe.SetsFlag.IsNone())
	{
		if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
		{
			Progress->SetFlag(Recipe.SetsFlag);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Recipe '%s': no progress subsystem to set '%s'."), *Recipe.Id.ToString(), *Recipe.SetsFlag.ToString());
		}
	}
	if (Message.IsEmpty())
	{
		Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Combined: %s + %s"), *NameA, *NameB);
	}

	ShowHudMessage(Message);
	OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
	return true;
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.Items = Items;
	Snapshot.QuickUseItem = QuickUseItem;
	Snapshot.EquippedArmor = EquippedArmor;
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{
	TSet<FName> ChangedIds;
	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Pair : Items)
	{
		ChangedIds.Add(Pair.Key);
	}

	Items.Reset();
	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Pair : Snapshot.Items)
	{
		if (Pair.Value > 0)
		{
			Items.Add(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
			ChangedIds.Add(Pair.Key);
		}
	}

	QuickUseItem = HasItem(Snapshot.QuickUseItem) ? Snapshot.QuickUseItem : FindQuickUseFrom(Snapshot.QuickUseItem, 1);
	EquippedArmor = HasItem(Snapshot.EquippedArmor) ? Snapshot.EquippedArmor : NAME_None;
	ApplyArmorModifiers();

	for (const FName& Id : ChangedIds)
	{
		OnItemCountChanged.Broadcast(Id, GetCount(Id));
	}
	OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::HandleDataReloaded()
{
	ApplyArmorModifiers();
	OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::ShowHudMessage(const FString& Text) const
{
	if (AGBHUD* Hud = AGBHUD::Get(this))
	{
		Hud->ShowMessage(Text);
	}
}

#include "AI/GBEnemyCharacter.h"
