// Named (not anonymous) so unity builds cannot collide with another file's helpers.
namespace GBEnemyCharacterLocal
{
	const FName AttackModeMelee(TEXT("Melee"));
	const FName AttackModeRanged(TEXT("Ranged"));
	const TCHAR* const DefaultEnemyProjectileSprite = TEXT("enemy_bolt");

	// Pure layout / technical constants (not gameplay tuning).
	constexpr float GroundProbeDepth = 1000.f;     // cm below the requested spawn point searched for a floor
	constexpr float SpawnLift = 2.f;               // cm above the floor, so the capsule does not start inside it
	constexpr float NavRetryDelay = 1.f;           // seconds of straight steering after a failed path request
	constexpr float ChestHeightFraction = 0.5f;    // of the capsule half height, above the center
	constexpr float TelegraphSpriteScale = 1.12f;
	constexpr float TelegraphTintBlend = 0.55f;    // towards white
}

AGBEnemyCharacter::AGBEnemyCharacter()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;

	Stats = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBStatsComponent>(TEXT("Stats"));
	Attributes = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBAttributeComponent>(TEXT("Attributes"));
	Attributes->Team = EGBTeam::Enemy;
	StatusEffects = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBStatusEffectComponent>(TEXT("StatusEffects"));
	EraComponent = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBEraComponent>(TEXT("Era"));

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(GetCapsuleComponent());
	Sprite->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Sprite->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);

	BodyMesh = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("BodyMesh"));
	BodyMesh->SetupAttachment(GetCapsuleComponent());
	BodyMesh->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	BodyMesh->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
	BodyMesh->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
	BodyMesh->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);

	// Pawn profile ignores Visibility; hitscan guns and line-of-sight traces use it, so block it.
	UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
	Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Block);
	Capsule->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Visibility, ECR_Block);

	AIControllerClass = AAIController::StaticClass();
	AutoPossessAI = EAutoPossessAI::PlacedInWorldOrSpawned;

	bUseControllerRotationYaw = false;
	bUseControllerRotationPitch = false;
	bUseControllerRotationRoll = false;
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->bOrientRotationToMovement = true;
	}
}

AGBEnemyCharacter* AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(UWorld* World, FName InEnemyId, const FVector& Location, const FRotator& Rotation, EGBEraPresence Presence)
{
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(World);
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindEnemy(InEnemyId) : nullptr;
	if (!Def)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnEnemy: unknown enemy id '%s'"), *InEnemyId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	UClass* Class = AGBEnemyCharacter::StaticClass();
	if (!Def->ActorClass.IsEmpty())
	{
		if (UClass* Loaded = LoadClass<AGBEnemyCharacter>(nullptr, *Def->ActorClass))
		{
			Class = Loaded;
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnEnemy: class '%s' of '%s' not found, using AGBEnemyCharacter"), *Def->ActorClass, *InEnemyId.ToString());
		}
	}

	const AGBEnemyCharacter* CDO = Class->GetDefaultObject<AGBEnemyCharacter>();
	const float DefaultHalfHeight = (CDO && CDO->GetCapsuleComponent()) ? CDO->GetCapsuleComponent()->GetUnscaledCapsuleHalfHeight() : 0.f;
	const float* DefHalfHeight = Def->Stats.Find(GBStat::CapsuleHalfHeight);
	const float HalfHeight = DefHalfHeight ? FMath::Max(*DefHalfHeight, 1.f) : DefaultHalfHeight;

	// Stand on the floor under the requested point, if there is one.
	FVector Ground = Location;
	FHitResult Hit;
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBEnemySpawnGround), false);
	if (World->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, Location + FVector::UpVector * HalfHeight, Location - FVector::UpVector * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::GroundProbeDepth,
		FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		Ground = Hit.ImpactPoint;
	}

	const FTransform SpawnTransform(FRotator(0.f, Rotation.Yaw, 0.f), Ground + FVector::UpVector * (HalfHeight + GBEnemyCharacterLocal::SpawnLift));
	AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBEnemyCharacter>(Class, SpawnTransform, nullptr, nullptr,
		ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AdjustIfPossibleButAlwaysSpawn);
	if (!Enemy)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnEnemy: spawn of '%s' failed"), *InEnemyId.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	Enemy->EnemyId = InEnemyId;
	Enemy->InitFromDefinition(*Def);
	Enemy->EraComponent->Presence = Presence;
	Enemy->FinishSpawning(SpawnTransform);
	return Enemy;
}

const FGBEnemyDefinition* AGBEnemyCharacter::GetDefinition() const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	return Data ? Data->FindEnemy(EnemyId) : nullptr;
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	// Placed in a level by hand (not through SpawnEnemy): read the definition now.
	if (!bInitialized)
	{
		if (const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
		{
			InitFromDefinition(*Def);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: unknown enemy id '%s'"), *GetName(), *EnemyId.ToString());
		}
	}

	EraComponent->OnPresenceChanged.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandlePresenceChanged);
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDataReloaded);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.RemoveAll(this);
	}
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::InitFromDefinition(const FGBEnemyDefinition& Definition)
{
	bInitialized = true;
	bPixelArtLook = Definition.bPixelArt;
	AttackModeName = Definition.AttackMode;

	Stats->SetBaseStats(Definition.Stats);
	Attributes->InitializeFromStats();

	UCapsuleComponent* Capsule = GetCapsuleComponent();
	const float Radius = FMath::Max(Stats->GetStat(GBStat::CapsuleRadius, Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleRadius()), 1.f);
	const float HalfHeight = FMath::Max(Stats->GetStat(GBStat::CapsuleHalfHeight, Capsule->GetUnscaledCapsuleHalfHeight()), Radius);
	Capsule->SetCapsuleSize(Radius, HalfHeight);

	if (Definition.bPixelArt)
	{
		const FString SpriteId = Definition.Strings.FindRef(GBString::Sprite);
		if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this))
		{
			Library->SetupBillboard(Sprite, FName(*SpriteId), 2.f * HalfHeight);
		}
		SpriteBaseScale = Sprite->GetRelativeScale3D();
		Sprite->SetVisibility(true);
		BodyMesh->SetVisibility(false);
	}
	else
	{
		BodyColor = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Definition.Strings.FindRef(GBString::Color), FLinearColor::Gray);
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(BodyMesh, EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(2.f * Radius, 2.f * Radius, 2.f * HalfHeight), BodyColor);
		BodyMesh->SetVisibility(true);
		Sprite->SetVisibility(false);
	}

	Stats->OnStatsChanged.RemoveAll(this);
	Stats->OnStatsChanged.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::ApplyMovementStats);
	Attributes->OnDeath.RemoveAll(this);
	Attributes->OnDeath.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDeath);
	Attributes->OnDamaged.RemoveAll(this);
	Attributes->OnDamaged.AddUObject(this, &AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDamaged);

	ApplyMovementStats();
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDataReloaded()
{
	if (const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		AttackModeName = Def->AttackMode;
		Stats->SetBaseStats(Def->Stats);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::ApplyMovementStats()
{
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->MaxWalkSpeed = FMath::Max(0.f, Stats->GetStat(GBStat::MoveSpeed, Move->MaxWalkSpeed));
	}
}

AActor* AGBEnemyCharacter::FindTarget() const
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	if (Player && UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(Player) && UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(this, Player))
	{
		return Player;
	}
	return nullptr;
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::IsRanged() const
{
	return AttackModeName.Equals(GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeRanged.ToString(), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::SetState(EGBEnemyState NewState)
{
	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup && NewState != EGBEnemyState::Windup)
	{
		SetTelegraph(false);
	}
	State = NewState;
	StateTime = 0.f;
	if (NewState == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
	{
		SetTelegraph(true);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::SetTelegraph(bool bOn)
{
	if (bPixelArtLook)
	{
		Sprite->SetRelativeScale3D(bOn ? SpriteBaseScale * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::TelegraphSpriteScale : SpriteBaseScale);
	}
	else
	{
		const FLinearColor Color = bOn ? BodyColor + (FLinearColor::White - BodyColor) * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::TelegraphTintBlend : BodyColor;
		GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(BodyMesh, Color);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);

	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead || !EraComponent->IsPresent())
	{
		return;
	}

	StateTime += DeltaSeconds;
	NavRetryTimer = FMath::Max(0.f, NavRetryTimer - DeltaSeconds);

	AActor* Goal = FindTarget();
	Target = Goal;
	if (!Goal)
	{
		if (State != EGBEnemyState::Idle)
		{
			StopMoving();
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Idle);
		}
		bProvoked = false;
		return;
	}

	const float AggroRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AggroRange, 0.f);
	const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);
	const bool bCanAttack = AttackRange > 0.f && Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackDamage, 0.f) > 0.f;
	const float Distance = FVector::Dist(GetActorLocation(), Goal->GetActorLocation());

	switch (State)
	{
	case EGBEnemyState::Idle:
		// AggroRange <= 0: never acts (training dummy), even when hit.
		if (AggroRange > 0.f && (bProvoked || Distance <= AggroRange))
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Chase:
		if (bCanAttack && Distance <= AttackRange && (!IsRanged() || HasLineOfSight(Goal)))
		{
			StopMoving();
			FaceTowards(Goal);
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Windup);
		}
		else
		{
			MoveTowards(Goal, DeltaSeconds);
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Windup:
		FaceTowards(Goal);
		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackWindup, 0.f))
		{
			PerformAttack();
			if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
			{
				SetState(EGBEnemyState::Recover);
			}
		}
		break;

	case EGBEnemyState::Recover:
		FaceTowards(Goal);
		if (StateTime >= Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackInterval, 0.f))
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
		break;

	default:
		break;
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::MoveTowards(AActor* Goal, float DeltaSeconds)
{
	if (!Goal || GetCharacterMovement()->MaxWalkSpeed <= 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	bool bPathing = false;
	AAIController* AI = Cast<AAIController>(GetController());
	if (AI && NavRetryTimer <= 0.f)
	{
		const UNavigationSystemV1* Nav = UNavigationSystemV1::GetCurrent(GetWorld());
		if (Nav && Nav->GetDefaultNavDataInstance() != nullptr)
		{
			const UPathFollowingComponent* PathFollowing = AI->GetPathFollowingComponent();
			if (AI->GetMoveStatus() != EPathFollowingStatus::Idle && PathFollowing && PathFollowing->GetMoveGoal() == Goal)
			{
				bPathing = true;
			}
			else
			{
				// Melee stops a bit inside its reach; ranged keeps closing in until it has line of sight.
				const float Acceptance = IsRanged() ? 0.f : Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f) * 0.5f;
				const EPathFollowingRequestResult::Type Result = AI->MoveToActor(Goal, Acceptance, true, true, false);
				bPathing = Result == EPathFollowingRequestResult::RequestSuccessful;
				if (Result == EPathFollowingRequestResult::Failed)
				{
					NavRetryTimer = GBEnemyCharacterLocal::NavRetryDelay;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	if (!bPathing)
	{
		if (AI && AI->GetMoveStatus() != EPathFollowingStatus::Idle)
		{
			AI->StopMovement();
		}
		const FVector Direction = (Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation()).GetSafeNormal2D();
		AddMovementInput(Direction, 1.f);
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::StopMoving()
{
	if (AController* C = GetController())
	{
		C->StopMovement();
	}
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->StopMovementImmediately();
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::FaceTowards(const AActor* Goal)
{
	if (!Goal)
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector Direction = (Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation()).GetSafeNormal2D();
	if (!Direction.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		SetActorRotation(FRotator(0.f, Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f));
	}
}

FVector AGBEnemyCharacter::GetChestLocation() const
{
	return GetActorLocation() + FVector::UpVector * (GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight() * GBEnemyCharacterLocal::ChestHeightFraction);
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::HasLineOfSight(const AActor* Goal) const
{
	if (!Goal || !GetWorld())
	{
		return false;
	}
	FHitResult Hit;
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBEnemyLineOfSight), false, this);
	if (!GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, GetChestLocation(), Goal->GetActorLocation(), ECC_Visibility, Params))
	{
		return true;
	}
	// Allies in the way do not block: enemy shots ignore the enemy team.
	const AActor* HitActor = Hit.GetActor();
	return HitActor == Goal || (HitActor && !UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(this, HitActor) && UGBCombatLibrary::GetTeam(HitActor) == EGBTeam::Enemy);
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::PerformAttack()
{
	AActor* Goal = Target.Get();
	const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	if (!Goal || !Def || !GetWorld())
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Damage = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackDamage, 0.f);
	const float AttackRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AttackRange, 0.f);

	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = Damage;
	Info.Element = Def->Element;
	Info.Instigator = this;
	Info.Causer = this;
	Info.SourceId = EnemyId;
	Info.OnHitEffect = FName(*Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::OnHitEffect));

	const FName Mode(*Def->AttackMode);
	if (Mode == GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeMelee)
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const float Tolerance = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::MeleeReachTolerance, 1.2f) : 1.2f;
		const FVector ToGoal = Goal->GetActorLocation() - GetActorLocation();
		if (ToGoal.Size() <= AttackRange * Tolerance)
		{
			Info.HitLocation = Goal->GetActorLocation();
			Info.HitDirection = ToGoal.GetSafeNormal();
			UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Goal, Info);
		}
	}
	else if (Mode == GBEnemyCharacterLocal::AttackModeRanged)
	{
		FGBProjectileConfig Config;
		Config.Damage = Info;
		Config.Damage.Causer = nullptr;
		Config.Speed = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileSpeed, Config.Speed);
		Config.Radius = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::ProjectileRadius, Config.Radius);
		Config.MaxRange = Stats->GetStat(GBStat::AggroRange, 0.f) * 1.5f;
		Config.bPixelArt = Def->bPixelArt;
		if (Def->bPixelArt)
		{
			const FString SpriteId = Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::ProjectileSprite);
			Config.SpriteId = FName(SpriteId.IsEmpty() ? GBEnemyCharacterLocal::DefaultEnemyProjectileSprite : *SpriteId);
		}
		else
		{
			Config.Color = UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Def->Strings.FindRef(GBEnemyKey::ProjectileColor),
				GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Def->Element)));
		}

		const FVector Forward = GetActorForwardVector();
		const FVector Origin = GetChestLocation() + Forward * (GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleRadius() + Config.Radius);
		FVector Direction = Goal->GetActorLocation() - Origin;
		if (Direction.IsNearlyZero())
		{
			Direction = Forward;
		}
		AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(GetWorld(), Config, Origin, Direction, this);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: unknown AttackMode '%s'"), *EnemyId.ToString(), *Def->AttackMode);
	}
}

bool AGBEnemyCharacter::IsHeadshotLocation(const FVector& HitLocation) const
{
	const float HalfHeight = GetCapsuleComponent()->GetScaledCapsuleHalfHeight();
	const float FeetZ = GetActorLocation().Z - HalfHeight;
	return UGBCombatLibrary::IsHeadshot(HitLocation.Z, FeetZ, 2.f * HalfHeight, Stats->GetStat(GBStat::HeadFraction, 0.f));
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDamaged(const FGBDamageInfo& Info, float ActualDamage)
{
	if (State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		bProvoked = true;
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandlePresenceChanged()
{
	if (!EraComponent->IsPresent() && State != EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		StopMoving();
		if (State == EGBEnemyState::Windup)
		{
			SetState(EGBEnemyState::Chase);
		}
	}
}

void AGBEnemyCharacter::HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	if (State == EGBEnemyState::Dead)
	{
		return;
	}
	SetState(EGBEnemyState::Dead);

	StopMoving();
	if (UCharacterMovementComponent* Move = GetCharacterMovement())
	{
		Move->DisableMovement();
	}
	DetachFromControllerPendingDestroy();
	SetActorEnableCollision(false);

	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (const FGBEnemyDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		AGBPickup::SpawnDrops(World, Def->Drops, GetActorLocation());
	}

	OnDied.Broadcast(this);

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const float CorpseTime = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBEnemyKey::CorpseTime, 1.5f) : 1.5f;
	// SetLifeSpan(0) would mean "forever"; keep a tiny minimum instead of destroying inside the damage call.
	SetLifeSpan(FMath::Max(CorpseTime, 0.05f));
}

int32 UGBInventoryComponent::GetCount(FName ItemId) const
{
	return Items.FindRef(ItemId);
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::HandleStackEmptied(FName ItemId)
{
	if (QuickUseItem == ItemId)
	{
		QuickUseItem = FindQuickUseFrom(ItemId, 1);
	}
	if (EquippedArmor == ItemId)
	{
		EquippedArmor = NAME_None;
		ApplyArmorModifiers();
	}
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::UseItem(FName ItemId)
{
	if (!HasItem(ItemId))
	{
		return false;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(ItemId) : nullptr;
	if (!Def)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("UseItem: unknown item '%s'."), *ItemId.ToString());
		return false;
	}

	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner());
	if (Player && Player->IsDead())
	{
		return false;
	}

	if (Def->Type == EGBItemType::Consumable)
	{
		UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = UGBStatusEffectComponent::Find(GetOwner());
		if (!Effects)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("UseItem: '%s' has no status effect component to apply '%s'."), *GetNameSafe(GetOwner()), *Def->UseEffect.ToString());
			return false;
		}
		if (!Effects->ApplyEffect(Def->UseEffect, GetOwner()))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("UseItem: item '%s' has an unknown UseEffect '%s'."), *ItemId.ToString(), *Def->UseEffect.ToString());
			return false;
		}
		return RemoveItem(ItemId, 1);
	}

	if (Def->Type == EGBItemType::Throwable)
	{
		// Copy: removing the item broadcasts, and a listener could reload the data.
		const FGBItemDefinition DefCopy = *Def;
		if (!UseThrowable(DefCopy))
		{
			return false;
		}
		return RemoveItem(ItemId, 1);
	}

	return false;
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::UseThrowable(const FGBItemDefinition& Def)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetOwner());
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!Player || !World)
	{
		return false;
	}

	FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	Config.Damage.Amount = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::Damage, 0.f);
	Config.Damage.Element = Def.Element;
	Config.Damage.Instigator = Player;
	Config.Damage.SourceId = Def.Id;
	if (const FString* OnHit = Def.Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
	{
		Config.Damage.OnHitEffect = FName(**OnHit);
	}

	Config.ExplosionRadius = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::Radius, 0.f);
	Config.Speed = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::Speed, Config.Speed);
	Config.FuseTime = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::FuseTime, 0.f);
	Config.GravityScale = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::ProjectileGravity, 1.f);
	Config.Radius = ReadItemStat(Def, GBStat::ProjectileRadius, Config.Radius);
	if (const float* Range = Def.Stats.Find(GBStat::Range))
	{
		Config.MaxRange = *Range;
	}

	// Art rule (GDD 3): the grenade is cyberpunk, so it is 3D.
	Config.bPixelArt = false;
	const FLinearColor ElementTint = GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Def.Element));
	const FString* ColorText = Def.Strings.Find(GBString::Color);
	Config.Color = ColorText ? UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(*ColorText, ElementTint) : ElementTint;

	FRotator ThrowRotation = Player->GetAimDirection().Rotation();
	const float Tilt = ReadItemStat(Def, GBItemStat::ThrowPitch, 8.f);
	ThrowRotation.Pitch = FMath::Clamp(ThrowRotation.Pitch + static_cast<double>(Tilt), -89.0, 89.0);

	return AGBProjectile::SpawnProjectile(World, Config, Player->GetMuzzleLocation(), ThrowRotation.Vector(), Player) != nullptr;
}

FName UGBInventoryComponent::FindQuickUseFrom(FName From, int32 Direction) const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		return NAME_None;
	}

	const TArray<FGBItemDefinition>& Defs = Data->GetItems();
	const int32 Num = Defs.Num();
	if (Num == 0)
	{
		return NAME_None;
	}

	const int32 Step = Direction >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
	int32 Start = Defs.IndexOfByPredicate([From](const FGBItemDefinition& Def) { return Def.Id == From; });
	if (Start == INDEX_NONE)
	{
		// So that the first step lands on the first (or last) item.
		Start = Step > 0 ? Num - 1 : 0;
	}

	for (int32 Offset = 1; Offset <= Num; ++Offset)
	{
		const int32 Index = ((Start + Step * Offset) % Num + Num) % Num;
		const FGBItemDefinition& Def = Defs[Index];
		if (Def.bQuickUse && GetCount(Def.Id) > 0)
		{
			return Def.Id;
		}
	}
	return NAME_None;
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::CycleQuickUse(int32 Direction)
{
	const FName Next = FindQuickUseFrom(QuickUseItem, Direction);
	if (Next != QuickUseItem)
	{
		QuickUseItem = Next;
		OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::UseQuickItem()
{
	if (QuickUseItem.IsNone())
	{
		return false;
	}
	return UseItem(QuickUseItem);
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::EquipArmor(FName ItemId)
{
	if (!ItemId.IsNone())
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(ItemId) : nullptr;
		if (!Def || Def->Type != EGBItemType::Armor)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("EquipArmor: '%s' is not an armor."), *ItemId.ToString());
			return false;
		}
		if (!HasItem(ItemId))
		{
			return false;
		}
	}

	EquippedArmor = ItemId;
	ApplyArmorModifiers();
	OnInventoryChanged.Broadcast();
	return true;
}

void UGBInventoryComponent::ApplyArmorModifiers()
{
	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	if (!Stats)
	{
		return;
	}

	Stats->RemoveModifiers(ArmorModifierSource);
	if (EquippedArmor.IsNone())
	{
		return;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(EquippedArmor) : nullptr;
	if (!Def || Def->Type != EGBItemType::Armor)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Equipped armor '%s' is no longer an armor in the data; unequipping it."), *EquippedArmor.ToString());
		EquippedArmor = NAME_None;
		return;
	}
	Stats->AddModifiers(ArmorModifierSource, Def->Modifiers);
}

const FGBRecipeDefinition* UGBInventoryComponent::FindRecipe(FName ItemA, FName ItemB) const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	for (const FGBRecipeDefinition& Recipe : Data->GetRecipes())
	{
		if ((Recipe.A == ItemA && Recipe.B == ItemB) || (Recipe.A == ItemB && Recipe.B == ItemA))
		{
			return &Recipe;
		}
	}
	return nullptr;
}

bool UGBInventoryComponent::TryCombine(FName ItemA, FName ItemB)
{
	const FGBRecipeDefinition* Found = FindRecipe(ItemA, ItemB);
	if (!Found)
	{
		ShowHudMessage(TEXT("These items cannot be combined"));
		return false;
	}
	// Copy: the steps below broadcast, and the pointer must not outlive this frame's data anyway.
	const FGBRecipeDefinition Recipe = *Found;

	const bool bSameItem = Recipe.A == Recipe.B;
	if (GetCount(Recipe.A) < (bSameItem ? 2 : 1) || !HasItem(Recipe.B))
	{
		return false;
	}

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Recipe.ResultItem.IsNone() && !(Data && Data->FindItem(Recipe.ResultItem)))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Recipe '%s': unknown ResultItem '%s'."), *Recipe.Id.ToString(), *Recipe.ResultItem.ToString());
		return false;
	}
	if (!Recipe.ResultWeapon.IsNone() && !(Data && Data->FindWeapon(Recipe.ResultWeapon)))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Recipe '%s': unknown ResultWeapon '%s'."), *Recipe.Id.ToString(), *Recipe.ResultWeapon.ToString());
		return false;
	}
	UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = GetOwner() ? GetOwner()->FindComponentByClass<UGBEquipmentComponent>() : nullptr;
	if (!Recipe.ResultWeapon.IsNone() && !Equipment)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Recipe '%s': the owner has no equipment to receive '%s'."), *Recipe.Id.ToString(), *Recipe.ResultWeapon.ToString());
		return false;
	}

	const FString NameA = ItemDisplayName(Data ? Data->FindItem(Recipe.A) : nullptr, Recipe.A);
	const FString NameB = ItemDisplayName(Data ? Data->FindItem(Recipe.B) : nullptr, Recipe.B);

	if (Recipe.bConsumeA)
	{
		RemoveItem(Recipe.A, 1);
	}
	if (Recipe.bConsumeB)
	{
		RemoveItem(Recipe.B, 1);
	}

	FString Message;
	if (!Recipe.ResultWeapon.IsNone())
	{
		if (AGBHeldItem* Weapon = Equipment->GiveWeapon(Recipe.ResultWeapon, true, false))
		{
			Weapon->SetElement(Recipe.ResultElement);
			Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Forged: %s (%s)"), *Weapon->GetDisplayName(), *ElementName(Recipe.ResultElement));
		}
	}
	if (!Recipe.ResultItem.IsNone())
	{
		UGBDataSubsystem* DataNow = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const FString ResultName = ItemDisplayName(DataNow ? DataNow->FindItem(Recipe.ResultItem) : nullptr, Recipe.ResultItem);
		if (AddItem(Recipe.ResultItem, 1) > 0 && Message.IsEmpty())
		{
			Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Crafted: %s"), *ResultName);
		}
	}
	if (!Recipe.SetsFlag.IsNone())
	{
		if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
		{
			Progress->SetFlag(Recipe.SetsFlag);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Recipe '%s': no progress subsystem to set '%s'."), *Recipe.Id.ToString(), *Recipe.SetsFlag.ToString());
		}
	}
	if (Message.IsEmpty())
	{
		Message = FString::Printf(TEXT("Combined: %s + %s"), *NameA, *NameB);

// Data definitions loaded from Content/Data/*.json by UGBDataSubsystem.
// Rule: gameplay numbers live here (data), never in code. Field names are the JSON keys.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "GBDataTypes.generated.h"

/**
 * One spell parameter (GDD 7): "every parameter is data and can be tagged as scaled by QTE part 1, part 2, or neither".
 *  - ScaledBy == None, or the QTE is off: the value is Base.
 *  - ScaledBy == Power / Reach and the QTE ran: Lerp(Min, Max, score of that part), score in 0..1.
 */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBScaledParam
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float Base = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float Min = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float Max = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBScaleSource ScaledBy = EGBScaleSource::None;

	/** bUseQTE false = fixed default strength (Base). Power and Reach are clamped to 0..1. */
	float Resolve(float Power, float Reach, bool bUseQTE) const;
};

/** One stat change: skill tree rank, armor, status effect, air passive, weapon enchant... */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBModifierDef
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	/** Stat key: an actor stat ("MoveSpeed"), "Weapon.<Id>.<Stat>", "Weapon.All.<Stat>", "Spell.<Id>.<Param>", "Spell.All.<Param>", "QTE.TimeDilation"... */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Stat = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBModOp Op = EGBModOp::Add;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float Value = 0.f;
};

/** Spells.json. A spell = this data + the actor class that gives it behavior. New spells need no new code if a behavior fits. */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBSpellDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString DisplayName;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString Description;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBElement Element = EGBElement::Arcane;

	/** Class path of the AGBSpellActor that runs the spell, e.g. "/Script/Glyphbound.GBSpell_Projectile". Empty for passives. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString ActorClass;

	/** Air passives (GDD 6): always active once known, never equipped; they only apply PassiveModifiers. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bPassive = false;

	/** The weapon enchant: only castable with a gun in hand, lives in its own fixed slot. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bRequiresGun = false;

	/** Sustained spells (flamethrower, ice shield): they run until Duration ends or the cast input is pressed again. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bSustained = false;

	/** Known from the start of the game (GDD 6 "Starting spells"). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bStartsKnown = false;

	/** Spells with the same group are learned together (the knowledge of an elemental zone), e.g. "Fire". */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName UnlockGroup = NAME_None;

	/** Every tunable number. Keys: see GBStat (ManaCost, Cooldown, Damage, Speed, Range, Radius, Duration...). A behavior may read any key it documents. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, FGBScaledParam> Params;

	/** Non-numeric settings. Keys: see GBString (Sprite, Color, OnHitEffect...). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, FString> Strings;

	/** Only for bPassive spells. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FGBModifierDef> PassiveModifiers;
};

/** Weapons.json: staffs, guns and the elemental sword. */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBWeaponDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString DisplayName;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBHeldSlot Slot = EGBHeldSlot::Gun;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBElement Element = EGBElement::Physical;

	/** Class path of the AGBHeldItem. Empty = default class for the slot (AGBStaff, AGBGun, AGBSword). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString ActorClass;

	/** "Hitscan", "Projectile" or "Melee". */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString FireMode = TEXT("Hitscan");

	/** Hold the trigger to keep firing. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bAutomatic = false;

	/** Keys: see GBStat "Weapon stats". */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, float> Stats;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, FString> Strings;
};

/** StatusEffects.json: slow, burn, heal over time, instant heal, the enchant buffs... */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBStatusEffectDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString DisplayName;

	/** Seconds. 0 = instant (only the Instant* fields apply). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float Duration = 0.f;

	/** Seconds between ticks of HealthPerTick / ManaPerTick. 0 = no ticking. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float TickInterval = 0.f;

	/** Positive heals, negative damages (damage uses Element and the effect's instigator). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float HealthPerTick = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float ManaPerTick = 0.f;

	/** Applied once when the effect starts. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float InstantHealth = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float InstantMana = 0.f;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBElement Element = EGBElement::Physical;

	/** Stat modifiers active while the effect lasts (slow = MoveSpeed x0.5, enchant = Weapon.All.Damage x1.3...). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers;

	/** Applying it again restarts the timer instead of stacking. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bRefreshOnReapply = true;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 MaxStacks = 1;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, FString> Strings;
};

/** Items.json: consumables, throwables, armor, key items, materials (gems, hilt), ammo. */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBItemDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString DisplayName;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString Description;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBItemType Type = EGBItemType::Consumable;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 MaxStack = 9;

	/** Goes in the quick-use slot (grenade / potion / injection share one slot, GDD 9). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bQuickUse = false;

	/** Consumable: status effect applied to the user. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName UseEffect = NAME_None;

	/** Armor: modifiers while equipped. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers;

	/** Throwable: Damage, Radius, Speed, FuseTime, ProjectileGravity. Ammo: SpareMagazines (how many it gives). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, float> Stats;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBElement Element = EGBElement::Physical;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, FString> Strings;
};

/** Items.json "Recipes": hilt + gem = elemental sword, quest item + quest item = final portal (GDD 11). */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBRecipeDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName A = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName B = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bConsumeA = true;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bConsumeB = true;

	/** Any of these may be set. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName ResultItem = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName ResultWeapon = NAME_None;

	/** Element given to ResultWeapon (the sword takes the gem's element). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBElement ResultElement = EGBElement::Physical;

	/** Progress flag set by the recipe (e.g. "FinalPortalOpen"). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName SetsFlag = NAME_None;
};

/** One possible drop (enemies, chests). Exactly one of ItemId / WeaponId / SpellGroup is normally set. */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBDropDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName ItemId = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName WeaponId = NAME_None;

	/** Teaches every spell of that UnlockGroup (the knowledge of an elemental zone). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName SpellGroup = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 Count = 1;

	/** 0..1 */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float Chance = 1.f;
};

/** Enemies.json. */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBEnemyDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString DisplayName;

	/** Class path of the AGBEnemyCharacter. Empty = AGBEnemyCharacter. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString ActorClass;

	/** "Melee" or "Ranged". */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString AttackMode = TEXT("Melee");

	/** Element of its attacks. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBElement Element = EGBElement::Physical;

	/** Art rule (GDD 3): true = pixel-art sprite that faces the camera, false = 3D mesh. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bPixelArt = true;

	/** Keys: GBStat actor stats + enemy stats (+ ProjectileSpeed, ProjectileRadius for ranged). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, float> Stats;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, FString> Strings;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FGBDropDefinition> Drops;
};

USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBEncounterSpawn
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName EnemyId = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 Count = 1;
};

USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBEncounterWave
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FGBEncounterSpawn> Spawns;

	/** Seconds between the previous wave being cleared and this one spawning. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	float DelayBefore = 1.f;
};

/** Encounters.json: waves, sub-bosses and bosses are all encounters (GDD 10). */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBEncounterDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FGBEncounterWave> Waves;

	/** Skill points for clearing it (GDD 5). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 SkillPoints = 1;

	/** Progress flag set when cleared; also what keeps it from running again (enemies do not respawn). Empty = "Encounter.<Id>.Cleared". */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName ClearedFlag = NAME_None;

	/** Extra rewards dropped at the encounter's location when cleared. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FGBDropDefinition> Rewards;
};

/** Skills.json: nodes of the two skill trees (GDD 5). */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBSkillNodeDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	/** "Spell" or "Weapon". */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Tree = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString DisplayName;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FString Description;

	/** Skill points per rank. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 Cost = 1;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 MaxRank = 1;

	/** Node ids that need at least rank 1 first. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FName> Requires;

	/** Applied once per rank owned. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FGBModifierDef> ModifiersPerRank;

	/** Position in the tree screen. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 Column = 0;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 Row = 0;
};

/** Sprites.json: placeholder pixel art as text. Each row is a string; each character is a key of Palette ('.' or ' ' = transparent). */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBSpriteDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName Id = NAME_None;

	/** One-character key -> "#RRGGBB" or "#RRGGBBAA". */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FString, FString> Palette;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FString> Rows;
};

/** Player.json */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBPlayerDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	/** Keys: GBStat actor stats. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, float> Stats;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FName> StartingWeapons;

	/** Extra spells known at start, besides the ones with bStartsKnown. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FName> StartingSpells;

	/** Up to 5 spell ids, in slot order. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FName> StartingLoadout;

	/** Item id -> count. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, int32> StartingItems;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	int32 StartingSkillPoints = 0;

	/** Id of the weapon enchant spell (fixed slot). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	FName EnchantSpell = NAME_None;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	EGBElement StartingEnchantElement = EGBElement::Fire;
};

/** QTE.json (GDD 7). */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBQTEDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	/** false = spells cast at their fixed default strength (every param uses Base). */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	bool bEnabled = true;

	/** Keys: QTE.TimeDilation, QTE.TimeLimit, QTE.SequenceLength, QTE.GestureCount, plus the recognizer thresholds. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, float> Stats;

	/** Gesture names that may be asked for (see EGBGesture). Empty = all. */
	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TArray<FString> Gestures;
};

/** Game.json: global values that belong to no single system (fade times, respawn delay, test arena switches...). */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBGameDefinition
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, float> Values;

	UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category = "Data")
	TMap<FName, FString> Strings;
};

#include "UI/GBPixelSpriteLibrary.h"

#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Engine/Engine.h"
#include "Engine/GameInstance.h"
#include "Engine/Texture2D.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "TextureResource.h"

namespace GBSpriteLocal
{
	const FColor MissingColor(255, 0, 255, 255);
	const FColor TransparentColor(0, 0, 0, 0);

	int32 HexValue(TCHAR C)
	{
		if (C >= TEXT('0') && C <= TEXT('9')) return C - TEXT('0');
		if (C >= TEXT('a') && C <= TEXT('f')) return 10 + C - TEXT('a');
		if (C >= TEXT('A') && C <= TEXT('F')) return 10 + C - TEXT('A');
		return -1;
	}

	/** "#RRGGBB" or "#RRGGBBAA" (the '#' is optional) -> sRGB color. Parsed here, byte-exact, so the art keeps its exact colors. */
	bool ParseHexColor(const FString& InHex, FColor& OutColor)
	{
		FString Hex = InHex.TrimStartAndEnd();
		if (Hex.StartsWith(TEXT("#")))
		{
			Hex.RightChopInline(1);
		}
		if (Hex.Len() != 6 && Hex.Len() != 8)
		{
			return false;
		}
		uint8 Bytes[4] = { 0, 0, 0, 255 };
		for (int32 i = 0; i < Hex.Len() / 2; ++i)
		{
			const int32 Hi = HexValue(Hex[i * 2]);
			const int32 Lo = HexValue(Hex[i * 2 + 1]);
			if (Hi < 0 || Lo < 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			Bytes[i] = (uint8)(Hi * 16 + Lo);
		}
		OutColor = FColor(Bytes[0], Bytes[1], Bytes[2], Bytes[3]);
		return true;
	}
}

UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject)
{
	const UWorld* World = (GEngine && WorldContextObject) ? GEngine->GetWorldFromContextObject(WorldContextObject, EGetWorldErrorMode::ReturnNull) : nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = World ? World->GetGameInstance() : nullptr;
	return GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBPixelSpriteLibrary>() : nullptr;
}

void UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Initialize(FSubsystemCollectionBase& Collection)
{
	Super::Initialize(Collection);

	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = Collection.InitializeDependency<UGBDataSubsystem>())
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.AddUObject(this, &UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::ClearCache);
	}
}

void UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::ClearCache()
{
	Cache.Reset();
}

bool UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::BuildPixels(const FGBSpriteDefinition& Definition, TArray<FColor>& OutPixels, int32& OutWidth, int32& OutHeight)
{
	OutPixels.Reset();
	OutWidth = 0;
	OutHeight = 0;

	if (Definition.Rows.Num() == 0 || Definition.Rows[0].Len() == 0)
	{
		return false;
	}
	const int32 Width = Definition.Rows[0].Len();
	for (const FString& Row : Definition.Rows)
	{
		if (Row.Len() != Width)
		{
			return false;
		}
	}

	TMap<TCHAR, FColor> Colors;
	for (const TPair<FString, FString>& Entry : Definition.Palette)
	{
		if (Entry.Key.Len() == 0)
		{
			continue;
		}
		FColor Color;
		Colors.Add(Entry.Key[0], GBSpriteLocal::ParseHexColor(Entry.Value, Color) ? Color : GBSpriteLocal::MissingColor);
	}

	const int32 Height = Definition.Rows.Num();
	OutPixels.Reserve(Width * Height);
	for (const FString& Row : Definition.Rows)
	{
		for (int32 X = 0; X < Width; ++X)
		{
			const TCHAR C = Row[X];
			if (C == TEXT('.') || C == TEXT(' '))
			{
				OutPixels.Add(GBSpriteLocal::TransparentColor);
			}
			else if (const FColor* Found = Colors.Find(C))
			{
				OutPixels.Add(*Found);
			}
			else
			{
				OutPixels.Add(GBSpriteLocal::MissingColor);
			}
		}
	}
	OutWidth = Width;
	OutHeight = Height;
	return true;
}

UTexture2D* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::CreateTexture(const TArray<FColor>& Pixels, int32 Width, int32 Height, FName DebugName)
{
	if (Width <= 0 || Height <= 0 || Pixels.Num() != Width * Height)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}

	UTexture2D* Texture = UTexture2D::CreateTransient(Width, Height, PF_B8G8R8A8);
	if (!Texture || !Texture->GetPlatformData() || Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips.Num() == 0)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Pixel sprite '%s': could not create a %dx%d texture."), *DebugName.ToString(), Width, Height);
		return nullptr;
	}

	Texture->Filter = TF_Nearest;
	Texture->SRGB = true;
	Texture->NeverStream = true;
	Texture->CompressionSettings = TC_EditorIcon;
	Texture->LODGroup = TEXTUREGROUP_Pixels2D;
	Texture->AddressX = TA_Clamp;
	Texture->AddressY = TA_Clamp;
#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
	Texture->MipGenSettings = TMGS_NoMipmaps;
#endif

	// FColor is laid out B, G, R, A in memory, which is exactly PF_B8G8R8A8; rows go top to bottom.
	FTexture2DMipMap& Mip = Texture->GetPlatformData()->Mips[0];
	void* Data = Mip.BulkData.Lock(LOCK_READ_WRITE);
	FMemory::Memcpy(Data, Pixels.GetData(), Pixels.Num() * sizeof(FColor));
	Mip.BulkData.Unlock();
	Texture->UpdateResource();
	return Texture;
}

UTexture2D* UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::GetSprite(FName SpriteId)
{
	if (TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>* Cached = Cache.Find(SpriteId))
	{
		if (*Cached)
		{
			return *Cached;
		}
	}

	if (!MissingTexture)
	{
		constexpr int32 Size = 8;
		TArray<FColor> Checker;
		Checker.Reserve(Size * Size);
		for (int32 Y = 0; Y < Size; ++Y)
		{
			for (int32 X = 0; X < Size; ++X)
			{
				Checker.Add(((X / 2 + Y / 2) % 2) ? FColor::Black : GBSpriteLocal::MissingColor);
			}
		}
		MissingTexture = CreateTexture(Checker, Size, Size, TEXT("Missing"));
	}

	UTexture2D* Result = nullptr;
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = GetGameInstance();
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>() : nullptr;
	const FGBSpriteDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSprite(SpriteId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Pixel sprite '%s' is not in Sprites.json; using the magenta checker."), *SpriteId.ToString());
	}
	else
	{
		TArray<FColor> Pixels;
		int32 Width = 0;
		int32 Height = 0;
		if (BuildPixels(*Definition, Pixels, Width, Height))
		{
			Result = CreateTexture(Pixels, Width, Height, SpriteId);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Pixel sprite '%s' has no rows or rows of different lengths; using the magenta checker."), *SpriteId.ToString());
		}
	}

	if (!Result)
	{
		Result = MissingTexture;
	}
	// Missing sprites are cached too, so the warning is logged once per id (until the next data reload).
	Cache.Add(SpriteId, Result);
	return Result;
}

FIntPoint UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::GetSpriteSize(FName SpriteId) const
{
	const UGameInstance* GameInstance = GetGameInstance();
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = GameInstance ? GameInstance->GetSubsystem<UGBDataSubsystem>() : nullptr;
	const FGBSpriteDefinition* Definition = Data ? Data->FindSprite(SpriteId) : nullptr;
	if (!Definition || Definition->Rows.Num() == 0)
	{
		return FIntPoint::ZeroValue;
	}
	return FIntPoint(Definition->Rows[0].Len(), Definition->Rows.Num());
}

void UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::SetupBillboard(UBillboardComponent* Billboard, FName SpriteId, float WorldHeight)
{
	if (!Billboard)
	{
		return;
	}
	UTexture2D* Texture = GetSprite(SpriteId);
	if (!Texture)
	{
		return;
	}

	// FSpriteSceneProxy: half extents = ComponentMaxAxisScale * SpriteScale * 0.25 * TextureSize (FBatchedElements
	// builds the quad from Position +- SizeX / SizeY), so the full height is 0.5 * Scale * TextureHeight.
	// In editor builds the proxy also multiplies by the owner's SpriteScale and UBillboardComponent::EditorScale.
	const float TextureHeight = (float)FMath::Max(1, Texture->GetSizeY());
	float Scale = WorldHeight > 0.f ? 2.f * WorldHeight / TextureHeight : 1.f;
#if WITH_EDITOR
	if (const AActor* Owner = Billboard->GetOwner())
	{
		if (Owner->SpriteScale > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
		{
			Scale /= Owner->SpriteScale;
		}
	}
#endif
#if WITH_EDITORONLY_DATA
	if (UBillboardComponent::EditorScale > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
	{
		Scale /= UBillboardComponent::EditorScale;
	}
#endif

	// Billboards use absolute scale by default, so the relative scale is the world scale.
	Billboard->SetRelativeScale3D(FVector(Scale));
	Billboard->bIsScreenSizeScaled = false;
	Billboard->SetSprite(Texture);
	Billboard->SetHiddenInGame(false);
	Billboard->SetVisibility(true);
	// The proxy reads the scale only when it is created: rebuild it.
	Billboard->MarkRenderStateDirty();
}

#include "Components/SphereComponent.h"

namespace GBPickupLocal
{
	// Placeholder look sizes and scatter offsets (cm): visuals only.
	const FVector ItemSpriteSize(30.f, 30.f, 40.f);
	const FVector ItemBoxSize(25.f, 25.f, 25.f);
	const FVector GunBoxSize(45.f, 12.f, 20.f);
	const FVector StaffSpriteSize(20.f, 20.f, 100.f);
	const FVector BookSpriteSize(40.f, 40.f, 45.f);
	const FLinearColor DefaultGunColor(0.08f, 0.08f, 0.1f);
	const FLinearColor DefaultItemColor(0.6f, 0.6f, 0.6f);
	constexpr float ScatterRadius = 90.f;
	constexpr float GroundTraceUp = 100.f;
	constexpr float GroundTraceDown = 2000.f;
	const FName BookSpriteId(TEXT("book"));
	const FName StaffSpriteId(TEXT("staff"));

	FName SpriteFromStrings(const TMap<FName, FString>& Strings, FName Fallback)
	{
		const FString* Found = Strings.Find(GBString::Sprite);
		return (Found && !Found->IsEmpty()) ? FName(**Found) : Fallback;
	}

	void ShowHudMessage(const UObject* WorldContextObject, const FString& Text)
	{
		if (AGBHUD* Hud = AGBHUD::Get(WorldContextObject))
		{
			Hud->ShowMessage(Text);
		}
	}
}

AGBPickup::AGBPickup()
{
}

AGBPickup* AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(UWorld* World, const FGBDropDefinition& InDrop, const FVector& Location, FName InFlagOnPickup)
{
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (!IsDropValid(World, InDrop))
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("SpawnPickup: empty or unknown drop (item %s, weapon %s, spell group %s)."),
			*InDrop.ItemId.ToString(), *InDrop.WeaponId.ToString(), *InDrop.SpellGroup.ToString());
		return nullptr;
	}

	// Snap to the ground below (or slightly above) the requested point.
	FVector SpawnLocation = Location;
	FHitResult Hit;
	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBPickupGround), false);
	if (World->LineTraceSingleByObjectType(Hit, Location + FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBPickupLocal::GroundTraceUp), Location - FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBPickupLocal::GroundTraceDown),
		FCollisionObjectQueryParams(ECC_WorldStatic), Params))
	{
		SpawnLocation = Hit.ImpactPoint;
	}

	const FTransform Transform(FRotator::ZeroRotator, SpawnLocation);
	AGBPickup* Pickup = World->SpawnActorDeferred<AGBPickup>(AGBPickup::StaticClass(), Transform, nullptr, nullptr,
		ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn);
	if (!Pickup)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	Pickup->InitPickup(InDrop, InFlagOnPickup);
	Pickup->FinishSpawning(Transform);
	return Pickup;
}

int32 AGBPickup::SpawnDrops(UWorld* World, TConstArrayView<FGBDropDefinition> Drops, const FVector& Location)
{
	int32 Spawned = 0;
	for (const FGBDropDefinition& Drop : Drops)
	{
		if (Drop.Chance < 1.f && FMath::FRand() > Drop.Chance)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FVector2D Offset = FMath::RandPointInCircle(GBPickupLocal::ScatterRadius);
		if (SpawnPickup(World, Drop, Location + FVector(Offset.X, Offset.Y, 0.f)))
		{
			++Spawned;
		}
	}
	return Spawned;
}

bool AGBPickup::IsDropValid(const UObject* WorldContextObject, const FGBDropDefinition& InDrop)
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(WorldContextObject);
	if (!Data)
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (!InDrop.ItemId.IsNone())
	{
		return InDrop.Count > 0 && Data->FindItem(InDrop.ItemId) != nullptr;
	}
	if (!InDrop.WeaponId.IsNone())
	{
		return Data->FindWeapon(InDrop.WeaponId) != nullptr;
	}
	if (!InDrop.SpellGroup.IsNone())
	{
		for (const FGBSpellDefinition& Spell : Data->GetSpells())
		{
			if (Spell.UnlockGroup == InDrop.SpellGroup)
			{
				return true;
			}
		}
	}
	return false;
}

FString AGBPickup::GetDropDisplayName(const UObject* WorldContextObject, const FGBDropDefinition& InDrop, bool bWithCount)
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(WorldContextObject);
	if (!InDrop.ItemId.IsNone())
	{
		const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(InDrop.ItemId) : nullptr;
		const FString Name = (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : InDrop.ItemId.ToString();
		return (bWithCount && InDrop.Count > 1) ? FString::Printf(TEXT("%dx %s"), InDrop.Count, *Name) : Name;
	}
	if (!InDrop.WeaponId.IsNone())
	{
		const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(InDrop.WeaponId) : nullptr;
		return (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : InDrop.WeaponId.ToString();
	}
	if (!InDrop.SpellGroup.IsNone())
	{
		return FString::Printf(TEXT("%s spellbook"), *InDrop.SpellGroup.ToString());
	}
	return TEXT("nothing");
}

bool AGBPickup::GiveDrop(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player, FGBDropDefinition& InOutDrop, bool& bOutAllTaken)
{
	bOutAllTaken = false;
	if (!Player)
	{
		return false;
	}

	if (!InOutDrop.ItemId.IsNone())
	{
		UGBInventoryComponent* Inventory = Player->GetInventory();
		if (!Inventory)
		{
			return false;
		}
		const int32 Wanted = FMath::Max(1, InOutDrop.Count);
		const int32 Taken = Inventory->AddItem(InOutDrop.ItemId, Wanted);
		if (Taken <= 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		InOutDrop.Count = Wanted - Taken;
		bOutAllTaken = InOutDrop.Count <= 0;
		return true;
	}

	if (!InOutDrop.WeaponId.IsNone())
	{
		UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player->GetEquipment();
		if (Equipment && Equipment->GiveWeapon(InOutDrop.WeaponId, true, true))
		{
			bOutAllTaken = true;
			return true;
		}
		return false;
	}

	if (!InOutDrop.SpellGroup.IsNone())
	{
		UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player->GetSpellbook();
		if (!Spellbook)
		{
			return false;
		}
		// Knowledge already owned is still "taken": the book is used up either way.
		Spellbook->LearnGroup(InOutDrop.SpellGroup);
		bOutAllTaken = true;
		return true;
	}

	return false;
}

void AGBPickup::InitPickup(const FGBDropDefinition& InDrop, FName InFlagOnPickup)
{
	Drop = InDrop;
	FlagOnPickup = InFlagOnPickup;
	if (HasActorBegunPlay())
	{
		ApplyLook();
	}
}

void AGBPickup::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	InteractVolume->OnComponentBeginOverlap.AddDynamic(this, &AGBPickup::HandleOverlap);
	ApplyLook();
}

bool AGBPickup::IsWalkOver() const
{
	return !Drop.ItemId.IsNone() && Drop.WeaponId.IsNone() && Drop.SpellGroup.IsNone();
}

void AGBPickup::ApplyLook()
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);

	if (!Drop.ItemId.IsNone())
	{
		const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(Drop.ItemId) : nullptr;
		const FName SpriteId = Def ? GBPickupLocal::SpriteFromStrings(Def->Strings, NAME_None) : NAME_None;
		const bool bForce3D = Def && Def->Strings.FindRef(GBPickupString::PickupLook).Equals(TEXT("3D"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase);
		if (!SpriteId.IsNone() && !bForce3D)
		{
			// Medieval / magic items (potions, gems) are pixel art.
			SetPlaceholderLook(true, SpriteId, FLinearColor::White, GBPickupLocal::ItemSpriteSize);
		}
		else
		{
			// Cyberpunk items (grenade, injection, ammo) are 3D.
			const FLinearColor Fallback = Def ? GBPlaceholder::ElementColor(static_cast<uint8>(Def->Element)) : GBPickupLocal::DefaultItemColor;
			const FLinearColor Color = Def ? UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::Color), Fallback) : Fallback;
			SetPlaceholderLook(false, NAME_None, Color, GBPickupLocal::ItemBoxSize);
		}
		return;
	}

	if (!Drop.WeaponId.IsNone())
	{
		const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(Drop.WeaponId) : nullptr;
		if (Def && Def->Slot != EGBHeldSlot::Gun)
		{
			SetPlaceholderLook(true, GBPickupLocal::SpriteFromStrings(Def->Strings, GBPickupLocal::StaffSpriteId), FLinearColor::White, GBPickupLocal::StaffSpriteSize);
		}
		else
		{
			const FLinearColor Color = Def ? UGBDataSubsystem::ParseColor(Def->Strings.FindRef(GBString::Color), GBPickupLocal::DefaultGunColor) : GBPickupLocal::DefaultGunColor;
			SetPlaceholderLook(false, NAME_None, Color, GBPickupLocal::GunBoxSize);
		}
		return;
	}

	if (!Drop.SpellGroup.IsNone())
	{
		SetPlaceholderLook(true, GBPickupLocal::BookSpriteId, FLinearColor::White, GBPickupLocal::BookSpriteSize);
	}
}

bool AGBPickup::CanInteract(const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	return Super::CanInteract(Player) && !IsActorBeingDestroyed() && Player && !Player->IsDead();
}

void AGBPickup::Interact(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (CanInteract(Player))
	{
		TryPickUp(Player);
	}
}

FString AGBPickup::GetInteractPrompt(const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	return FString::Printf(TEXT("Pick up %s"), *GetDropDisplayName(this, Drop, true));
}

void AGBPickup::HandleOverlap(UPrimitiveComponent* OverlappedComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp, int32 OtherBodyIndex, bool bFromSweep, const FHitResult& SweepResult)
{
	if (!IsWalkOver())
	{
		return;
	}
	// Only the capsule counts, so a player with several colliding components triggers one pickup.
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(OtherActor);
	if (Player && OtherComp == Player->GetRootComponent() && CanInteract(Player))
	{
		TryPickUp(Player);
	}
}

bool AGBPickup::GiveTo(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	bool bAllTaken = false;
	return GiveDrop(Player, Drop, bAllTaken);
}

void AGBPickup::TryPickUp(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!Player)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FGBDropDefinition Before = Drop;
	bool bAllTaken = false;
	if (!GiveDrop(Player, Drop, bAllTaken))
	{
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const FGBItemDefinition* Item = (Data && !Drop.ItemId.IsNone()) ? Data->FindItem(Drop.ItemId) : nullptr;
		const UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player->GetEquipment();
		if (Item && Item->Type == EGBItemType::Ammo && Equipment && !Equipment->HasWeaponInSlot(EGBHeldSlot::Gun))
		{
			GBPickupLocal::ShowHudMessage(this, FString::Printf(TEXT("No gun for %s"), *GetDropDisplayName(this, Before)));
		}
		else
		{
			GBPickupLocal::ShowHudMessage(this, FString::Printf(TEXT("Cannot carry more %s"), *GetDropDisplayName(this, Before)));
		}
		return;
	}

	if (!FlagOnPickup.IsNone())
	{
		if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
		{
			Progress->SetFlag(FlagOnPickup);
		}
	}

	FGBDropDefinition TakenPart = Before;
	if (!Before.ItemId.IsNone())
	{
		TakenPart.Count = FMath::Max(1, Before.Count) - FMath::Max(0, Drop.Count);
	}
	GBPickupLocal::ShowHudMessage(this, FString::Printf(TEXT("Picked up %s"), *GetDropDisplayName(this, TakenPart, true)));

	if (bAllTaken)
	{
		Destroy();
	}
}

#include "Player/GBPlayerController.h"

#include "Player/GBInputSet.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Core/GBGameMode.h"
#include "UI/GBHUD.h"

#include "EnhancedInputComponent.h"
#include "EnhancedInputSubsystems.h"
#include "InputActionValue.h"
#include "Engine/LocalPlayer.h"

namespace
{
	// The menu context sits above the gameplay one, so during the death screen (both may be active) menu keys win.
	constexpr int32 GameplayContextPriority = 0;
	constexpr int32 MenuContextPriority = 1;
	constexpr float MenuAxisStepThreshold = 0.1f;

	bool IsPausingMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
	{
		return Menu == EGBMenu::Pause || Menu == EGBMenu::Inventory || Menu == EGBMenu::SkillTrees;
	}
}

AGBPlayerController::AGBPlayerController()
{
	// Menus are driven by keyboard / gamepad only.
	bShowMouseCursor = false;
}

void AGBPlayerController::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	if (IsLocalController())
	{
		SetInputMode(FInputModeGameOnly());
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::SetupInput(UGBInputSet* InInputSet)
{
	if (!InInputSet)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerController: SetupInput called without an input set."));
		return;
	}
	const bool bNewSet = InputSet != InInputSet;
	InputSet = InInputSet;

	if (UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem* Subsystem = ULocalPlayer::GetSubsystem<UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem>(GetLocalPlayer()))
	{
		if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
		{
			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext, GameplayContextPriority);
		}
		else
		{
			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext, MenuContextPriority);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerController: no Enhanced Input subsystem, input will not work."));
	}

	UEnhancedInputComponent* EIC = Cast<UEnhancedInputComponent>(InputComponent);
	if (!EIC)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("GBPlayerController: the controller input component is not an UEnhancedInputComponent."));
		return;
	}
	if (!bNewSet && EIC->GetActionEventBindings().Num() > 0)
	{
		return; // already bound to this set
	}
	EIC->ClearActionBindings();

	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->Inventory, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputInventory);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->SkillTrees, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputSkillTrees);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->Pause, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputPause);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuNavigate, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuNavigate);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuConfirm, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuConfirm);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuBack, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuBack);
	EIC->BindAction(InputSet->MenuTab, ETriggerEvent::Started, this, &ThisClass::InputMenuTab);
}

void AGBPlayerController::SetMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	if (Menu == OpenMenu)
	{
		return;
	}
	const EGBMenu OldMenu = OpenMenu;
	AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD());

	if (OldMenu != EGBMenu::None && HUD)
	{
		HUD->OnMenuClosed(OldMenu);
	}
	OpenMenu = Menu;

	UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem* Subsystem = ULocalPlayer::GetSubsystem<UEnhancedInputLocalPlayerSubsystem>(GetLocalPlayer());
	if (Menu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		SetPause(false);
		if (Subsystem && InputSet)
		{
			Subsystem->RemoveMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext);
			Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext, GameplayContextPriority);
		}
		return;
	}

	if (OldMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		if (AGBPlayerCharacter* PlayerCharacter = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(GetPawn()))
		{
			PlayerCharacter->ReleaseHeldInputs();
		}
	}
	if (Subsystem && InputSet)
	{
		// The death screen also drops the gameplay keys: nothing to do in the world while dead.
		Subsystem->RemoveMappingContext(InputSet->GameplayContext);
		Subsystem->AddMappingContext(InputSet->MenuContext, MenuContextPriority);
	}
	SetPause(IsPausingMenu(Menu));

	if (HUD)
	{
		HUD->OnMenuOpened(Menu);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::ToggleMenu(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	SetMenu(OpenMenu == Menu ? EGBMenu::None : Menu);
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputInventory()
{
	if (OpenMenu != EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		ToggleMenu(EGBMenu::Inventory);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputSkillTrees()
{
	if (OpenMenu != EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		ToggleMenu(EGBMenu::SkillTrees);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputPause()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	SetMenu(OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None ? EGBMenu::Pause : EGBMenu::None);
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuNavigate(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		return;
	}
	// Value: x right, y up. One step per press along the dominant axis; the HUD wants y down.
	const FVector2D Axis = Value.Get<FVector2D>();
	if (Axis.IsNearlyZero())
	{
		return;
	}
	FIntPoint Step(0, 0);
	if (FMath::Abs(Axis.X) >= FMath::Abs(Axis.Y))
	{
		Step.X = Axis.X > 0.f ? 1 : -1;
	}
	else
	{
		Step.Y = Axis.Y > 0.f ? -1 : 1;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuNavigate(Step);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuConfirm()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		if (AGBGameMode* GameMode = AGBGameMode::Get(this))
		{
			GameMode->RespawnPlayer();
		}
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuConfirm();
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuBack()
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None || OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD());
	if (!HUD || !HUD->MenuBack())
	{
		SetMenu(EGBMenu::None);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerController::InputMenuTab(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (OpenMenu == EGBMenu::None || OpenMenu == EGBMenu::Death)
	{
		return;
	}
	const float Axis = Value.Get<float>();
	if (FMath::Abs(Axis) < MenuAxisStepThreshold)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AGBHUD* HUD = Cast<AGBHUD>(GetHUD()))
	{
		HUD->MenuTab(Axis > 0.f ? 1 : -1);
	}
}
#include "Progression/GBSkillTreeComponent.h"

#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"
#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

UGBSkillTreeComponent::UGBSkillTreeComponent()
{
	PrimaryComponentTick.bCanEverTick = false;
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		DataReloadedHandle = Data->OnDataReloaded.AddUObject(this, &UGBSkillTreeComponent::HandleDataReloaded);
	}
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Data->OnDataReloaded.Remove(DataReloadedHandle);
	}
	DataReloadedHandle.Reset();

	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::InitializeFromData()
{
	RemoveAllModifiers();
	Ranks.Reset();

	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Data)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Skill tree: no data subsystem, starting with 0 points."));
	}
	Points = Data ? FMath::Max(0, Data->GetPlayer().StartingSkillPoints) : 0;
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::AddPoints(int32 Amount)
{
	if (Amount == 0)
	{
		return;
	}
	Points = FMath::Max(0, Points + Amount);
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
}

int32 UGBSkillTreeComponent::GetRank(FName NodeId) const
{
	return Ranks.FindRef(NodeId);
}

EGBSkillUnlockResult UGBSkillTreeComponent::CanUnlock(FName NodeId) const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Node = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr;
	if (!Node)
	{
		return EGBSkillUnlockResult::UnknownNode;
	}
	return EvaluateUnlock(*Node, Ranks, Points);
}

EGBSkillUnlockResult UGBSkillTreeComponent::Unlock(FName NodeId)
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Node = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr;
	if (!Node)
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Unlock: unknown skill node '%s'."), *NodeId.ToString());
		return EGBSkillUnlockResult::UnknownNode;
	}

	const EGBSkillUnlockResult Result = EvaluateUnlock(*Node, Ranks, Points);
	if (Result != EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok)
	{
		return Result;
	}

	Points -= FMath::Max(0, Node->Cost);
	Ranks.FindOrAdd(NodeId) += 1;
	ApplyNodeModifiers(NodeId);
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
	return EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok;
}

EGBSkillUnlockResult UGBSkillTreeComponent::EvaluateUnlock(const FGBSkillNodeDefinition& Node, const TMap<FName, int32>& InRanks, int32 AvailablePoints)
{
	if (InRanks.FindRef(Node.Id) >= Node.MaxRank)
	{
		return EGBSkillUnlockResult::MaxRank;
	}
	for (const FName& Required : Node.Requires)
	{
		if (!Required.IsNone() && InRanks.FindRef(Required) <= 0)
		{
			return EGBSkillUnlockResult::MissingRequirement;
		}
	}
	if (AvailablePoints < Node.Cost)
	{
		return EGBSkillUnlockResult::NotEnoughPoints;
	}
	return EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok;
}

FName UGBSkillTreeComponent::ModifierSource(FName NodeId)
{
	return FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Skill.%s"), *NodeId.ToString()));
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::ApplyNodeModifiers(FName NodeId)
{
	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	if (!Stats)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FName Source = ModifierSource(NodeId);
	const int32 Rank = GetRank(NodeId);
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Node = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr;
	if (!Node || Rank <= 0)
	{
		if (!Node && Rank > 0)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Skill node '%s' is owned but missing from Skills.json; its modifiers are off."), *NodeId.ToString());
		}
		Stats->RemoveModifiers(Source);
		return;
	}

	// ValueScale = rank: Add modifiers count once per rank, Multiply modifiers stack linearly (not compounding).
	Stats->AddModifiers(Source, Node->ModifiersPerRank, static_cast<float>(Rank));
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::RemoveAllModifiers()
{
	UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
	if (!Stats)
	{
		return;
	}
	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Pair : Ranks)
	{
		Stats->RemoveModifiers(ModifierSource(Pair.Key));
	}
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::ReapplyAllModifiers()
{
	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Pair : Ranks)
	{
		ApplyNodeModifiers(Pair.Key);
	}
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.SkillPoints = Points;
	Snapshot.SkillRanks = Ranks;
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
{
	RemoveAllModifiers();

	Points = FMath::Max(0, Snapshot.SkillPoints);
	Ranks.Reset();
	for (const TPair<FName, int32>& Pair : Snapshot.SkillRanks)
	{
		if (Pair.Value > 0)
		{
			Ranks.Add(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
		}
	}

	ReapplyAllModifiers();
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
}

void UGBSkillTreeComponent::HandleDataReloaded()
{
	ReapplyAllModifiers();
	OnSkillsChanged.Broadcast();
}

namespace GBChestLocal
{
	// Placeholder look (cm), visuals only. The chest faces +X.
	const FVector BodySize(110.f, 70.f, 60.f);
	const FVector LidSize(114.f, 74.f, 14.f);
	const FVector LidClosedLocation(0.f, 0.f, 67.f);
	const FVector LidOpenLocation(-42.f, 0.f, 100.f);
	const FRotator LidOpenRotation(75.f, 0.f, 0.f);
	const FLinearColor BodyColor(0.32f, 0.18f, 0.07f);
	const FLinearColor LidColor(0.55f, 0.4f, 0.12f);
	const FLinearColor OpenBodyColor(0.16f, 0.09f, 0.04f);
	const FLinearColor OpenLidColor(0.3f, 0.22f, 0.08f);
	// Where things the player could not carry are left: in front of the chest, spread sideways.
	constexpr float LeftoverForward = 120.f;
	constexpr float LeftoverSpacing = 60.f;
	constexpr float ChestInteractRadius = 120.f;
}

AGBChest::AGBChest()
{
	bSolid = true;

	Lid = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Lid"));
	Lid->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Lid->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
	Lid->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
	Lid->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Lid->SetRelativeLocation(GBChestLocal::LidClosedLocation);

	// The body blocks the player, so the volume must reach past it.
	InteractVolume->InitSphereRadius(GBChestLocal::ChestInteractRadius);
}

void AGBChest::Configure(TConstArrayView<FGBDropDefinition> InContents, FName InOpenedFlag)
{
	Contents.Reset();
	Contents.Append(InContents.GetData(), InContents.Num());
	OpenedFlag = InOpenedFlag;
	if (HasActorBegunPlay())
	{
		const UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
		SetOpenLook(Progress && Progress->HasFlag(GetOpenedFlag()));
	}
}

void AGBChest::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	SetPlaceholderLook(false, NAME_None, GBChestLocal::BodyColor, GBChestLocal::BodySize);
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Lid, EGBShape::Cube, GBChestLocal::LidSize, GBChestLocal::LidColor, false);

	const UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this);
	SetOpenLook(Progress && Progress->HasFlag(GetOpenedFlag()));
}

FName AGBChest::GetOpenedFlag() const
{
	return OpenedFlag.IsNone() ? FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Chest.%s"), *GetName())) : OpenedFlag;
}

void AGBChest::SetOpenLook(bool bInOpen)
{
	bOpen = bInOpen;
	Lid->SetRelativeLocationAndRotation(bInOpen ? GBChestLocal::LidOpenLocation : GBChestLocal::LidClosedLocation, bInOpen ? GBChestLocal::LidOpenRotation : FRotator::ZeroRotator);
	GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(Mesh, bInOpen ? GBChestLocal::OpenBodyColor : GBChestLocal::BodyColor);
	GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(Lid, bInOpen ? GBChestLocal::OpenLidColor : GBChestLocal::LidColor);
	InteractVolume->SetCollisionEnabled(bInOpen ? ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision : ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
}

bool AGBChest::CanInteract(const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	return Super::CanInteract(Player) && !bOpen && Player && !Player->IsDead();
}

FString AGBChest::GetInteractPrompt(const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	return TEXT("Open chest");
}

void AGBChest::Interact(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!CanInteract(Player))
	{
		return;
	}

	TArray<FString> Found;
	int32 Leftovers = 0;
	for (const FGBDropDefinition& Entry : Contents)
	{
		if (Entry.Chance < 1.f && FMath::FRand() > Entry.Chance)
		{
			continue;
		}
		if (!AGBPickup::IsDropValid(this, Entry))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: skipping empty or unknown content (item %s, weapon %s, spell group %s)."), *GetName(),
				*Entry.ItemId.ToString(), *Entry.WeaponId.ToString(), *Entry.SpellGroup.ToString());
			continue;
		}

		FGBDropDefinition Remaining = Entry;
		bool bAllTaken = false;
		if (AGBPickup::GiveDrop(Player, Remaining, bAllTaken))
		{
			FGBDropDefinition TakenPart = Entry;
			if (!Entry.ItemId.IsNone())
			{
				TakenPart.Count = FMath::Max(1, Entry.Count) - FMath::Max(0, Remaining.Count);
			}
			Found.Add(AGBPickup::GetDropDisplayName(this, TakenPart, true));
		}
		if (!bAllTaken)
		{
			const float Side = (Leftovers % 2 == 0 ? 1.f : -1.f) * GBChestLocal::LeftoverSpacing * ((Leftovers + 1) / 2);
			const FVector Location = GetActorLocation() + GetActorForwardVector() * GBChestLocal::LeftoverForward + GetActorRightVector() * Side;
			AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(GetWorld(), Remaining, Location);
			++Leftovers;
		}
	}

	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Progress->SetFlag(GetOpenedFlag());
	}
	SetOpenLook(true);

	if (AGBHUD* Hud = AGBHUD::Get(this))
	{
		Hud->ShowMessage(Found.Num() > 0 ? FString::Printf(TEXT("Found: %s"), *FString::Join(Found, TEXT(", "))) : FString(TEXT("The chest is empty")));
	}
}
#include "World/GBCheckpointVolume.h"

#include "Components/BoxComponent.h"
#include "Engine/World.h"
#include "TimerManager.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "UI/GBHUD.h"
#include "World/GBProgressSubsystem.h"

AGBCheckpointVolume::AGBCheckpointVolume()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = false;

	Box = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBoxComponent>(TEXT("Box"));
	RootComponent = Box;
	Box->InitBoxExtent(FVector(300.f, 300.f, 200.f));
	Box->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
	Box->SetCollisionObjectType(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	Box->SetCollisionResponseToAllChannels(ECR_Ignore);
	Box->SetCollisionResponseToChannel(ECC_Pawn, ECR_Overlap);
	Box->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
	Box->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);
	Box->SetHiddenInGame(true);
}

void AGBCheckpointVolume::SetExtent(const FVector& Extent)
{
	Box->SetBoxExtent(Extent, true);
}

void AGBCheckpointVolume::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();
	Box->OnComponentBeginOverlap.AddDynamic(this, &AGBCheckpointVolume::HandleBeginOverlap);
	Box->OnComponentEndOverlap.AddDynamic(this, &AGBCheckpointVolume::HandleEndOverlap);
	GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimerForNextTick(this, &AGBCheckpointVolume::CheckInitialOverlap);
}

void AGBCheckpointVolume::HandleBeginOverlap(UPrimitiveComponent* OverlappedComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp, int32 OtherBodyIndex, bool bFromSweep, const FHitResult& SweepResult)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(OtherActor);
	if (Player && OtherComp == Player->GetRootComponent())
	{
		PlayerEntered(Player);
	}
}

void AGBCheckpointVolume::HandleEndOverlap(UPrimitiveComponent* OverlappedComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp, int32 OtherBodyIndex)
{
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = Cast<AGBPlayerCharacter>(OtherActor);
	if (Player && OtherComp == Player->GetRootComponent())
	{
		bPlayerInside = false;
	}
}

void AGBCheckpointVolume::CheckInitialOverlap()
{
	if (bPlayerInside)
	{
		return;
	}
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = AGBPlayerCharacter::Get(this);
	if (Player && Player->GetRootComponent() && Box->IsOverlappingComponent(Cast<UPrimitiveComponent>(Player->GetRootComponent())))
	{
		PlayerEntered(Player);
	}
}

void AGBCheckpointVolume::PlayerEntered(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (bPlayerInside || !Player || Player->IsDead())
	{
		return;
	}
	bPlayerInside = true;
	SaveFor(Player);
}

void AGBCheckpointVolume::SaveFor(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!Player)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (!Player->HasActorBegunPlay())
	{
		// Its components are not initialized yet: a snapshot now would be empty.
		TWeakObjectPtr<AGBPlayerCharacter> WeakPlayer(Player);
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimerForNextTick(FTimerDelegate::CreateWeakLambda(this, [this, WeakPlayer]()
		{
			SaveFor(WeakPlayer.Get());
		}));
		return;
	}

	if (UGBProgressSubsystem* Progress = UGBProgressSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Progress->SaveCheckpoint(Player);
	}
	if (AGBHUD* Hud = AGBHUD::Get(this))
	{
		Hud->ShowMessage(TEXT("Checkpoint"));
	}
}
