// LAYOUT (level design, not gameplay tuning). Units: cm, degrees, lux / candelas. Floor top is Z = 0.
// The player starts on the west side looking east (+X) at the central portal.
//   North (+Y): movement course, west to east: steps that need the double jump, then a gap that needs the dash.
//   North-east: raised platform with a chest, reached only by a FUTURE-only ramp that appears once the PAST
//               encounter Test_PastWave is cleared (cause in the past, effect in the future).
//   South-east: era-gated room: its doorway is blocked by a PAST-only wall (open in the future). Shotgun inside.
//   East:       encounter Test_PastWave (past only).   South-west: encounter Test_FutureWave (future only).
// Assumed player numbers (Player.json may change them): single jump ~100 cm high, double jump ~250 cm, so steps of
// 100 are single jumps, steps of 200 need the double jump, and the 600 cm platform needs the ramp.
// =====================================================================================================================

		FVector(1100.f, 1000.f, 50.f), FVector(1100.f, 1110.f, 50.f), FVector(-300.f, 1500.f, 50.f) };
	const FVector Containers[] = { FVector(-1150.f, -1000.f, 65.f), FVector(1100.f, 1050.f, 65.f), FVector(-300.f, 1500.f, 65.f) };
	const FVector LowWallCenter(-500.f, -1500.f, 75.f);
	const FVector LowWallSize(600.f, 120.f, 150.f);
	const FVector LowWall2Center(700.f, 1600.f, 75.f);
	const FVector LowWall2Size(120.f, 500.f, 150.f);
	constexpr float NeonLaneY = 250.f;                // two neon lines on the floor from the start to the portal
	constexpr float NeonLaneStartX = -1700.f;
	constexpr float NeonLaneEndX = -150.f;

	// Colors
	const FLinearColor Floor(0.33f, 0.33f, 0.31f);
	const FLinearColor Wall(0.28f, 0.28f, 0.3f);
	const FLinearColor Course(0.3f, 0.32f, 0.38f);
	const FLinearColor Platform(0.32f, 0.3f, 0.28f);
	const FLinearColor Stone(0.55f, 0.45f, 0.32f);
	const FLinearColor Crate(0.45f, 0.27f, 0.11f);
	const FLinearColor TorchWood(0.25f, 0.15f, 0.06f);
	const FLinearColor Flame(1.f, 0.5f, 0.12f);
	const FLinearColor Metal(0.07f, 0.08f, 0.1f);
	const FLinearColor NeonCyan(0.1f, 0.9f, 1.f);
	const FLinearColor NeonMagenta(1.f, 0.15f, 0.8f);

	// Lights
	const FRotator SunRotation(-40.f, 35.f, 0.f);
	const FLinearColor PastSun(1.f, 0.82f, 0.6f);
	constexpr float PastSunLux = 7.f;
	const FLinearColor FutureSun(0.5f, 0.6f, 1.f);
	constexpr float FutureSunLux = 1.5f;
	constexpr float PastSkyIntensity = 1.f;
	constexpr float FutureSkyIntensity = 0.6f;
	const FLinearColor PastFog(0.9f, 0.7f, 0.5f);
	constexpr float PastFogDensity = 0.015f;
	const FLinearColor FutureFog(0.15f, 0.2f, 0.35f);
	constexpr float FutureFogDensity = 0.03f;
	const FLinearColor TorchLight(1.f, 0.55f, 0.2f);
	constexpr float TorchCandelas = 60.f;
	constexpr float TorchRadius = 900.f;
	constexpr float NeonCandelas = 40.f;
	constexpr float NeonRadius = 800.f;
}

namespace GBArenaIds
{
	// Names of things defined in the other data files (Weapons, Items, Enemies, Encounters, Spells).
	const FName PistolId(TEXT("Pistol"));
	const FName ShotgunId(TEXT("Shotgun"));
	const FName RifleId(TEXT("Rifle"));
	const FName StaffEmberId(TEXT("Staff_Ember"));
	const FName AmmoId(TEXT("Ammo_Magazine"));
	const FName HealthPotionId(TEXT("HealthPotion"));
	const FName ManaPotionId(TEXT("ManaPotion"));
	const FName GrenadeId(TEXT("ElectricGrenade"));
	const FName InjectionId(TEXT("HealthInjection"));
	const FName SwordHiltId(TEXT("SwordHilt"));
	const FName GemFireId(TEXT("Gem_Fire"));
	const FName GemIceId(TEXT("Gem_Ice"));
	const FName ArmorId(TEXT("Armor_Chronoweave"));
	const FName DummyId(TEXT("TrainingDummy"));
	const FName PastWaveId(TEXT("Test_PastWave"));
	const FName FutureWaveId(TEXT("Test_FutureWave"));
	const FName SpellGroups[] = { FName(TEXT("Fire")), FName(TEXT("Lightning")), FName(TEXT("Ice")), FName(TEXT("Air")) };

	// Progress flags owned by this level.
	const FName GotFirstGunFlag(TEXT("GotFirstGun"));
	const FName StartChestFlag(TEXT("Arena.Chest.Start"));
	const FName PlatformChestFlag(TEXT("Arena.Chest.Platform"));

	FActorSpawnParameters AlwaysSpawnParams()
	{
		FActorSpawnParameters Params;
		Params.SpawnCollisionHandlingOverride = ESpawnActorCollisionHandlingMethod::AlwaysSpawn;
		return Params;
	}
}

AGBTestArena::AGBTestArena()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = false;
	Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	RootComponent = Root;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers

AStaticMeshActor* AGBTestArena::SpawnShape(EGBShape Shape, const FVector& Center, const FVector& Size, const FLinearColor& Color,
	bool bGlow, bool bCollision, const FRotator& Rotation)
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (!World)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	AStaticMeshActor* Actor = World->SpawnActor<AStaticMeshActor>(AStaticMeshActor::StaticClass(), Center, Rotation, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());
	if (!Actor)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	UStaticMeshComponent* MeshComponent = Actor->GetStaticMeshComponent();
	// Movable: a static component cannot get a mesh or a scale once the actor exists at runtime.
	MeshComponent->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(MeshComponent, Shape, Size, Color, bGlow);
	MeshComponent->SetCollisionProfileName(bCollision ? UCollisionProfile::BlockAll_ProfileName : UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
	MeshComponent->SetCastShadow(!bGlow);
	return Actor;
}

APointLight* AGBTestArena::SpawnPointLight(const FVector& Location, const FLinearColor& Color, float Candelas, float Radius)
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	APointLight* Light = World ? World->SpawnActor<APointLight>(APointLight::StaticClass(), Location, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams()) : nullptr;
	if (!Light)
	{
		return nullptr;
	}
	if (UPointLightComponent* Component = Cast<UPointLightComponent>(Light->GetLightComponent()))
	{
		Component->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
		Component->SetIntensityUnits(ELightUnits::Candelas);
		Component->SetIntensity(Candelas);
		Component->SetLightColor(Color);
		Component->SetAttenuationRadius(Radius);
		Component->SetCastShadows(false);
	}
	return Light;
}

void AGBTestArena::SetPresence(AActor* Actor, EGBEraPresence Presence, FName RequiredFlag)
{
	if (!Actor)
	{
		return;
	}
	UGBEraComponent* Era = NewObject<UGBEraComponent>(Actor);
	Actor->AddInstanceComponent(Era);
	Era->RegisterComponent();
	Era->Configure(Presence, RequiredFlag);
}

FGBDropDefinition AGBTestArena::MakeItemDrop(FName ItemId, int32 Count)
{
	FGBDropDefinition Drop;
	Drop.ItemId = ItemId;
	Drop.Count = Count;
	return Drop;
}

FGBDropDefinition AGBTestArena::MakeWeaponDrop(FName WeaponId)
{
	FGBDropDefinition Drop;
	Drop.WeaponId = WeaponId;
	return Drop;
}

FGBDropDefinition AGBTestArena::MakeSpellDrop(FName SpellGroup)
{
	FGBDropDefinition Drop;
	Drop.SpellGroup = SpellGroup;
	return Drop;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Static part: before the player exists

void AGBTestArena::BuildStatic()
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (bStaticBuilt || !World)
	{
		return;
	}
	bStaticBuilt = true;

	// ---- Lighting: movable sun, real-time sky light, atmosphere, height fog ----
	Sun = World->SpawnActor<ADirectionalLight>(ADirectionalLight::StaticClass(), FVector(0.f, 0.f, 1000.f), GBArena::SunRotation, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());
	if (Sun)
	{
		if (UDirectionalLightComponent* SunComponent = Sun->GetComponent())
		{
			SunComponent->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
			SunComponent->SetAtmosphereSunLight(true);
			SunComponent->SetCastShadows(true);
		}
	}
	SkyLight = World->SpawnActor<ASkyLight>(ASkyLight::StaticClass(), FVector(0.f, 0.f, 500.f), FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());
	if (SkyLight)
	{
		if (USkyLightComponent* SkyComponent = SkyLight->GetLightComponent())
		{
			SkyComponent->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
			SkyComponent->SetRealTimeCapture(true);
		}
	}
	World->SpawnActor<ASkyAtmosphere>(ASkyAtmosphere::StaticClass(), FVector::ZeroVector, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());
	Fog = World->SpawnActor<AExponentialHeightFog>(AExponentialHeightFog::StaticClass(), FVector::ZeroVector, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());
	ApplyEraLighting(EGBEra::Past);

	// ---- Floor and perimeter walls ----
	const float Outer = GBArena::HalfSize + GBArena::WallThickness * 0.5f;
	const float FullSpan = GBArena::HalfSize * 2.f + GBArena::WallThickness * 2.f;
	SpawnBlock(FVector(0.f, 0.f, -GBArena::FloorThickness * 0.5f), FVector(FullSpan, FullSpan, GBArena::FloorThickness), GBArena::Floor);
	SpawnBlock(FVector(0.f, Outer, GBArena::WallHeight * 0.5f), FVector(FullSpan, GBArena::WallThickness, GBArena::WallHeight), GBArena::Wall);
	SpawnBlock(FVector(0.f, -Outer, GBArena::WallHeight * 0.5f), FVector(FullSpan, GBArena::WallThickness, GBArena::WallHeight), GBArena::Wall);
	SpawnBlock(FVector(Outer, 0.f, GBArena::WallHeight * 0.5f), FVector(GBArena::WallThickness, FullSpan, GBArena::WallHeight), GBArena::Wall);
	SpawnBlock(FVector(-Outer, 0.f, GBArena::WallHeight * 0.5f), FVector(GBArena::WallThickness, FullSpan, GBArena::WallHeight), GBArena::Wall);

	// ---- Movement course: double-jump steps, then a dash gap to the landing block ----
	for (const FVector2D& Step : GBArena::Steps)
	{
		SpawnBlock(FVector(Step.X, GBArena::CourseY, Step.Y * 0.5f), FVector(GBArena::StepSize, GBArena::StepSize, Step.Y), GBArena::Course);
	}
	const FVector2D& LastStep = GBArena::Steps[UE_ARRAY_COUNT(GBArena::Steps) - 1];
	const float LandingX = LastStep.X + GBArena::StepSize + GBArena::DashGap;
	SpawnBlock(FVector(LandingX, GBArena::CourseY, LastStep.Y * 0.5f), FVector(GBArena::StepSize, GBArena::StepSize, LastStep.Y), GBArena::Course);

	// ---- Raised platform (the ramp is gameplay: it depends on era and a flag) ----
	SpawnBlock(FVector(GBArena::PlatformCenter.X, GBArena::PlatformCenter.Y, GBArena::PlatformTop * 0.5f), FVector(GBArena::PlatformSize.X, GBArena::PlatformSize.Y, GBArena::PlatformTop), GBArena::Platform);

	// ---- Era-gated room walls (the doorway blocker is gameplay) ----
	const float RoomSouthY = -GBArena::HalfSize;
	const float RoomEastX = GBArena::HalfSize;
	const float RoomDepth = GBArena::RoomNorthY - RoomSouthY + GBArena::WallThickness * 0.5f;
	SpawnBlock(FVector(GBArena::RoomWestX, RoomSouthY + RoomDepth * 0.5f, GBArena::WallHeight * 0.5f), FVector(GBArena::WallThickness, RoomDepth, GBArena::WallHeight), GBArena::Wall);
	const float DoorWestX = GBArena::DoorCenterX - GBArena::DoorWidth * 0.5f;
	const float DoorEastX = GBArena::DoorCenterX + GBArena::DoorWidth * 0.5f;
	const float WestSegment = DoorWestX - GBArena::RoomWestX;
	const float EastSegment = RoomEastX - DoorEastX;
	SpawnBlock(FVector(GBArena::RoomWestX + WestSegment * 0.5f, GBArena::RoomNorthY, GBArena::WallHeight * 0.5f), FVector(WestSegment, GBArena::WallThickness, GBArena::WallHeight), GBArena::Wall);
	SpawnBlock(FVector(DoorEastX + EastSegment * 0.5f, GBArena::RoomNorthY, GBArena::WallHeight * 0.5f), FVector(EastSegment, GBArena::WallThickness, GBArena::WallHeight), GBArena::Wall);

	// ---- Player start ----
	PlayerStartTransform = FTransform(FRotator(0.f, GBArena::PlayerStartYaw, 0.f), GBArena::PlayerStart);
	World->SpawnActor<APlayerStart>(APlayerStart::StaticClass(), GBArena::PlayerStart, FRotator(0.f, GBArena::PlayerStartYaw, 0.f), GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());
}

void AGBTestArena::ApplyEraLighting(EGBEra Era)
{
	const bool bPast = Era == EGBEra::Past;
	if (Sun)
	{
		if (UDirectionalLightComponent* SunComponent = Sun->GetComponent())
		{
			SunComponent->SetLightColor(bPast ? GBArena::PastSun : GBArena::FutureSun);
			SunComponent->SetIntensity(bPast ? GBArena::PastSunLux : GBArena::FutureSunLux);
		}
	}
	if (SkyLight)
	{
		if (USkyLightComponent* SkyComponent = SkyLight->GetLightComponent())
		{
			SkyComponent->SetIntensity(bPast ? GBArena::PastSkyIntensity : GBArena::FutureSkyIntensity);
		}
	}
	if (Fog)
	{
		if (UExponentialHeightFogComponent* FogComponent = Fog->GetComponent())
		{
			FogComponent->SetFogInscatteringColor(bPast ? GBArena::PastFog : GBArena::FutureFog);
			FogComponent->SetFogDensity(bPast ? GBArena::PastFogDensity : GBArena::FutureFogDensity);
		}
	}
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Gameplay part

void AGBTestArena::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	BuildStatic();

	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		EraChangedHandle = Era->OnEraChanged.AddUObject(this, &AGBTestArena::ApplyEraLighting);
		ApplyEraLighting(Era->GetCurrentEra());
	}

	BuildGameplay();
}

void AGBTestArena::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->OnEraChanged.Remove(EraChangedHandle);
	}
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBTestArena::BuildGameplay()
{
	UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	if (bGameplayBuilt || !World)
	{
		return;
	}
	bGameplayBuilt = true;

	BuildPastDecor();
	BuildFutureDecor();

	// ---- Start area: checkpoint, dummies, pickups, spell knowledge, chest ----
	if (AGBCheckpointVolume* Checkpoint = World->SpawnActor<AGBCheckpointVolume>(AGBCheckpointVolume::StaticClass(),
		GBArena::PlayerStart + FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBArena::StartCheckpointExtent.Z - GBArena::PlayerStart.Z), FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams()))
	{
		Checkpoint->SetExtent(GBArena::StartCheckpointExtent);
	}

	for (const FVector& DummyLocation : GBArena::Dummies)
	{
		if (!AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(World, GBArenaIds::DummyId, DummyLocation, FRotator(0.f, GBArena::DummyYaw, 0.f), EGBEraPresence::Both))
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Test arena: could not spawn enemy %s."), *GBArenaIds::DummyId.ToString());
		}
	}

	const FGBDropDefinition StartPickups[] = {
		MakeWeaponDrop(GBArenaIds::PistolId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::AmmoId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::AmmoId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::HealthPotionId),
		MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::ManaPotionId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::GrenadeId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::InjectionId) };
	static_assert(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(StartPickups) == UE_ARRAY_COUNT(GBArena::PickupRowY), "One row position per start pickup.");
	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < static_cast<int32>(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(StartPickups)); ++Index)
	{
		const FName Flag = StartPickups[Index].WeaponId == GBArenaIds::PistolId ? GBArenaIds::GotFirstGunFlag : NAME_None;
		AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(World, StartPickups[Index], FVector(GBArena::PickupRowX, GBArena::PickupRowY[Index], 0.f), Flag);
	}

	static_assert(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(GBArenaIds::SpellGroups) == UE_ARRAY_COUNT(GBArena::SpellRowX), "One row position per spell group.");
	for (int32 Index = 0; Index < static_cast<int32>(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(GBArenaIds::SpellGroups)); ++Index)
	{
		AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(World, MakeSpellDrop(GBArenaIds::SpellGroups[Index]), FVector(GBArena::SpellRowX[Index], GBArena::SpellRowY, 0.f));
	}

	if (AGBChest* Chest = World->SpawnActor<AGBChest>(AGBChest::StaticClass(), GBArena::StartChest, FRotator(0.f, GBArena::StartChestYaw, 0.f), GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams()))
	{
		const FGBDropDefinition Contents[] = { MakeWeaponDrop(GBArenaIds::StaffEmberId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::SwordHiltId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::GemFireId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::GemIceId) };
		Chest->Configure(Contents, GBArenaIds::StartChestFlag);
	}

	// ---- Central portal, present in both eras; the player stays in place and changes time ----
	World->SpawnActor<AGBPortal>(AGBPortal::StaticClass(), GBArena::Portal, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());

	// ---- Movement course reward on the landing block ----
	{
		const FVector2D& LastStep = GBArena::Steps[UE_ARRAY_COUNT(GBArena::Steps) - 1];
		const float LandingX = LastStep.X + GBArena::StepSize + GBArena::DashGap;
		AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(World, MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::AmmoId), FVector(LandingX, GBArena::CourseY, LastStep.Y + 10.f));
	}

	// ---- Encounters ----
	AGBEncounter* PastEncounter = World->SpawnActor<AGBEncounter>(AGBEncounter::StaticClass(), GBArena::PastWave, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());
	if (PastEncounter)
	{
		PastEncounter->Configure(GBArenaIds::PastWaveId, EGBEraPresence::PastOnly, GBArena::EncounterTrigger, GBArena::EncounterSpawns);
	}
	if (AGBEncounter* FutureEncounter = World->SpawnActor<AGBEncounter>(AGBEncounter::StaticClass(), GBArena::FutureWave, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams()))
	{
		FutureEncounter->Configure(GBArenaIds::FutureWaveId, EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly, GBArena::EncounterTrigger, GBArena::EncounterSpawns);
	}

	// ---- Past-to-future unlock: a future-only ramp to the raised platform, once the past wave is cleared ----
	FName PastClearedFlag = PastEncounter ? PastEncounter->GetClearedFlag() : NAME_None;
	if (PastClearedFlag.IsNone())
	{
		PastClearedFlag = FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Encounter.%s.Cleared"), *GBArenaIds::PastWaveId.ToString()));
	}
	{
		const float Run = GBArena::PlatformCenter.X - GBArena::PlatformSize.X * 0.5f - GBArena::RampStartX;
		const float Rise = GBArena::PlatformTop;
		const float Length = FMath::Sqrt(Run * Run + Rise * Rise) + GBArena::RampThickness;
		const float PitchRad = FMath::Atan2(Rise, Run);
		const FRotator RampRotation(FMath::RadiansToDegrees(PitchRad), 0.f, 0.f);
		// The top surface goes from the foot (RampStartX, 0) to the platform edge; the box center sits half a thickness below it.
		const FVector Up = RampRotation.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
		const FVector SurfaceMid(GBArena::RampStartX + Run * 0.5f, GBArena::RampY, Rise * 0.5f);
		const FVector RampCenter = SurfaceMid - Up * (GBArena::RampThickness * 0.5f);
		AStaticMeshActor* Ramp = SpawnShape(EGBShape::Cube, RampCenter, FVector(Length, GBArena::RampWidth, GBArena::RampThickness), GBArena::Metal, false, true, RampRotation);
		SetPresence(Ramp, EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly, PastClearedFlag);
		const FVector Side = RampRotation.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector) * (GBArena::RampWidth * 0.5f - 6.f);
		for (const float Sign : { -1.f, 1.f })
		{
			AStaticMeshActor* Strip = SpawnGlow(SurfaceMid + Up * 2.f + Side * Sign, FVector(Length, 8.f, 4.f), GBArena::NeonCyan, RampRotation);
			SetPresence(Strip, EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly, PastClearedFlag);
		}
	}
	if (AGBChest* PlatformChestActor = World->SpawnActor<AGBChest>(AGBChest::StaticClass(), GBArena::PlatformChest, FRotator(0.f, GBArena::PlatformChestYaw, 0.f), GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams()))
	{
		const FGBDropDefinition Contents[] = { MakeWeaponDrop(GBArenaIds::RifleId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::ArmorId) };
		PlatformChestActor->Configure(Contents, GBArenaIds::PlatformChestFlag);
	}

	// ---- Era-gated room: the doorway is walled up in the past, open in the future ----
	AStaticMeshActor* DoorBlock = SpawnBlock(FVector(GBArena::DoorCenterX, GBArena::RoomNorthY, GBArena::WallHeight * 0.5f), FVector(GBArena::DoorWidth, GBArena::WallThickness, GBArena::WallHeight), GBArena::Stone);
	SetPresence(DoorBlock, EGBEraPresence::PastOnly);
	AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(World, MakeWeaponDrop(GBArenaIds::ShotgunId), GBArena::RoomShotgun);
	for (const FVector& AmmoLocation : GBArena::RoomAmmo)
	{
		AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(World, MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::AmmoId), AmmoLocation);
	}
}

void AGBTestArena::BuildPastDecor()
{
	const EGBEraPresence Past = EGBEraPresence::PastOnly;

	// Stone pillars with a brazier on top.
	for (const FVector2D& Column : GBArena::Columns)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnShape(EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight * 0.5f), FVector(100.f, 100.f, GBArena::ColumnHeight), GBArena::Stone), Past);
		SetPresence(SpawnShape(EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight + 25.f), FVector(45.f, 45.f, 45.f), GBArena::Flame, true, false), Past);
		SetPresence(SpawnPointLight(FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight + 100.f), GBArena::TorchLight, GBArena::TorchCandelas, GBArena::TorchRadius), Past);
	}

	// Wall torches.
	for (const FVector& Torch : GBArena::Torches)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnShape(EGBShape::Cylinder, Torch, FVector(14.f, 14.f, 70.f), GBArena::TorchWood, false, false), Past);
		SetPresence(SpawnGlow(Torch + FVector(0.f, 0.f, 45.f), FVector(18.f, 18.f, 26.f), GBArena::Flame), Past);
		SetPresence(SpawnPointLight(Torch + FVector(0.f, 0.f, 80.f), GBArena::TorchLight, GBArena::TorchCandelas, GBArena::TorchRadius), Past);
	}

	// Crates and low stone walls.
	for (const FVector& CrateLocation : GBArena::Crates)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnBlock(CrateLocation, FVector(100.f, 100.f, 100.f), GBArena::Crate), Past);
	}
	SetPresence(SpawnBlock(GBArena::LowWallCenter, GBArena::LowWallSize, GBArena::Stone), Past);
	SetPresence(SpawnBlock(GBArena::LowWall2Center, GBArena::LowWall2Size, GBArena::Stone), Past);
}

void AGBTestArena::BuildFutureDecor()
{
	const EGBEraPresence Future = EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly;

	// Dark metal columns with neon strips where the past pillars stand.
	for (const FVector2D& Column : GBArena::Columns)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnBlock(FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight * 0.5f), FVector(120.f, 120.f, GBArena::ColumnHeight), GBArena::Metal), Future);
		for (const float Sign : { -1.f, 1.f })
		{
			SetPresence(SpawnGlow(FVector(Column.X + Sign * 62.f, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight * 0.5f), FVector(6.f, 20.f, GBArena::ColumnHeight - 40.f), GBArena::NeonCyan), Future);
		}
		SetPresence(SpawnPointLight(FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight - 50.f), GBArena::NeonCyan, GBArena::NeonCandelas, GBArena::NeonRadius), Future);
	}

	// Containers where the crates were, with a magenta neon line on top.
	for (const FVector& Container : GBArena::Containers)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnBlock(Container, FVector(240.f, 120.f, 130.f), GBArena::Metal), Future);
		SetPresence(SpawnGlow(Container + FVector(0.f, 0.f, 67.f), FVector(240.f, 10.f, 6.f), GBArena::NeonMagenta), Future);
		SetPresence(SpawnPointLight(Container + FVector(0.f, 0.f, 150.f), GBArena::NeonMagenta, GBArena::NeonCandelas, GBArena::NeonRadius), Future);
	}

	// Neon lanes on the floor from the start to the portal.
	const float LaneLength = GBArena::NeonLaneEndX - GBArena::NeonLaneStartX;
	for (const float Sign : { -1.f, 1.f })
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnGlow(FVector(GBArena::NeonLaneStartX + LaneLength * 0.5f, Sign * GBArena::NeonLaneY, 1.5f), FVector(LaneLength, 12.f, 3.f), GBArena::NeonCyan), Future);
	}
}
#include "Player/GBInputSet.h"

#include "InputAction.h"
#include "InputMappingContext.h"
#include "InputModifiers.h"
#include "InputCoreTypes.h"

namespace
{
	// Stick dead zones (input feel, not gameplay numbers).
	constexpr float GameplayStickDeadZone = 0.2f;
	constexpr float MenuStickDeadZone = 0.5f;

	/** Where a digital key pushes a 2D axis. */
	enum class EDir2D : uint8 { Right, Left, Up, Down };

	UInputAction* MakeAction(UObject* Outer, const TCHAR* Name, EInputActionValueType Type, bool bTriggerWhenPaused = false)
	{
		UInputAction* Action = NewObject<UInputAction>(Outer, MakeUniqueObjectName(Outer, UInputAction::StaticClass(), FName(Name)));
		Action->ValueType = Type;
		Action->bTriggerWhenPaused = bTriggerWhenPaused;
		return Action;
	}

	UInputModifierNegate* MakeNegate(UObject* Outer)
	{
		return NewObject<UInputModifierNegate>(Outer);
	}

	UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* MakeSwizzleYXZ(UObject* Outer)
	{
		UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* Swizzle = NewObject<UInputModifierSwizzleAxis>(Outer);
		Swizzle->Order = EInputAxisSwizzle::YXZ;
		return Swizzle;
	}

	UInputModifierDeadZone* MakeDeadZone(UObject* Outer, float Lower)
	{
		UInputModifierDeadZone* DeadZone = NewObject<UInputModifierDeadZone>(Outer);
		DeadZone->LowerThreshold = Lower;
		DeadZone->UpperThreshold = 1.f;
		DeadZone->Type = EDeadZoneType::Radial;
		return DeadZone;
	}

	// MapKey returns a reference into the context's array: every helper adds its modifiers before the next MapKey.

	void MapButton(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key)
	{
		Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
	}

	/** Axis1D: +1 or -1. */
	void MapAxis1D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, bool bNegative = false)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (bNegative)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a digital key: x = right, y = up / forward. */
	void MapDir2D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, EDir2D Dir)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Up || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeSwizzleYXZ(Context));
		}
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Left || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a stick, with a radial dead zone. */
	void MapStick(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, float DeadZone)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeDeadZone(Context, DeadZone));
	}

	void MapWASD(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action)
	{
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::W, EDir2D::Up);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::S, EDir2D::Down);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::A, EDir2D::Left);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::D, EDir2D::Right);
	}
}

void UGBInputSet::Build()
{
	if (GameplayContext)
	{
		return; // built once per player
	}

	using EType = EInputActionValueType;

	// ---- Actions ----
	Move = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Move"), EType::Axis2D);
	Look = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Look"), EType::Axis2D);
	LookStick = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_LookStick"), EType::Axis2D);
	SwitchWeapon = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SwitchWeapon"), EType::Axis1D);
	CycleQuickUse = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_CycleQuickUse"), EType::Axis1D);

	Jump = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Jump"), EType::Boolean);
	Dash = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Dash"), EType::Boolean);
	Primary = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Primary"), EType::Boolean);
	Secondary = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Secondary"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot1 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot1"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot2 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot2"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot3 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot3"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot4 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot4"), EType::Boolean);
	SpellSlot5 = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SpellSlot5"), EType::Boolean);
	Enchant = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Enchant"), EType::Boolean);
	Reload = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Reload"), EType::Boolean);
	Interact = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Interact"), EType::Boolean);
	QuickUse = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QuickUse"), EType::Boolean);
	// These three open / close menus, so they must also fire while the game is paused.
	Inventory = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Inventory"), EType::Boolean, true);
	SkillTrees = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_SkillTrees"), EType::Boolean, true);
	Pause = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_Pause"), EType::Boolean, true);

	FaceUp = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceUp"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceLeft = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceLeft"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceDown = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceDown"), EType::Boolean);
	FaceRight = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_FaceRight"), EType::Boolean);

	QTEUp = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTEUp"), EType::Boolean);
	QTELeft = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTELeft"), EType::Boolean);
	QTEDown = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTEDown"), EType::Boolean);
	QTERight = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_QTERight"), EType::Boolean);

	MenuNavigate = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuNavigate"), EType::Axis2D, true);
	MenuConfirm = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuConfirm"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuBack = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuBack"), EType::Boolean, true);
	MenuTab = MakeAction(this, TEXT("IA_MenuTab"), EType::Axis1D, true);

	// ---- Gameplay context (GDD 9) ----
	GameplayContext = NewObject<UInputMappingContext>(this, MakeUniqueObjectName(this, UInputMappingContext::StaticClass(), TEXT("IMC_Gameplay")));
	UInputMappingContext* G = GameplayContext;

	MapWASD(G, Move);
	MapStick(G, Move, EKeys::Gamepad_Left2D, GameplayStickDeadZone);

	G->MapKey(Look, EKeys::Mouse2D);
	MapStick(G, LookStick, EKeys::Gamepad_Right2D, GameplayStickDeadZone);

	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::MouseWheelAxis);
	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::LeftAlt);
	MapAxis1D(G, SwitchWeapon, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Up);

	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::LeftShift);
	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Right);
	MapAxis1D(G, CycleQuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Left, true);

	MapButton(G, Jump, EKeys::SpaceBar);
	MapButton(G, Dash, EKeys::LeftControl);

	MapButton(G, Primary, EKeys::LeftMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, Primary, EKeys::Gamepad_RightTrigger);
	MapButton(G, Secondary, EKeys::RightMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, Secondary, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftTrigger);

	MapButton(G, SpellSlot1, EKeys::RightMouseButton);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot1, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot2, EKeys::One);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot3, EKeys::Two);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot4, EKeys::Three);
	MapButton(G, SpellSlot5, EKeys::Four);

	MapButton(G, Enchant, EKeys::T);
	MapButton(G, Enchant, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder);
	MapButton(G, Reload, EKeys::R);
	MapButton(G, Interact, EKeys::G);
	MapButton(G, QuickUse, EKeys::F);
	MapButton(G, QuickUse, EKeys::Gamepad_RightShoulder);

	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::Tab);
	MapButton(G, Inventory, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Left);
	MapButton(G, SkillTrees, EKeys::K);
	MapButton(G, Pause, EKeys::Escape);
	MapButton(G, Pause, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Right);

	MapButton(G, FaceUp, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Top);
	MapButton(G, FaceLeft, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Left);
	MapButton(G, FaceDown, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Bottom);
	MapButton(G, FaceRight, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Right);

	MapButton(G, QTEUp, EKeys::W);
	MapButton(G, QTELeft, EKeys::A);
	MapButton(G, QTEDown, EKeys::S);
	MapButton(G, QTERight, EKeys::D);

	// ---- Menu context ----
	MenuContext = NewObject<UInputMappingContext>(this, MakeUniqueObjectName(this, UInputMappingContext::StaticClass(), TEXT("IMC_Menu")));
	UInputMappingContext* M = MenuContext;

	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Up, EDir2D::Up);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Down, EDir2D::Down);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Left, EDir2D::Left);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Right, EDir2D::Right);
	MapWASD(M, MenuNavigate);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Up, EDir2D::Up);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Down, EDir2D::Down);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Left, EDir2D::Left);
	MapDir2D(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_DPad_Right, EDir2D::Right);
	MapStick(M, MenuNavigate, EKeys::Gamepad_Left2D, MenuStickDeadZone);

	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::Enter);
	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::SpaceBar);
	MapButton(M, MenuConfirm, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Bottom);

	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Escape);
	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Tab);
	MapButton(M, MenuBack, EKeys::Gamepad_FaceButton_Right);

	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Q, true);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::E);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_LeftShoulder, true);
	MapAxis1D(M, MenuTab, EKeys::Gamepad_RightShoulder);

	// Inside menus: the gamepad Menu / View buttons and K still toggle their screens (Esc and Tab are "back" here).
	MapButton(M, Pause, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Right);
	MapButton(M, Inventory, EKeys::Gamepad_Special_Left);
	MapButton(M, SkillTrees, EKeys::K);
}

void UCharacterMovementComponent::CalcVelocity(float DeltaTime, float Friction, bool bFluid, float BrakingDeceleration)
{
	// Do not update velocity when using root motion or when SimulatedProxy and not simulating root motion - SimulatedProxy are repped their Velocity
	if (!HasValidData() || HasAnimRootMotion() || DeltaTime < MIN_TICK_TIME || (CharacterOwner && CharacterOwner->GetLocalRole() == ROLE_SimulatedProxy && !bWasSimulatingRootMotion))
	{
		return;
	}

	Friction = FMath::Max(0.f, Friction);
	const float MaxAccel = GetMaxAcceleration();
	float MaxSpeed = GetMaxSpeed();
	
	// Check if path following requested movement
	bool bZeroRequestedAcceleration = true;
	FVector RequestedAcceleration = FVector::ZeroVector;
	float RequestedSpeed = 0.0f;
	if (ApplyRequestedMove(DeltaTime, MaxAccel, MaxSpeed, Friction, BrakingDeceleration, RequestedAcceleration, RequestedSpeed))
	{
		bZeroRequestedAcceleration = false;
	}

	if (bForceMaxAccel)
	{
		// Force acceleration at full speed.
		// In consideration order for direction: Acceleration, then Velocity, then Pawn's rotation.
		if (Acceleration.SizeSquared() > UE_SMALL_NUMBER)
		{
			Acceleration = Acceleration.GetSafeNormal() * MaxAccel;
		}
		else 
		{
			Acceleration = MaxAccel * (Velocity.SizeSquared() < UE_SMALL_NUMBER ? UpdatedComponent->GetForwardVector() : Velocity.GetSafeNormal());
		}

		AnalogInputModifier = 1.f;
	}

	// Path following above didn't care about the analog modifier, but we do for everything else below, so get the fully modified value.
	// Use max of requested speed and max speed if we modified the speed in ApplyRequestedMove above.
	const float MaxInputSpeed = FMath::Max(MaxSpeed * AnalogInputModifier, GetMinAnalogSpeed());
	MaxSpeed = FMath::Max(RequestedSpeed, MaxInputSpeed);

	// Apply braking or deceleration
	const bool bZeroAcceleration = Acceleration.IsZero();
	const bool bVelocityOverMax = IsExceedingMaxSpeed(MaxSpeed);
	
	// Only apply braking if there is no acceleration, or we are over our max speed and need to slow down to it.
	if ((bZeroAcceleration && bZeroRequestedAcceleration) || bVelocityOverMax)
	{
		const FVector OldVelocity = Velocity;

		const float ActualBrakingFriction = (bUseSeparateBrakingFriction ? BrakingFriction : Friction);
		ApplyVelocityBraking(DeltaTime, ActualBrakingFriction, BrakingDeceleration);
	
		// Don't allow braking to lower us below max speed if we started above it.
		if (bVelocityOverMax && Velocity.SizeSquared() < FMath::Square(MaxSpeed) && FVector::DotProduct(Acceleration, OldVelocity) > 0.0f)
		{
			Velocity = OldVelocity.GetSafeNormal() * MaxSpeed;
		}
	}
	else if (!bZeroAcceleration)
	{
		// Friction affects our ability to change direction. This is only done for input acceleration, not path following.
		const FVector AccelDir = Acceleration.GetSafeNormal();
		const float VelSize = Velocity.Size();
		Velocity = Velocity - (Velocity - AccelDir * VelSize) * FMath::Min(DeltaTime * Friction, 1.f);
	}

	// Apply fluid friction
	if (bFluid)
	{
		Velocity = Velocity * (1.f - FMath::Min(Friction * DeltaTime, 1.f));
	}

	// Apply input acceleration
	if (!bZeroAcceleration)
	{
		const float NewMaxInputSpeed = IsExceedingMaxSpeed(MaxInputSpeed) ? Velocity.Size() : MaxInputSpeed;
		Velocity += Acceleration * DeltaTime;
		Velocity = Velocity.GetClampedToMaxSize(NewMaxInputSpeed);
	}

	// Apply additional requested acceleration
	if (!bZeroRequestedAcceleration)
	{
		const float NewMaxRequestedSpeed = IsExceedingMaxSpeed(RequestedSpeed) ? Velocity.Size() : RequestedSpeed;
		Velocity += RequestedAcceleration * DeltaTime;
		Velocity = Velocity.GetClampedToMaxSize(NewMaxRequestedSpeed);
	}

	if (bUseRVOAvoidance)
	{
		CalcAvoidanceVelocity(DeltaTime);
	}
}

bool UCharacterMovementComponent::ShouldComputeAccelerationToReachRequestedVelocity(const float RequestedSpeed) const
{
	// Compute acceleration if accelerating toward requested speed, 1% buffer.
	return bRequestedMoveUseAcceleration && Velocity.SizeSquared() < FMath::Square(RequestedSpeed * 1.01f);
}

bool UCharacterMovementComponent::ApplyRequestedMove(float DeltaTime, float MaxAccel, float MaxSpeed, float Friction, float BrakingDeceleration, FVector& OutAcceleration, float& OutRequestedSpeed)
{
	if (bHasRequestedVelocity)
	{
		const float RequestedSpeedSquared = RequestedVelocity.SizeSquared();
		if (RequestedSpeedSquared < UE_KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER)
		{
			return false;
		}

		// Compute requested speed from path following
		float RequestedSpeed = FMath::Sqrt(RequestedSpeedSquared);
		const FVector RequestedMoveDir = RequestedVelocity / RequestedSpeed;
		RequestedSpeed = (bRequestedMoveWithMaxSpeed ? MaxSpeed : FMath::Min(MaxSpeed, RequestedSpeed));
		
		// Compute actual requested velocity
		const FVector MoveVelocity = RequestedMoveDir * RequestedSpeed;
		
void UCharacterMovementComponent::PhysFlying(float deltaTime, int32 Iterations)
{
	if (deltaTime < MIN_TICK_TIME)
	{
		return;
	}

	RestorePreAdditiveRootMotionVelocity();

	if( !HasAnimRootMotion() && !CurrentRootMotion.HasOverrideVelocity() )
	{
		if( bCheatFlying && Acceleration.IsZero() )
		{
			Velocity = FVector::ZeroVector;
		}
		const float Friction = 0.5f * GetPhysicsVolume()->FluidFriction;
		CalcVelocity(deltaTime, Friction, true, GetMaxBrakingDeceleration());
	}

	ApplyRootMotionToVelocity(deltaTime);

	Iterations++;
	bJustTeleported = false;

	FVector OldLocation = UpdatedComponent->GetComponentLocation();
	const FVector Adjusted = Velocity * deltaTime;
	FHitResult Hit(1.f);
	SafeMoveUpdatedComponent(Adjusted, UpdatedComponent->GetComponentQuat(), true, Hit);

	if (Hit.Time < 1.f)
	{
		const FVector VelDir = Velocity.GetSafeNormal();
		const float UpDown = VelDir | GetGravityDirection();

		bool bSteppedUp = false;
		if ((FMath::Abs(GetGravitySpaceZ(Hit.ImpactNormal)) < 0.2f) && (UpDown < 0.5f) && (UpDown > -0.2f) && CanStepUp(Hit))
		{
			const FVector::FReal StepZ = GetGravitySpaceZ(UpdatedComponent->GetComponentLocation());
			bSteppedUp = StepUp(GetGravityDirection(), Adjusted * (1.f - Hit.Time), Hit);
			if (bSteppedUp)
			{
				const FVector::FReal LocationZ = GetGravitySpaceZ(UpdatedComponent->GetComponentLocation()) + (GetGravitySpaceZ(OldLocation) - StepZ);
				SetGravitySpaceZ(OldLocation, LocationZ);
			}
		}

		if (!bSteppedUp)
		{
			//adjust and try again
			HandleImpact(Hit, deltaTime, Adjusted);
			SlideAlongSurface(Adjusted, (1.f - Hit.Time), Hit.Normal, Hit, true);
		}
	}

	if( !bJustTeleported && !HasAnimRootMotion() && !CurrentRootMotion.HasOverrideVelocity() )
	{
		Velocity = (UpdatedComponent->GetComponentLocation() - OldLocation) / deltaTime;
	}
}

void UCharacterMovementComponent::RestorePreAdditiveRootMotionVelocity()
{
	// Restore last frame's pre-additive Velocity if we had additive applied 
	// so that we're not adding more additive velocity than intended
	if( CurrentRootMotion.bIsAdditiveVelocityApplied )
	{
#if ROOT_MOTION_DEBUG
		if (RootMotionSourceDebug::CVarDebugRootMotionSources.GetValueOnGameThread() == 1)
		{
			FString AdjustedDebugString = FString::Printf(TEXT("RestorePreAdditiveRootMotionVelocity Velocity(%s) LastPreAdditiveVelocity(%s)"), 
				*Velocity.ToCompactString(), *CurrentRootMotion.LastPreAdditiveVelocity.ToCompactString());
			RootMotionSourceDebug::PrintOnScreen(*CharacterOwner, AdjustedDebugString);
		}
#endif

// One generic projectile for everything that flies: staff bolts, fireballs, lightning bolts, enemy shots, grenades.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "GameFramework/Actor.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "GBProjectile.generated.h"

class USphereComponent;
class UProjectileMovementComponent;
class UBillboardComponent;
class UStaticMeshComponent;
class UPrimitiveComponent;

/** Everything a projectile needs; filled from weapon stats, spell params or item stats by whoever fires it. */
USTRUCT(BlueprintType)
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBProjectileConfig
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	/** The hit dealt on impact (Amount, Element, Instigator, SourceId, OnHitEffect...). Causer is set to the projectile. */
	UPROPERTY() FGBDamageInfo Damage;

	UPROPERTY() float Speed = 3000.f;
	/** Collision radius, cm. */
	UPROPERTY() float Radius = 12.f;
	/** 0 = flies straight; 1 = normal gravity (grenades). */
	UPROPERTY() float GravityScale = 0.f;
	/** The projectile disappears after travelling this far (cm). 0 = use MaxLifetime only. */
	UPROPERTY() float MaxRange = 5000.f;
	UPROPERTY() float MaxLifetime = 10.f;
	/** > 0: on impact (or when the fuse / range ends) it deals Damage in this radius instead of a single hit. */
	UPROPERTY() float ExplosionRadius = 0.f;
	UPROPERTY() bool bExplosionFalloff = true;
	/** > 0: it does not explode on contact with the world; it bounces and explodes after this many seconds (grenades). */
	UPROPERTY() float FuseTime = 0.f;
	/** Keeps going through actors it damages (each actor once). */
	UPROPERTY() bool bPierce = false;

	// ---- Looks (art rule: bPixelArt = camera-facing sprite, otherwise a small 3D mesh) ----
	UPROPERTY() bool bPixelArt = true;
	/** Sprite id from Sprites.json (pixel art). */
	UPROPERTY() FName SpriteId = NAME_None;
	UPROPERTY() FLinearColor Color = FLinearColor::White;
	/** Visual scale relative to the collision radius. */
	UPROPERTY() float VisualScale = 1.f;
};

DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_ThreeParams(FGBOnProjectileImpact, class AGBProjectile* /*Projectile*/, AActor* /*HitActor (may be null)*/, const FVector& /*Location*/);

UCLASS()
class GLYPHBOUND_API AGBProjectile : public AActor
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	AGBProjectile();

	/**
	 * Spawns and launches a projectile. It ignores its instigator and the instigator's team.
	 * Direction does not need to be normalized. Returns null if the spawn fails.
	 */
	static AGBProjectile* SpawnProjectile(UWorld* World, const FGBProjectileConfig& Config, const FVector& Origin, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator);

	const FGBProjectileConfig& GetConfig() const { return Config; }

	/** Fired once, when it hits something or explodes (before it is destroyed). Spells use it to chain behavior. */
	FGBOnProjectileImpact OnImpact;

	virtual void Tick(float DeltaSeconds) override;

protected:
	virtual void BeginPlay() override;
	virtual void EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason) override;

	void InitProjectile(const FGBProjectileConfig& InConfig, const FVector& Direction, AActor* InInstigator);

	UFUNCTION()
	void OnCollisionHit(UPrimitiveComponent* HitComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp, FVector NormalImpulse, const FHitResult& Hit);

	UFUNCTION()
	void OnCollisionOverlap(UPrimitiveComponent* OverlappedComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp, int32 OtherBodyIndex, bool bFromSweep, const FHitResult& SweepResult);

	/** True if Other is something this projectile should damage now (hostile, alive, has attributes, not hit before). */
	bool IsValidTarget(const AActor* Other) const;
	/** Damages (or explodes on) a valid target, then finishes unless it pierces. */
	void HitTarget(AActor* Other, const FVector& ImpactPoint);
	void Explode(const FVector& Origin);
	/** Broadcasts OnImpact once and destroys the projectile. */
	void Finish(AActor* HitActor, const FVector& Location);

	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<USphereComponent> Collision;
	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<UProjectileMovementComponent> Movement;
	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<UBillboardComponent> Sprite;
	UPROPERTY(VisibleAnywhere) TObjectPtr<UStaticMeshComponent> Mesh;

	UPROPERTY() FGBProjectileConfig Config;
	FVector StartLocation = FVector::ZeroVector;
	bool bFinished = false;
	/** Actors already damaged (pierce: each one is hit once). */
	TArray<TWeakObjectPtr<AActor>> DamagedActors;
};
// Placeholder pixel art generated from text (Sprites.json): textures for HUD sprites and world billboards.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Subsystems/GameInstanceSubsystem.h"
#include "Data/GBDataTypes.h"
#include "GBPixelSpriteLibrary.generated.h"

class UTexture2D;
class UBillboardComponent;

/**
 * Art rule (GDD 3): everything medieval / magic is pixel art. Until the artists deliver, sprites are drawn as
 * rows of characters in Sprites.json and turned into small textures here (nearest filtering, no mips, no
 * compression), so placeholders are text in git and need no asset import. Real art later replaces a sprite id
 * with an imported texture without touching the callers.
 */
UCLASS()
class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBPixelSpriteLibrary : public UGameInstanceSubsystem
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	static UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject);

	/**
	 * The texture of a sprite id, built on first use and cached. Unknown ids return a small magenta checker
	 * (never null), so a missing sprite is visible instead of crashing.
	 */
	UTexture2D* GetSprite(FName SpriteId);

	/** Size in pixels of a sprite (0,0 if unknown). */
	FIntPoint GetSpriteSize(FName SpriteId) const;

	/**
	 * Sets up a billboard as a camera-facing world sprite: texture, visible in game, world size so that the
	 * sprite is WorldHeight cm tall. Tint is not supported by billboards; use different sprites for colors.
	 */
	void SetupBillboard(UBillboardComponent* Billboard, FName SpriteId, float WorldHeight);

	/** Pure conversion, also used by the tests: rows + palette -> BGRA pixels. Returns false if the rows are empty or ragged. */
	static bool BuildPixels(const FGBSpriteDefinition& Definition, TArray<FColor>& OutPixels, int32& OutWidth, int32& OutHeight);

	/** Drops the cache (after a data reload). */
	void ClearCache();

protected:
	virtual void Initialize(FSubsystemCollectionBase& Collection) override;

	UTexture2D* CreateTexture(const TArray<FColor>& Pixels, int32 Width, int32 Height, FName DebugName);

	UPROPERTY() TMap<FName, TObjectPtr<UTexture2D>> Cache;
	UPROPERTY() TObjectPtr<UTexture2D> MissingTexture;
};

#include "Player/GBInputSet.h"

#include "InputAction.h"
#include "InputMappingContext.h"
#include "InputModifiers.h"
#include "InputCoreTypes.h"

namespace
{
	// Stick dead zones (input feel, not gameplay numbers).
	constexpr float GameplayStickDeadZone = 0.2f;
	constexpr float MenuStickDeadZone = 0.5f;

	/** Where a digital key pushes a 2D axis. */
	enum class EDir2D : uint8 { Right, Left, Up, Down };

	UInputAction* MakeAction(UObject* Outer, const TCHAR* Name, EInputActionValueType Type, bool bTriggerWhenPaused = false)
	{
		UInputAction* Action = NewObject<UInputAction>(Outer, MakeUniqueObjectName(Outer, UInputAction::StaticClass(), FName(Name)));
		Action->ValueType = Type;
		Action->bTriggerWhenPaused = bTriggerWhenPaused;
		return Action;
	}

	UInputModifierNegate* MakeNegate(UObject* Outer)
	{
		return NewObject<UInputModifierNegate>(Outer);
	}

	UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* MakeSwizzleYXZ(UObject* Outer)
	{
		UInputModifierSwizzleAxis* Swizzle = NewObject<UInputModifierSwizzleAxis>(Outer);
		Swizzle->Order = EInputAxisSwizzle::YXZ;
		return Swizzle;
	}

	UInputModifierDeadZone* MakeDeadZone(UObject* Outer, float Lower)
	{
		UInputModifierDeadZone* DeadZone = NewObject<UInputModifierDeadZone>(Outer);
		DeadZone->LowerThreshold = Lower;
		DeadZone->UpperThreshold = 1.f;
		DeadZone->Type = EDeadZoneType::Radial;
		return DeadZone;
	}

	// MapKey returns a reference into the context's array: every helper adds its modifiers before the next MapKey.

	void MapButton(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key)
	{
		Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
	}

	/** Axis1D: +1 or -1. */
	void MapAxis1D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, bool bNegative = false)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (bNegative)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a digital key: x = right, y = up / forward. */
	void MapDir2D(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, EDir2D Dir)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Up || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeSwizzleYXZ(Context));
		}
		if (Dir == EDir2D::Left || Dir == EDir2D::Down)
		{
			Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeNegate(Context));
		}
	}

	/** Axis2D from a stick, with a radial dead zone. */
	void MapStick(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action, const FKey& Key, float DeadZone)
	{
		FEnhancedActionKeyMapping& Mapping = Context->MapKey(Action, Key);
		Mapping.Modifiers.Add(MakeDeadZone(Context, DeadZone));
	}

	void MapWASD(UInputMappingContext* Context, UInputAction* Action)
	{
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::W, EDir2D::Up);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::S, EDir2D::Down);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::A, EDir2D::Left);
		MapDir2D(Context, Action, EKeys::D, EDir2D::Right);
	}
}

void UGBInputSet::Build()
{
	if (GameplayContext)
	{
		return; // built once per player
	}

	using EType = EInputActionValueType;
#include "World/GBPortal.h"

#include "Components/SphereComponent.h"
#include "Components/StaticMeshComponent.h"
#include "Engine/CollisionProfile.h"
#include "Core/GBPlaceholder.h"
#include "Player/GBPlayerCharacter.h"
#include "World/GBEraSubsystem.h"

namespace GBPortalLocal
{
	// Frame layout (cm), visuals only. The opening faces +X / -X; pillars stand on +Y / -Y.
	const FVector PillarSize(40.f, 40.f, 360.f);
	constexpr float PillarOffsetY = 140.f;
	const FVector LintelSize(40.f, 320.f, 40.f);
	const FVector SwirlSize(40.f, 240.f, 320.f);
	constexpr float PortalInteractRadius = 150.f;
	const FName PortalSpriteId(TEXT("portal"));
	// Cold cyan leads to the future, warm orange leads to the past.
	const FLinearColor ToFutureColor(0.15f, 0.85f, 1.f);
	const FLinearColor ToPastColor(1.f, 0.55f, 0.15f);

	UStaticMeshComponent* MakeFramePiece(AActor* Owner, USceneComponent* Parent, const TCHAR* Name)
	{
		UStaticMeshComponent* Piece = Owner->CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(Name);
		Piece->SetupAttachment(Parent);
		Piece->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
		Piece->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
		Piece->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
		Piece->SetCastShadow(false);
		return Piece;
	}
}

AGBPortal::AGBPortal()
{
	PillarLeft = GBPortalLocal::MakeFramePiece(this, Root, TEXT("PillarLeft"));
	PillarLeft->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, -GBPortalLocal::PillarOffsetY, GBPortalLocal::PillarSize.Z * 0.5f));
	PillarRight = GBPortalLocal::MakeFramePiece(this, Root, TEXT("PillarRight"));
	PillarRight->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, GBPortalLocal::PillarOffsetY, GBPortalLocal::PillarSize.Z * 0.5f));
	Lintel = GBPortalLocal::MakeFramePiece(this, Root, TEXT("Lintel"));
	Lintel->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBPortalLocal::PillarSize.Z + GBPortalLocal::LintelSize.Z * 0.5f));

	InteractVolume->InitSphereRadius(GBPortalLocal::PortalInteractRadius);
}

void AGBPortal::BeginPlay()
{
	Super::BeginPlay();

	SetPlaceholderLook(true, GBPortalLocal::PortalSpriteId, FLinearColor::White, GBPortalLocal::SwirlSize);
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(PillarLeft, EGBShape::Cube, GBPortalLocal::PillarSize, GBPortalLocal::ToFutureColor, true);
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(PillarRight, EGBShape::Cube, GBPortalLocal::PillarSize, GBPortalLocal::ToFutureColor, true);
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Lintel, EGBShape::Cube, GBPortalLocal::LintelSize, GBPortalLocal::ToFutureColor, true);

	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		EraChangedHandle = Era->OnEraChanged.AddUObject(this, &AGBPortal::UpdateColor);
		UpdateColor(Era->GetCurrentEra());
	}
}

void AGBPortal::EndPlay(const EEndPlayReason::Type EndPlayReason)
{
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->OnEraChanged.Remove(EraChangedHandle);
	}
	Super::EndPlay(EndPlayReason);
}

void AGBPortal::UpdateColor(EGBEra CurrentEra)
{
	const FLinearColor Color = UGBEraSubsystem::OtherEra(CurrentEra) == EGBEra::Future ? GBPortalLocal::ToFutureColor : GBPortalLocal::ToPastColor;
	GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(PillarLeft, Color);
	GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(PillarRight, Color);
	GBPlaceholder::SetShapeColor(Lintel, Color);
}

EGBEra AGBPortal::GetTargetEra() const
{
	const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this);
	return UGBEraSubsystem::OtherEra(Era ? Era->GetCurrentEra() : EGBEra::Past);
}

bool AGBPortal::CanInteract(const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this);
	return Super::CanInteract(Player) && Era && !Era->IsTravelling() && Player && !Player->IsDead();
}

void AGBPortal::Interact(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
{
	if (!CanInteract(Player))
	{
		return;
	}
	if (UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
		Era->RequestTravel(GetTargetEra(), Player, bTeleport, Destination);
	}
}

FString AGBPortal::GetInteractPrompt(const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	return GetTargetEra() == EGBEra::Future ? TEXT("Travel to the future") : TEXT("Travel to the past");
}
#include "World/GBInteractable.h"

#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
#include "Components/SceneComponent.h"

namespace GBInteractableLocal
{
	// Size of the sphere the player's interact query finds (cm). Collision helper, not a gameplay number.
	constexpr float DefaultInteractRadius = 80.f;
}

AGBInteractableActor::AGBInteractableActor()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = false;

	Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	RootComponent = Root;

	Mesh = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Mesh"));
	Mesh->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Mesh->SetMobility(EComponentMobility::Movable);
	Mesh->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
	Mesh->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Mesh->SetVisibility(false);

	Sprite = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBillboardComponent>(TEXT("Sprite"));
	Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Sprite->SetHiddenInGame(true);

	InteractVolume = CreateDefaultSubobject<USphereComponent>(TEXT("InteractVolume"));
	InteractVolume->SetupAttachment(Root);
	InteractVolume->InitSphereRadius(GBInteractableLocal::DefaultInteractRadius);
	InteractVolume->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, GBInteractableLocal::DefaultInteractRadius));
	InteractVolume->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::QueryOnly);
	InteractVolume->SetCollisionObjectType(ECC_WorldDynamic);
	InteractVolume->SetCollisionResponseToAllChannels(ECR_Overlap);
	InteractVolume->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(true);
	InteractVolume->SetCanEverAffectNavigation(false);

	EraComponent = CreateDefaultSubobject<UGBEraComponent>(TEXT("Era"));
}

bool AGBInteractableActor::CanInteract(const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player) const
{
	return !EraComponent || EraComponent->IsPresent();
}

void AGBInteractableActor::SetPlaceholderLook(bool bPixelArt, FName SpriteId, const FLinearColor& Color, const FVector& Size)
{
	const float HalfHeight = Size.Z * 0.5f;

	// The box is set up when it is seen (3D look) or when it must block (solid pixel-art things keep an invisible box).
	if (!bPixelArt || bSolid)
	{
		Mesh->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, HalfHeight));
		GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Mesh, EGBShape::Cube, Size, Color, false);
		Mesh->SetVisibility(!bPixelArt);
		Mesh->SetCollisionProfileName(bSolid ? UCollisionProfile::BlockAll_ProfileName : UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
	}
	else
	{
		Mesh->SetVisibility(false);
		Mesh->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::NoCollision_ProfileName);
	}

	if (bPixelArt)
	{
		Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, HalfHeight));
		if (UGBPixelSpriteLibrary* Library = UGBPixelSpriteLibrary::Get(this))
		{
			Library->SetupBillboard(Sprite, SpriteId, Size.Z);
		}
		else
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("%s: no pixel sprite library, sprite %s not shown."), *GetName(), *SpriteId.ToString());
		}
		Sprite->SetHiddenInGame(false);
		Sprite->SetVisibility(true);
	}
	else
	{
		Sprite->SetHiddenInGame(true);
	}

	InteractVolume->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, 0.f, FMath::Max(HalfHeight, InteractVolume->GetUnscaledSphereRadius() * 0.5f)));
}

// Menus of AGBHUD: pause, inventory (items, spells, skills), skill trees, death screen. Keyboard / gamepad only.
#include "UI/GBHUD.h"

namespace GBHudMenuLocal
{
	const FLinearColor MenuWhite(1.f, 1.f, 1.f, 1.f);
	const FLinearColor MenuDim(0.f, 0.f, 0.f, 0.6f);
	const FLinearColor MenuGrey(0.55f, 0.55f, 0.55f, 1.f);

	int32 Wrap(int32 Value, int32 Num)
	{
		return Num > 0 ? ((Value % Num) + Num) % Num : 0;
	}

	const TCHAR* SlotKey(int32 Slot)
	{
		static const TCHAR* Keys[] = { TEXT("RMB"), TEXT("1"), TEXT("2"), TEXT("3"), TEXT("4") };
		return (Slot >= 0 && Slot < (int32)UE_ARRAY_COUNT(Keys)) ? Keys[Slot] : TEXT("?");
	}

	FString ItemTypeName(EGBItemType Type)
	{
		switch (Type)
		{
		case EGBItemType::Consumable: return TEXT("Consumable");
		case EGBItemType::Throwable: return TEXT("Throwable");
		case EGBItemType::Armor: return TEXT("Armor");
		case EGBItemType::KeyItem: return TEXT("Key item");
		case EGBItemType::Material: return TEXT("Material");
		case EGBItemType::Ammo: return TEXT("Ammo");
		}
		return FString();
	}

	FString ItemName(const UGBDataSubsystem* Data, FName ItemId)
	{
		const FGBItemDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindItem(ItemId) : nullptr;
		return (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : ItemId.ToString();
	}

	FString SkillName(const UGBDataSubsystem* Data, FName NodeId)
	{
		const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr;
		return (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : NodeId.ToString();
	}

	FString SkillResultText(const UGBDataSubsystem* Data, FName NodeId, EGBSkillUnlockResult Result)
	{
		switch (Result)
		{
		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::Ok: return TEXT("Can be unlocked");
		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::MaxRank: return TEXT("Max rank reached");
		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::NotEnoughPoints: return TEXT("Not enough skill points");
		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::UnknownNode: return TEXT("Unknown skill");
		case EGBSkillUnlockResult::MissingRequirement:
		{
			FString Names;
			if (const FGBSkillNodeDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindSkillNode(NodeId) : nullptr)
			{
				for (const FName& Req : Def->Requires)
				{
					Names += (Names.IsEmpty() ? TEXT("") : TEXT(", ")) + SkillName(Data, Req);
				}
			}
			return Names.IsEmpty() ? FString(TEXT("Unlock the required skills first")) : FString::Printf(TEXT("Requires: %s"), *Names);
		}
		}
		return FString();
	}

	bool IsWeaponTree(FName Tree)
	{
		// FName comparison ignores case.
		return Tree == FName(TEXT("Weapon")) || Tree == FName(TEXT("Weapons"));
	}
}

// ======================================================================== state and input

AGBPlayerController* AGBHUD::GetGBController() const
{
	return Cast<AGBPlayerController>(GetOwningPlayerController());
}

EGBMenu AGBHUD::GetOpenMenu() const
{
	const AGBPlayerController* PC = GetGBController();
	return PC ? PC->GetOpenMenu() : EGBMenu::None;
}

void AGBHUD::OnMenuOpened(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	MenuState = FMenuState();
	if (Menu == EGBMenu::SkillTrees)
	{
		const TArray<FSkillNodeRef> Nodes = GetSkillNodes();
		MenuState.SkillCursor = Nodes.Num() > 0 ? Nodes[0].Id : NAME_None;
	}
}

void AGBHUD::OnMenuClosed(EGBMenu Menu)
{
	MenuState = FMenuState();
}

void AGBHUD::MenuNavigate(FIntPoint Direction)
{
	using namespace GBHudMenuLocal;
	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayer();
	switch (GetOpenMenu())
	{
	case EGBMenu::Pause:
		if (Direction.Y != 0)
		{
			MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, 2);
		}
		break;
	case EGBMenu::SkillTrees:
		NavigateSkills(Direction);
		break;
	case EGBMenu::Inventory:
		if (MenuState.Page == PageSkills)
		{
			NavigateSkills(Direction);
		}
		else if (MenuState.Page == PageSpells)
		{
			if (Direction.Y == 0)
			{
				break;
			}
			if (MenuState.bChoosingSpell)
			{
				MenuState.SubCursor = Wrap(MenuState.SubCursor + Direction.Y, GetAssignableSpells(Player).Num());
			}
			else
			{
				MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, UGBSpellbookComponent::NumSlots + 1);
			}
		}
		else if (Direction.Y != 0)
		{
			MenuState.Cursor = Wrap(MenuState.Cursor + Direction.Y, GetInventoryList(Player).Num());
		}
		break;
	default:
		break;
	}
}

		if (MenuState.Cursor == 0)
		{
			if (AGBPlayerController* PC = GetGBController())

	if (AGBChest* Chest = World->SpawnActor<AGBChest>(AGBChest::StaticClass(), GBArena::StartChest, FRotator(0.f, GBArena::StartChestYaw, 0.f), GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams()))
	{
		const FGBDropDefinition Contents[] = { MakeWeaponDrop(GBArenaIds::StaffEmberId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::SwordHiltId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::GemFireId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::GemIceId) };
		Chest->Configure(Contents, GBArenaIds::StartChestFlag);
	}

	// ---- Central portal, present in both eras; the player stays in place and changes time ----
	World->SpawnActor<AGBPortal>(AGBPortal::StaticClass(), GBArena::Portal, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());

	// ---- Movement course reward on the landing block ----
	{
		const FVector2D& LastStep = GBArena::Steps[UE_ARRAY_COUNT(GBArena::Steps) - 1];
		const float LandingX = LastStep.X + GBArena::StepSize + GBArena::DashGap;
		AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(World, MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::AmmoId), FVector(LandingX, GBArena::CourseY, LastStep.Y + 10.f));
	}

	// ---- Encounters ----
	AGBEncounter* PastEncounter = World->SpawnActor<AGBEncounter>(AGBEncounter::StaticClass(), GBArena::PastWave, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams());
	if (PastEncounter)
	{
		PastEncounter->Configure(GBArenaIds::PastWaveId, EGBEraPresence::PastOnly, GBArena::EncounterTrigger, GBArena::EncounterSpawns);
	}
	if (AGBEncounter* FutureEncounter = World->SpawnActor<AGBEncounter>(AGBEncounter::StaticClass(), GBArena::FutureWave, FRotator::ZeroRotator, GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams()))
	{
		FutureEncounter->Configure(GBArenaIds::FutureWaveId, EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly, GBArena::EncounterTrigger, GBArena::EncounterSpawns);
	}

	// ---- Past-to-future unlock: a future-only ramp to the raised platform, once the past wave is cleared ----
	FName PastClearedFlag = PastEncounter ? PastEncounter->GetClearedFlag() : NAME_None;
	if (PastClearedFlag.IsNone())
	{
		PastClearedFlag = FName(*FString::Printf(TEXT("Encounter.%s.Cleared"), *GBArenaIds::PastWaveId.ToString()));
	}
	{
		const float Run = GBArena::PlatformCenter.X - GBArena::PlatformSize.X * 0.5f - GBArena::RampStartX;
		const float Rise = GBArena::PlatformTop;
		const float Length = FMath::Sqrt(Run * Run + Rise * Rise) + GBArena::RampThickness;
		const float PitchRad = FMath::Atan2(Rise, Run);
		const FRotator RampRotation(FMath::RadiansToDegrees(PitchRad), 0.f, 0.f);
		// The top surface goes from the foot (RampStartX, 0) to the platform edge; the box center sits half a thickness below it.
		const FVector Up = RampRotation.RotateVector(FVector::UpVector);
		const FVector SurfaceMid(GBArena::RampStartX + Run * 0.5f, GBArena::RampY, Rise * 0.5f);
		const FVector RampCenter = SurfaceMid - Up * (GBArena::RampThickness * 0.5f);
		AStaticMeshActor* Ramp = SpawnShape(EGBShape::Cube, RampCenter, FVector(Length, GBArena::RampWidth, GBArena::RampThickness), GBArena::Metal, false, true, RampRotation);
		SetPresence(Ramp, EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly, PastClearedFlag);
		const FVector Side = RampRotation.RotateVector(FVector::RightVector) * (GBArena::RampWidth * 0.5f - 6.f);
		for (const float Sign : { -1.f, 1.f })
		{
			AStaticMeshActor* Strip = SpawnGlow(SurfaceMid + Up * 2.f + Side * Sign, FVector(Length, 8.f, 4.f), GBArena::NeonCyan, RampRotation);
			SetPresence(Strip, EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly, PastClearedFlag);
		}
	}
	if (AGBChest* PlatformChestActor = World->SpawnActor<AGBChest>(AGBChest::StaticClass(), GBArena::PlatformChest, FRotator(0.f, GBArena::PlatformChestYaw, 0.f), GBArenaIds::AlwaysSpawnParams()))
	{
		const FGBDropDefinition Contents[] = { MakeWeaponDrop(GBArenaIds::RifleId), MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::ArmorId) };
		PlatformChestActor->Configure(Contents, GBArenaIds::PlatformChestFlag);
	}

	// ---- Era-gated room: the doorway is walled up in the past, open in the future ----
	AStaticMeshActor* DoorBlock = SpawnBlock(FVector(GBArena::DoorCenterX, GBArena::RoomNorthY, GBArena::WallHeight * 0.5f), FVector(GBArena::DoorWidth, GBArena::WallThickness, GBArena::WallHeight), GBArena::Stone);
	SetPresence(DoorBlock, EGBEraPresence::PastOnly);
	AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(World, MakeWeaponDrop(GBArenaIds::ShotgunId), GBArena::RoomShotgun);
	for (const FVector& AmmoLocation : GBArena::RoomAmmo)
	{
		AGBPickup::SpawnPickup(World, MakeItemDrop(GBArenaIds::AmmoId), AmmoLocation);
	}
}

void AGBTestArena::BuildPastDecor()
{
	const EGBEraPresence Past = EGBEraPresence::PastOnly;

	// Stone pillars with a brazier on top.
	for (const FVector2D& Column : GBArena::Columns)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnShape(EGBShape::Cylinder, FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight * 0.5f), FVector(100.f, 100.f, GBArena::ColumnHeight), GBArena::Stone), Past);
		SetPresence(SpawnShape(EGBShape::Sphere, FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight + 25.f), FVector(45.f, 45.f, 45.f), GBArena::Flame, true, false), Past);
		SetPresence(SpawnPointLight(FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight + 100.f), GBArena::TorchLight, GBArena::TorchCandelas, GBArena::TorchRadius), Past);
	}

	// Wall torches.
	for (const FVector& Torch : GBArena::Torches)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnShape(EGBShape::Cylinder, Torch, FVector(14.f, 14.f, 70.f), GBArena::TorchWood, false, false), Past);
		SetPresence(SpawnGlow(Torch + FVector(0.f, 0.f, 45.f), FVector(18.f, 18.f, 26.f), GBArena::Flame), Past);
		SetPresence(SpawnPointLight(Torch + FVector(0.f, 0.f, 80.f), GBArena::TorchLight, GBArena::TorchCandelas, GBArena::TorchRadius), Past);
	}

	// Crates and low stone walls.
	for (const FVector& CrateLocation : GBArena::Crates)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnBlock(CrateLocation, FVector(100.f, 100.f, 100.f), GBArena::Crate), Past);
	}
	SetPresence(SpawnBlock(GBArena::LowWallCenter, GBArena::LowWallSize, GBArena::Stone), Past);
	SetPresence(SpawnBlock(GBArena::LowWall2Center, GBArena::LowWall2Size, GBArena::Stone), Past);
}

void AGBTestArena::BuildFutureDecor()
{
	const EGBEraPresence Future = EGBEraPresence::FutureOnly;

	// Dark metal columns with neon strips where the past pillars stand.
	for (const FVector2D& Column : GBArena::Columns)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnBlock(FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight * 0.5f), FVector(120.f, 120.f, GBArena::ColumnHeight), GBArena::Metal), Future);
		for (const float Sign : { -1.f, 1.f })
		{
			SetPresence(SpawnGlow(FVector(Column.X + Sign * 62.f, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight * 0.5f), FVector(6.f, 20.f, GBArena::ColumnHeight - 40.f), GBArena::NeonCyan), Future);
		}
		SetPresence(SpawnPointLight(FVector(Column.X, Column.Y, GBArena::ColumnHeight - 50.f), GBArena::NeonCyan, GBArena::NeonCandelas, GBArena::NeonRadius), Future);
	}

	// Containers where the crates were, with a magenta neon line on top.
	for (const FVector& Container : GBArena::Containers)
	{
		SetPresence(SpawnBlock(Container, FVector(240.f, 120.f, 130.f), GBArena::Metal), Future);
		SetPresence(SpawnGlow(Container + FVector(0.f, 0.f, 67.f), FVector(240.f, 10.f, 6.f), GBArena::NeonMagenta), Future);
		SetPresence(SpawnPointLight(Container + FVector(0.f, 0.f, 150.f), GBArena::NeonMagenta, GBArena::NeonCandelas, GBArena::NeonRadius), Future);

void AGBGun::StopSecondary()
{
	SetAiming(false);
}

void AGBGun::SetAiming(bool bInAiming)
{
	if (bAiming == bInAiming)
	{
		return;
	}
	bAiming = bInAiming;
	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner())
	{
		Player->SetMoveSpeedScale(bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimMoveSpeedMult, 1.f) : 1.f);
	}
}

void AGBGun::UpdateAimFOV(float DeltaSeconds, bool bInstant)
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	UCameraComponent* Camera = Player ? Player->GetCamera() : nullptr;
	if (!Camera)
	{
		return;
	}

	const float Target = bAiming ? 1.f : 0.f;
	if (AimAlpha == Target)
	{
		return;
	}
	if (AimAlpha <= 0.f)
	{
		HipFOV = Camera->FieldOfView;
	}

	if (bInstant)
	{
		AimAlpha = Target;
	}
	else
	{
		const float AimTime = GetStat(GBWeaponStat::AimTime, 0.15f);
		const float Step = AimTime > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? DeltaSeconds / AimTime : 1.f;
		AimAlpha = FMath::Clamp(Target > AimAlpha ? AimAlpha + Step : AimAlpha - Step, 0.f, 1.f);

void AGBProjectile::HitTarget(AActor* Other, const FVector& ImpactPoint)
{
	DamagedActors.Add(Other);

	if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
	{
		Explode(ImpactPoint);
	}
	else
	{
		FGBDamageInfo Info = Config.Damage;
		Info.Causer = this;
		Info.HitLocation = ImpactPoint;
		Info.HitDirection = Movement->Velocity.GetSafeNormal();
		if (const AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = Cast<AGBEnemyCharacter>(Other))
		{
			Info.bHeadshot = Enemy->IsHeadshotLocation(ImpactPoint);
		}
		UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Other, Info);
	}

	// Neutral things with attributes (ice walls, destructibles) stop the shot even when it pierces.
	const bool bHitNeutral = UGBCombatLibrary::GetTeam(Other) == EGBTeam::Neutral;
	if (!Config.bPierce || bHitNeutral)
	{
		Finish(Other, ImpactPoint);
	}
}

void AGBProjectile::OnCollisionOverlap(UPrimitiveComponent* OverlappedComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp,
	int32 OtherBodyIndex, bool bFromSweep, const FHitResult& SweepResult)
{
	if (bFinished || !IsValidTarget(OtherActor))
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector ImpactPoint = (bFromSweep && !SweepResult.bStartPenetrating) ? FVector(SweepResult.ImpactPoint) : GetActorLocation();
	HitTarget(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
}

void AGBProjectile::OnCollisionHit(UPrimitiveComponent* HitComponent, AActor* OtherActor, UPrimitiveComponent* OtherComp,
	FVector NormalImpulse, const FHitResult& Hit)
{
	if (bFinished)
	{
		return;
	}

	const FVector ImpactPoint = Hit.ImpactPoint;

	if (IsValidTarget(OtherActor))
	{
		HitTarget(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
		return;
	}

	// The world: grenades keep bouncing until the fuse ends.
	if (Config.FuseTime > 0.f)
	{
		return;
	}

	if (Config.ExplosionRadius > 0.f)
	{
		Explode(ImpactPoint);
	}
	Finish(OtherActor, ImpactPoint);
}

void AGBProjectile::Explode(const FVector& Origin)
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info = Config.Damage;
	Info.Causer = this;
	UGBCombatLibrary::DealRadialDamage(this, Origin, Config.ExplosionRadius, Info, TConstArrayView<AActor*>(), Config.bExplosionFalloff);
}

void AGBProjectile::Finish(AActor* HitActor, const FVector& Location)
{
	if (bFinished)
	{
		return;
	}
	bFinished = true;

	if (Movement)
	{
		Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
	}
	OnImpact.Broadcast(this, HitActor, Location);
	Destroy();
}

	for (const FOverlapResult& Overlap : Overlaps)
	{
		AActor* Target = Overlap.GetActor();
		if (!IsValid(Target) || Visited.Contains(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}
		Visited.Add(Target);

		if (IgnoreActors.Contains(Target) || !UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		if (!CanDamage(DamageInstigator, Target))
		{
			continue;
		}

		const FVector TargetLocation = Target->GetActorLocation();
		const float Distance = FVector::Dist(TargetLocation, Origin);
		if (Distance > Radius)
		{
			continue;
		}

		FGBDamageInfo TargetInfo = Info;
		TargetInfo.HitLocation = TargetLocation;
		TargetInfo.HitDirection = (TargetLocation - Origin).GetSafeNormal();
		if (bLinearFalloff)
		{
			TargetInfo.Amount *= FMath::Clamp(1.f - Distance / Radius, 0.f, 1.f);
		}

		if (DealDamage(Target, TargetInfo) > 0.f)
		{
			++DamagedCount;
		}
	}

	return DamagedCount;
}

EGBTeam UGBCombatLibrary::GetTeam(const AActor* Actor)
	return true;
}

void UGBQTEComponent::CancelQTE()
{
	if (!bActive)
	{
		return;
	}
	Session.Phase = EGBQTEPhase::Finished;
	Finish();
}

void UGBQTEComponent::HandleKey(EGBQTEKey Key)
{
	if (!bActive)
	{
		return;
	}
	Session.PressKey(Key);
{
	bControlEnabled = bEnabled;
	if (!bEnabled)
	{
		ReleaseHeldInputs();
		EndDash();
	}
	if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Equipment ? Equipment->GetActiveItem() : nullptr)
	{
		Item->SetActorHiddenInGame(!bEnabled);
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::HandleDeath(const FGBDamageInfo& KillingBlow)
{
	SetControlEnabled(false);
	Spellbook->CancelSustained();
	QTE->CancelQTE();
	if (AGBGameMode* GameMode = AGBGameMode::Get(this))
	{
		GameMode->HandlePlayerDeath(this);
	}
	else
	{
		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GBPlayerCharacter: died without an AGBGameMode, nothing will respawn the player."));
	}
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::SaveToSnapshot(FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot) const
{
	Snapshot.bValid = true;
	Snapshot.Transform = GetActorTransform();
	Snapshot.ControlRotation = Controller ? Controller->GetControlRotation() : GetActorRotation();
	if (const UGBEraSubsystem* Era = UGBEraSubsystem::Get(this))
	{
void UGBEraComponent::ApplyPresence(bool bNewPresent)
{
	AActor* Owner = GetOwner();
	if (!Owner || bNewPresent == bPresent)
	{
		return;
	}
	bPresent = bNewPresent;

	APawn* Pawn = Cast<APawn>(Owner);
	UMovementComponent* Movement = Pawn ? Pawn->GetMovementComponent() : nullptr;

	if (!bNewPresent)
	{
		// Remember the owner's own settings (it may have turned collision or tick off itself).
		bSavedHidden = Owner->IsHidden();
		bSavedCollision = Owner->GetActorEnableCollision();
		bSavedTick = Owner->IsActorTickEnabled();
		bHasSaved = true;

		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(false);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(false);
		// An enemy of the other era must neither fall (its collision is off) nor walk.
		if (Movement)
		{
			Movement->StopMovementImmediately();
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(false);
		}
	}
	else
	{
		Owner->SetActorHiddenInGame(bHasSaved ? bSavedHidden : false);
		Owner->SetActorEnableCollision(bHasSaved ? bSavedCollision : true);
		Owner->SetActorTickEnabled(bHasSaved ? bSavedTick : true);
		if (Movement)
		{
			Movement->SetComponentTickEnabled(true);
		}
	}

	OnPresenceChanged.Broadcast();
}

#include "Spells/GBSpell_Wall.h"
#include "Components/BillboardComponent.h"
