namespace
{
	// Visual and geometric limits only.
	constexpr int32 MaxWallSprites = 8;
	/** Pawns pressed against a blocking wall still count as touching it for damage ticks. */
	constexpr float WallTouchMarginCm = 15.f;
	constexpr float WallGroundSearchExtraCm = 2000.f;
}

AGBSpell_Wall::AGBSpell_Wall()
{
	Box = CreateDefaultSubobject<UBoxComponent>(TEXT("Box"));
	Box->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Box->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Box->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);

	Visual = CreateDefaultSubobject<UStaticMeshComponent>(TEXT("Visual"));
	Visual->SetupAttachment(Root);
	Visual->SetCollisionEnabled(ECollisionEnabled::NoCollision);
	Visual->SetGenerateOverlapEvents(false);
	Visual->SetCastShadow(false);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::StartSpell()
{
	const float Distance = ReadParam(GBSpellParam::Distance, 300.f);
	const float Width = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Width, 300.f));
	const float Height = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Height, 200.f));
	const float Thickness = FMath::Max(1.f, ReadParam(GBStat::Thickness, 50.f));
	const float Duration = ReadParam(GBStat::Duration, 5.f);
	const bool bBlocking = SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::Blocking, 0.f) > 0.5f;

	// Stand on the ground in front of the caster, facing the caster's yaw.
	AActor* Caster = GetCaster();
	const FVector CasterLocation = Caster ? Caster->GetActorLocation() : SpellCast.Origin;
	const FRotator WallRotation(0.f, SpellCast.Direction.Rotation().Yaw, 0.f);
	const FVector Point = CasterLocation + WallRotation.Vector() * Distance;
	FVector Ground;
	if (!FindGround(Point + FVector::UpVector * Height, Ground, Height + WallGroundSearchExtraCm))
	{
		Ground = Point;
		Ground.Z = CasterLocation.Z - (Caster ? Caster->GetSimpleCollisionHalfHeight() : 0.f);
	}
	SetActorLocationAndRotation(Ground + FVector::UpVector * (Height * 0.5f), WallRotation, false, nullptr, ETeleportType::TeleportPhysics);

	const FVector Size(Thickness, Width, Height);
	Box->SetBoxExtent(Size * 0.5f);
	GBPlaceholder::SetupShape(Visual, EGBShape::Cube, Size, GetSpellColor(), true);

	// A row of camera-facing sprites along the wall (pixel-art flames / ice).
	if (!GetSpriteId().IsNone())
	{
		const float SpriteHeight = FMath::Max(1.f, SpellCast.Get(GBSpellParam::SpriteSize, Height));
		const int32 Count = FMath::Clamp(FMath::RoundToInt(Width / SpriteHeight), 1, MaxWallSprites);
		for (int32 Index = 0; Index < Count; ++Index)
		{
			UBillboardComponent* Sprite = NewObject<UBillboardComponent>(this);
			Sprite->SetupAttachment(Root);
			Sprite->RegisterComponent();
			AddInstanceComponent(Sprite);
			Sprite->SetRelativeLocation(FVector(0.f, -Width * 0.5f + (Index + 0.5f) * Width / Count, 0.f));
			SetupSpellSprite(Sprite, Height);
			Sprites.Add(Sprite);
		}
	}

	if (bBlocking)
	{
		Box->SetCollisionProfileName(UCollisionProfile::BlockAllDynamic_ProfileName);
		SetupHealth(ReadParam(GBStat::Health, 100.f));
	}

	const float DamagePerSecond = SpellCast.Get(GBStat::DamagePerSecond, 0.f);
	if (DamagePerSecond > 0.f)
	{
		const float Interval = SafeInterval(ReadParam(GBStat::TickInterval, 0.5f));
		DamagePerTick = DamagePerSecond * Interval;
		GetWorldTimerManager().SetTimer(TickTimer, this, &AGBSpell_Wall::DamageTick, Interval, true);
	}

	EndSpellAfter(Duration);
}

void AGBSpell_Wall::SetupHealth(float Health)
bool UGBCombatLibrary::AreHostile(const AActor* A, const AActor* B)
{
	if (!A || !B)
	{
		return false;
	}
	return AreTeamsHostile(GetTeam(A), GetTeam(B));
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::AreTeamsHostile(EGBTeam A, EGBTeam B)
{
	return (A == EGBTeam::Player && B == EGBTeam::Enemy) || (A == EGBTeam::Enemy && B == EGBTeam::Player);
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::IsAlive(const AActor* Actor)
{
	if (!IsValid(Actor))
	{
		return false;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* Attributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Actor);
	return !Attributes || !Attributes->IsDead();
}

bool UGBCombatLibrary::CanDamage(const AActor* Instigator, const AActor* Target)
{
	if (!IsValid(Target))
	{
		return false;
	}
	const UGBAttributeComponent* TargetAttributes = UGBAttributeComponent::Find(Target);
	if (!TargetAttributes || TargetAttributes->IsDead())
	{
		return false;
	}
	if (!IsValid(Instigator))
	{
		return true;
	}
	const EGBTeam InstigatorTeam = GetTeam(Instigator);
	const EGBTeam TargetTeam = TargetAttributes->Team;
	if (InstigatorTeam == EGBTeam::Neutral || TargetTeam == EGBTeam::Neutral)
	{
		return true;
	}
	return AreTeamsHostile(InstigatorTeam, TargetTeam);
}

float UGBCombatLibrary::DistanceFalloffMultiplier(float Distance, float FalloffStart, float FalloffEnd, float NearMult, float FarMult)
{
	if (Distance <= FalloffStart)
	{
		return NearMult;
	}
	if (FalloffEnd <= FalloffStart || Distance >= FalloffEnd)
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput())
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector2D Delta = Value.Get<FVector2D>(); // mouse: x right, y up
	if (IsQTEActive())
	{
		// Camera locked; the mouse draws the gesture in screen space (y down).
		QTE->HandleGestureInput(FVector2D(Delta.X, -Delta.Y) / MouseUnitsPerPixel);
		return;
	}
	AddLookDelta(static_cast<float>(Delta.X) * MouseDegreesPerUnit, static_cast<float>(Delta.Y) * MouseDegreesPerUnit);
}

void AGBPlayerCharacter::InputLookStick(const FInputActionValue& Value)
{
	if (!CanUseGameplayInput())
	{
		return;
	}
	const FVector2D Stick = Value.Get<FVector2D>(); // x right, y up
	if (IsQTEActive())
	{
		// Undilated: the world runs at 0.1 speed during the QTE, the gesture runs in real time.
		const float Scale = QTE->GetStickGestureScale();
		const float RealDelta = static_cast<float>(FApp::GetDeltaTime());
		QTE->HandleGestureInput(FVector2D(Stick.X, -Stick.Y) * Scale * RealDelta);
		return;
	}
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	// Real time: the QTE runs in real time while the world is slowed.
	const float RealDelta = World ? World->DeltaRealTimeSeconds : 0.f;
	AddLookDelta(static_cast<float>(Stick.X) * StickYawDegreesPerSecond * RealDelta, static_cast<float>(Stick.Y) * StickPitchDegreesPerSecond * RealDelta);
}
// The stat system: base values from data + stacked modifiers. One per actor that has tunable numbers.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Components/ActorComponent.h"
#include "Data/GBDataTypes.h"
#include "GBStatsComponent.generated.h"

/**
 * How every tunable number is read in the game:
 *   value = Evaluate(Key, Base)  ->  (Base + sum of Add) * product of Multiply, or the last Override if any.
 * - Actor stats (MoveSpeed, MaxHealth...): base values are stored here with SetBaseStats and read with GetStat.
 * - Weapon and spell numbers: the base comes from the weapon / spell definition and the caller passes the
 *   modifier keys ("Weapon.<Id>.<Stat>", "Weapon.All.<Stat>", "Spell.<Id>.<Param>", "Spell.All.<Param>").
 * Modifiers are added in groups identified by a Source name ("Skill.FireballDamage", "Armor", "Effect.Slow#3",
 * "Passive.DoubleJump"), and removed by that same name. Skill trees, armor, status effects, air passives and
 * the weapon enchant are all just sources of modifiers.
 */
UCLASS(ClassGroup = (Glyphbound), meta = (BlueprintSpawnableComponent))
class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBStatsComponent : public UActorComponent
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	UGBStatsComponent();

	/** The stats component of an actor, or null. */
	static UGBStatsComponent* Find(const AActor* Actor);

	/** Replaces the base stats (from Player.json / Enemies.json) and broadcasts OnStatsChanged. */
	void SetBaseStats(const TMap<FName, float>& InBaseStats);
	void SetBaseStat(FName Stat, float Value);
	bool HasBaseStat(FName Stat) const;

	/** Base stat of this actor with its modifiers applied. DefaultValue is the base when the stat is not in the data. */
	UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable, Category = "Glyphbound|Stats")
	float GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue = 0.f) const;

	/** Applies the modifiers registered under Key to an external base value. */
	float Evaluate(FName Key, float Base) const;

	/** Same, merging the modifiers of several keys (e.g. "Weapon.Pistol.Damage" and "Weapon.All.Damage"). */
	float EvaluateKeys(TConstArrayView<FName> Keys, float Base) const;

	/** Adds a group of modifiers. Calling it again with the same Source replaces that source's modifiers. */
	void AddModifiers(FName Source, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers, float ValueScale = 1.f);
	void RemoveModifiers(FName Source);
	bool HasModifierSource(FName Source) const;

	/** Pure math, also used by the tests: applies Modifiers (already filtered to one stat) to Base. */
	static float ApplyModifiers(float Base, TConstArrayView<FGBModifierDef> Modifiers);

	/** Base stats or modifiers changed: re-read whatever you cached (walk speed, max jumps...). */
	FSimpleMulticastDelegate OnStatsChanged;

protected:
	struct FSourcedModifier
	{
		FName Source;
		FGBModifierDef Modifier;
	};

	UPROPERTY()
	TMap<FName, float> BaseStats;

	TArray<FSourcedModifier> ActiveModifiers;
};
// Run progress that must outlive the pawn and the level: progress flags and the last checkpoint.
#pragma once

#include "CoreMinimal.h"
#include "Subsystems/GameInstanceSubsystem.h"
#include "Core/GBTypes.h"
#include "Weapons/GBHeldItem.h"
#include "GBProgressSubsystem.generated.h"

class AGBPlayerCharacter;

/**
 * Everything of the player that a checkpoint (or a map change to the final-boss dimension) must bring back.
 * Each player component fills / reads its own part with SaveToSnapshot / LoadFromSnapshot.
 */
USTRUCT()
struct GLYPHBOUND_API FGBPlayerSnapshot
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

	UPROPERTY() bool bValid = false;
	UPROPERTY() FTransform Transform;
	UPROPERTY() FRotator ControlRotation = FRotator::ZeroRotator;
	UPROPERTY() EGBEra Era = EGBEra::Past;
	UPROPERTY() float Health = 0.f;
	UPROPERTY() float Mana = 0.f;

	// Equipment
	/** Weapon ids held, any order (the slot comes from each definition). */
	UPROPERTY() TArray<FName> Weapons;
	UPROPERTY() TMap<FName, FGBAmmoState> Ammo;
	UPROPERTY() TMap<FName, EGBElement> WeaponElements;
	UPROPERTY() EGBHeldSlot ActiveSlot = EGBHeldSlot::Staff;

	// Spellbook
	UPROPERTY() TArray<FName> KnownSpells;
	UPROPERTY() TArray<FName> Loadout;
	UPROPERTY() EGBElement EnchantElement = EGBElement::Fire;

	// Inventory
	UPROPERTY() TMap<FName, int32> Items;
	UPROPERTY() FName QuickUseItem = NAME_None;
	UPROPERTY() FName EquippedArmor = NAME_None;

	// Skill trees
	UPROPERTY() int32 SkillPoints = 0;
	UPROPERTY() TMap<FName, int32> SkillRanks;
};

DECLARE_MULTICAST_DELEGATE_OneParam(FGBOnFlagChanged, FName /*Flag*/);

/**
 * Saving is checkpoints only, in memory (GDD 0.1): nothing is written to disk between sessions.
 *  - Flags: named booleans for everything that happened ("Encounter.Courtyard.Cleared", "GotFirstGun",
 *    "FinalPortalOpen"). UGBEraComponent, encounters and doors read them. Flags are NOT rolled back on death
 *    (enemies do not respawn; a cleared zone stays cleared).
 *  - Checkpoint: a FGBPlayerSnapshot taken automatically (portals, checkpoint volumes, encounter cleared).
 */
UCLASS()
class GLYPHBOUND_API UGBProgressSubsystem : public UGameInstanceSubsystem
{
	GENERATED_BODY()

public:
	static UGBProgressSubsystem* Get(const UObject* WorldContextObject);

	// ---- Flags ----
	bool HasFlag(FName Flag) const;
	/** Broadcasts OnFlagChanged if the value really changed. */
	void SetFlag(FName Flag, bool bValue = true);
	const TSet<FName>& GetFlags() const { return Flags; }

	// ---- Checkpoints ----
	/** Takes a snapshot of the player (all components + transform + era). */
	void SaveCheckpoint(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player);
	bool HasCheckpoint() const { return Checkpoint.bValid; }
	const FGBPlayerSnapshot& GetCheckpoint() const { return Checkpoint; }
	/** Puts the player back to the checkpoint state (also revives and switches era, no fade). False if there is none. */
	bool RestoreCheckpoint(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player);

	/** New run: clears flags and the checkpoint. */
	void ResetProgress();

	FGBOnFlagChanged OnFlagChanged;
	FSimpleMulticastDelegate OnCheckpointSaved;

protected:
	UPROPERTY() TSet<FName> Flags;
	UPROPERTY() FGBPlayerSnapshot Checkpoint;
};

1. **[Player/GBPlayerCharacter.cpp:541](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Player/GBPlayerCharacter.cpp:541), [World/GBChest.cpp:136](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/World/GBChest.cpp:136), [World/GBPickup.cpp:352](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/World/GBPickup.cpp:352)** — Death restores checkpoint inventory, weapons, and spell knowledge, but collected pickups remain destroyed and chest-open flags remain set. Opening the starting chest or collecting spellbooks, then dying before another checkpoint, permanently loses those rewards; save a checkpoint after irreversible acquisitions, or restore their world sources alongside the player.

2. **[Spells/GBSpell_Wall.cpp:26](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Spells/GBSpell_Wall.cpp:26), [Combat/GBProjectile.cpp:24](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Combat/GBProjectile.cpp:24)** — Ice walls use `WorldDynamic`, which projectiles overlap, but the wall disables overlap events. Staff bolts, enemy shots, and spell projectiles pass through without damaging the wall; enable overlap generation on blocking walls so the existing projectile impact handler runs.

3. **[QTE/GBQTEComponent.cpp:134](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/QTE/GBQTEComponent.cpp:134), [Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.cpp:487](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.cpp:487)** — `CancelQTE()` finishes using the accumulated score, so cancellation succeeds if any step was correct. Death calls this after cancelling sustained spells, allowing a new projectile, wall, or shield to spawn from the dead player; make cancellation return an unsuccessful result and reject cast completion when the owner is dead.

4. **[World/GBEraSubsystem.cpp:172](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/World/GBEraSubsystem.cpp:172)** — Travel continues if the player dies during the fade, then `RestoreAll()` revives them and saves over the checkpoint while the death menu and scheduled respawn remain active. The player can revive inside an ongoing death flow and then be restored again; cancel pending travel on death and guard restoration against a dead traveller.

5. **[World/GBEraComponent.cpp:108](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/World/GBEraComponent.cpp:108)** — Hiding an enemy disables its actor tick and movement tick, but leaves attribute and status-effect components ticking. Burning enemies can die, drop rewards, and advance encounters while their era is inactive, and regeneration continues invisibly; suspend those component ticks while absent and restore their previous settings on return.

6. **[Combat/GBCombatLibrary.cpp:126](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Combat/GBCombatLibrary.cpp:126)** — Radial damage checks distance but never checks obstruction. Fireballs, grenades, and runes damage enemies through solid walls and ice-wall cover; add an obstruction trace before applying each target’s radial damage.

7. **[AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:477](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/AI/GBEnemyCharacter.cpp:477)** — Enemy melee attacks check only distance before dealing damage. An orc within reach can hit the player through an ice wall or thin level geometry; require an unobstructed trace to the player before applying the melee hit.

8. **[Weapons/GBHeldItem.cpp:61](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBHeldItem.cpp:61), [StatusEffects.json:83](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Content/Data/StatusEffects.json:83)** — Enchants modify `Weapon.All.Damage`, which staffs and swords evaluate too; `AcceptsEnchant()` only gates element and on-hit-effect changes. Enchanting a gun and switching weapons therefore grants the enchant’s damage bonus to weapons that should not receive it; introduce a gun-only modifier key and use it for the three enchant effects.

9. **[Items/GBInventoryComponent.cpp:116](C:/GameJam/Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Items/GBInventoryComponent.cpp:116)** — Ammo acquisition returns the entire requested count whenever any magazines fit. The future encounter’s two-magazine reward disappears completely when only one spare-magazine slot is free; return the count actually accepted so the pickup retains its remainder.
